    "lib/core-v1/contracts/libraries/GeometricTWAP.sol": {
      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only\npragma solidity ^0.8.34;\n\nimport {Math} from '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol';\nimport {SafeCast} from '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/SafeCast.sol';\n\nimport {Convert} from 'contracts/libraries/Convert.sol';\nimport {TickMath} from 'contracts/libraries/TickMath.sol';\nimport {DEFAULT_TICK_DELTA_FACTOR, MAX_TICK_DELTA, Q32} from 'contracts/libraries/constants.sol';\n\n/**\n * @title GeometricTWAP: Library for Geometric TWAP calculation and observations.\n */\nlibrary GeometricTWAP {\n    uint256 internal constant MID_TERM_ARRAY_LENGTH = 51;\n    uint256 internal constant LONG_TERM_ARRAY_LENGTH = 9;\n    uint256 internal constant LOG_BASE_OF_ROOT_TWO = 178;\n    uint256 internal constant MID_TERM_ARRAY_LAST_INDEX = MID_TERM_ARRAY_LENGTH - 1;\n    uint256 internal constant LONG_TERM_ARRAY_LAST_INDEX = LONG_TERM_ARRAY_LENGTH - 1;\n    /**\n     * @notice Minimum long-term interval factor is used to verify the long-term interval\n     *      is at least 14 times the mid-term interval. This ensures that the long term\n     *      interval is required to be at least 14 times the mid-term interval, this is\n     *      ```math\n     *      \\left \\lceil \\frac{2 * MID\\_TERM\\_ARRAY\\_LAST\\_INDEX}{LONG\\_TERM\\_ARRAY\\_LAST\\_INDEX} \\right \\rceil.\n     *      ```\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant MINIMUM_LONG_TERM_INTERVAL_FACTOR = 14;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Struct for storing observations related to the Geometric TWAP calculation.\n     * @dev This struct holds various data points used in the Time-Weighted Average Price (TWAP)\n     *      calculation.\n     * @member midTermIndex: The index representing the total duration observed for the\n     *      short-term period. Its initial value is 0.\n     * @member longTermIndex: The index representing the total duration observed for the\n     *      long-term period. Its initial value is 0.\n     * @member lastTick: The latest recorded tick, bounded to avoid outliers.\n     * @member midTermIntervalConfig: The configurable interval for each period of the mid-term buffer.\n     * @member longTermIntervalConfig: The time required to pass before the long term twap is updated.\n     * @member lendingCumulativeSum: The cumulative sum of mid-term values for lending state.\n     * @member midTermTimeInterval: An array tracking timestamp at which each mid-term observation is recorded.\n     * @member longTermTimeInterval: An array tracking timestamp at which each long-term observation is recorded.\n     * @member midTerm: An array of mid-term observations. Each element stores cumulative tick value.\n     *      The array size is 51 for it observes 50 price changes for last 10 minutes (12 * 50 = 600 seconds).\n     * @member longTerm: An array of long-term observations. Each element stores cumulative tick\n     *      value of a buffer. The buffer size is configured by `longTermIntervalConfig`. The term is\n     *      observed every `longTermIntervalConfig` duration.\n     */\n    struct Observations {\n        bool isMidTermBufferInitialized;\n        bool isLongTermBufferInitialized;\n        uint8 midTermIndex;\n        uint8 longTermIndex;\n        int16 lastTick;\n        uint24 midTermIntervalConfig;\n        uint24 longTermIntervalConfig;\n        int56 lendingCumulativeSum;\n        int56[MID_TERM_ARRAY_LENGTH] midTermCumulativeSum;\n        int56[LONG_TERM_ARRAY_LENGTH] longTermCumulativeSum;\n        uint32[MID_TERM_ARRAY_LENGTH] midTermTimeInterval;\n        uint32[LONG_TERM_ARRAY_LENGTH] longTermTimeInterval;\n    }\n\n    error InvalidIntervalConfig();\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Initializes the observation struct with the specified interval configurations.\n     * @dev This function sets the initial values for the mid-term and long-term interval configurations.\n     *\n     *      This forces the time to to go through the long term is twice as long as it takes to go\n     *      through the mid-term interval.\n     * @param self The storage reference to the Observations struct.\n     * @param midTermTimePerUpdate The time required to pass before the mid-term twap is updated.\n     * @param longTermTimePerUpdate The time required to pass before the long-term twap is updated.\n     */\n    function initializeObservationStruct(\n        Observations storage self,\n        uint24 midTermTimePerUpdate,\n        uint24 longTermTimePerUpdate\n    ) internal {\n        if (\n            midTermTimePerUpdate == 0\n                || longTermTimePerUpdate < MINIMUM_LONG_TERM_INTERVAL_FACTOR * midTermTimePerUpdate\n        ) {\n            revert InvalidIntervalConfig();\n        }\n        self.midTermIntervalConfig = midTermTimePerUpdate;\n        self.longTermIntervalConfig = longTermTimePerUpdate;\n    }\n\n    function addObservationAndSetLendingState(Observations storage self, int16 firstTick) internal {\n        // Update the mid-term & long-term states with first tick value.\n        setObservationData(self, firstTick, 0, 0, true);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Configures the interval of long-term observations.\n     * @dev This function is used to set the long-term interval between observations for the long-term buffer.\n     * @param self The storage reference to the Observations struct.\n     * @param longTermTimePerUpdate the time required to pass before the long term twap is update.\n     */\n    function configLongTermInterval(Observations storage self, uint24 longTermTimePerUpdate) internal {\n        uint256 midTermIntervalConfig = self.midTermIntervalConfig;\n        if (\n            longTermTimePerUpdate < MINIMUM_LONG_TERM_INTERVAL_FACTOR * midTermIntervalConfig\n                || midTermIntervalConfig == 0 // make sure the library is initialized\n        ) {\n            revert InvalidIntervalConfig();\n        }\n        self.longTermIntervalConfig = longTermTimePerUpdate;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Records a new observation tick value and updates the observation data.\n     * @dev This function is used to record new observation data for the contract. It ensures that\n     *      the provided tick value is stored appropriately in both mid-term and long-term\n     *      observations, updates interval counters, and handles tick cumulative values based\n     *      on the current interval configuration. Ensures that this function is called in\n     *      chronological order, with increasing timestamps. Returns in case the\n     *      provided block timestamp is less than or equal to the last recorded timestamp.\n     * @param self The storage structure containing observation data.\n     * @param newTick The new tick value to be recorded, representing the most recent update of\n     *      reserveXAssets and reserveYAssets.\n     * @param timeElapsed The time elapsed since the last observation.\n     * @return updated A boolean indicating whether the observation was updated.\n     */\n    function recordObservation(\n        Observations storage self,\n        int16 newTick,\n        uint32 timeElapsed\n    ) internal returns (bool updated) {\n        // Record observation only if the time elapsed is greater than the configured mid-term interval.\n        if (timeElapsed >= self.midTermIntervalConfig) {\n            uint256 currentMidTermIndex = self.midTermIndex;\n\n            newTick = boundTick(self, newTick);\n\n            // Update state for mid-term range observations.\n            int56 currentCumulativeTick = getMidTermAtLastIndex(self, currentMidTermIndex);\n            unchecked {\n                currentCumulativeTick += int56(newTick) * int56(uint56(timeElapsed));\n            }\n\n            setObservationData(self, newTick, currentCumulativeTick, currentMidTermIndex, false);\n            updated = true;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Gets the min and max range of tick values from the stored oracle observations.\n     * @dev This function calculates the minimum and maximum tick values among three observed ticks:\n     *          long-term tick, mid-term tick, and current tick.\n     * @param self The observation struct where stored oracle array containing the tick observations.\n     * @param currentTick The current (most recent) tick based on the current reserves.\n     * @return minTick The minimum tick value among the three observed ticks.\n     * @return maxTick The maximum tick value among the three observed ticks.\n     */\n    function getTickRange(Observations storage self, int16 currentTick) internal view returns (int16, int16) {\n        bool isLongTermBufferInitialized = self.isLongTermBufferInitialized;\n        (int16 longTermTick, int16 midTermTick, int16 blockTick) = getObservedTicks(self, isLongTermBufferInitialized);\n        uint256 factor = getLongTermBufferFactor(self, isLongTermBufferInitialized);\n        return getTickRangeInternal(longTermTick, midTermTick, blockTick, currentTick, factor);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Gets the min and max range of tick values from the stored oracle observations.\n     * @dev This function calculates the minimum and maximum tick values among the mid-term tick and current tick.\n     * @param self The observation struct where stored oracle array containing the tick observations.\n     * @return minTick The minimum tick value.\n     * @return maxTick The maximum tick value.\n     */\n    function getTickRangeWithoutLongTerm(\n        Observations storage self\n    ) internal view returns (int16 minTick, int16 maxTick) {\n        int16 midTermTick = getObservedMidTermTick(self);\n        int16 blockTick = self.lastTick;\n\n        // we do not include the current tick to avoid manipulation to increase liquidation payout.\n        (minTick, maxTick) = getMinAndMaxOfThree(midTermTick, blockTick, blockTick);\n        maxTick += 1;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Retrieves the long-term, mid-term, and current tick values based on the stored observations.\n     * @dev visibility is only `internal` for testing purposes\n     * @param self The observation struct.\n     * @param isLongTermBufferInitialized Boolean value which represents whether long-term buffer is filled or not.\n     * @return The long-term, mid-term, and last tick values.\n     */\n    function getObservedTicks(\n        Observations storage self,\n        bool isLongTermBufferInitialized\n    ) internal view returns (int16, int16, int16) {\n        return (getObservedLongTermTick(self, isLongTermBufferInitialized), getObservedMidTermTick(self), self.lastTick);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Retrieves the mid-term tick value based on the stored observations.\n     * @param self The observation struct.\n     * @return midTermTick The mid-term tick value.\n     */\n    function getObservedMidTermTick(\n        Observations storage self\n    ) internal view returns (int16 midTermTick) {\n        uint256 currentMidTermIndex = self.midTermIndex; // gas savings\n        uint256 lastMidTermInterval = getLastIndex(currentMidTermIndex, MID_TERM_ARRAY_LAST_INDEX);\n        bool isMidTermBufferInitialized = self.isMidTermBufferInitialized;\n\n        if (!isMidTermBufferInitialized && lastMidTermInterval == 0) {\n            midTermTick = self.lastTick;\n        } else {\n            unchecked {\n                // Timestamp can overflow after 136 years, this is safe as long as\n                // there is an update every 2.67 years on average for a cycle through\n                // the mid-term array.\n                midTermTick = calculateTickAverage(\n                    self.midTermCumulativeSum[lastMidTermInterval],\n                    (isMidTermBufferInitialized ? self.midTermCumulativeSum[currentMidTermIndex] : int56(0)),\n                    uint256(\n                        self.midTermTimeInterval[lastMidTermInterval]\n                            - (\n                                isMidTermBufferInitialized\n                                    ? self.midTermTimeInterval[currentMidTermIndex]\n                                    : self.midTermTimeInterval[0]\n                            )\n                    )\n                );\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Retrieves the long-term tick value based on the stored observations.\n     * @param self The observation struct.\n     * @param isLongTermBufferInitialized Boolean value which represents whether long-term buffer is filled or not.\n     * @return longTermTick The long-term tick value.\n     */\n    function getObservedLongTermTick(\n        Observations storage self,\n        bool isLongTermBufferInitialized\n    ) private view returns (int16 longTermTick) {\n        uint256 currentLongTermIndex = self.longTermIndex;\n        uint256 lastLongTermInterval = getLastIndex(currentLongTermIndex, LONG_TERM_ARRAY_LAST_INDEX);\n\n        if (!isLongTermBufferInitialized && lastLongTermInterval == 0) {\n            longTermTick = self.lastTick;\n        } else {\n            unchecked {\n                // we allow the timestamp to overflow every 136 years.\n                longTermTick = calculateTickAverage(\n                    self.longTermCumulativeSum[lastLongTermInterval],\n                    isLongTermBufferInitialized ? self.longTermCumulativeSum[currentLongTermIndex] : int56(0),\n                    uint256(\n                        self.longTermTimeInterval[lastLongTermInterval]\n                            - (\n                                isLongTermBufferInitialized\n                                    ? self.longTermTimeInterval[currentLongTermIndex]\n                                    : self.longTermTimeInterval[0]\n                            )\n                    )\n                );\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // visibility is only `internal` for testing purposes\n    function getTickRangeInternal(\n        int16 longTermTick,\n        int16 midTermTick,\n        int16 blockTick,\n        int16 currentTick,\n        uint256 factor\n    ) internal pure returns (int16 minTick, int16 maxTick) {\n        (minTick, maxTick) = getMinAndMaxOfFour(longTermTick, midTermTick, blockTick, currentTick);\n\n        // `minTick` & `maxTick` bound check to be within the range of `MIN_TICK` and `MAX_TICK`.\n        unchecked {\n            // 0 <= delta <= MAX_TICK-MIN_TICK\n            uint256 delta = uint256(int256(maxTick) - minTick);\n\n            int256 buffer = int256(\n                delta\n                    + Convert.mulDiv(\n                        LOG_BASE_OF_ROOT_TWO, LONG_TERM_ARRAY_LAST_INDEX - factor, LONG_TERM_ARRAY_LAST_INDEX, false\n                    )\n            );\n\n            // `minTick` & `maxTick` bound check to be within the range of `MIN_TICK` and `MAX_TICK`.\n            // safe from under flow\n            minTick =\n                int256(blockTick) - buffer > int256(TickMath.MIN_TICK) ? int16(blockTick - buffer) : TickMath.MIN_TICK;\n            maxTick = int256(blockTick) + buffer + 1 < int256(TickMath.MAX_TICK)\n                ? int16(blockTick + buffer + 1)\n                : TickMath.MAX_TICK;\n        }\n    }\n\n    function getMinAndMaxOfFour(int16 a, int16 b, int16 c, int16 d) private pure returns (int16 min, int16 max) {\n        (min, max) = getMinAndMaxOfThree(b, c, d);\n        (min, max) = getMinAndMaxOfThree(a, min, max);\n    }\n\n    function getMinAndMaxOfThree(int16 a, int16 b, int16 c) private pure returns (int16 min, int16 max) {\n        (min, max) = c < b ? (c, b) : (b, c);\n        if (a < min) {\n            min = a;\n        } else if (max < a) {\n            max = a;\n        }\n    }\n\n    function getMidTermAtLastIndex(Observations storage self, uint256 index) private view returns (int56) {\n        return self.midTermCumulativeSum[getLastIndex(index, MID_TERM_ARRAY_LAST_INDEX)];\n    }\n\n    function getLastIndex(uint256 index, uint256 lastIndex) private pure returns (uint256) {\n        unchecked {\n            return index == 0 ? lastIndex : index - 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    function getNextIndex(uint256 currentIndex, uint256 indexLength) private pure returns (uint8) {\n        unchecked {\n            return uint8((currentIndex + 1) % indexLength);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Gets the tick value representing the TWAP since the last\n     *         lending update and checkpoints the current lending cumulative sum\n     *         as `self.lendingCumulativeSum` and the current block timestamp as `self.lastLendingTimestamp`.\n     * @dev See `getLendingStateTick` for implementation details which was\n     *      separated to allow view access without any state updates.\n     * @param self Observations storage struct\n     * @param timeElapsedSinceUpdate The time elapsed since the last price update.\n     * @param timeElapsedSinceLendingUpdate The time elapsed since the last lending update.\n     * @return lendingStateTick The tick value representing the TWAP since the last lending update.\n     */\n    function getLendingStateTickAndCheckpoint(\n        Observations storage self,\n        uint32 timeElapsedSinceUpdate,\n        uint32 timeElapsedSinceLendingUpdate\n    ) internal returns (int16) {\n        (int16 lendingStateTick, int56 currentCumulativeSum) =\n            getLendingStateTick(self, 0, timeElapsedSinceUpdate, timeElapsedSinceLendingUpdate, false);\n\n        self.lendingCumulativeSum = currentCumulativeSum;\n        return lendingStateTick;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Gets the tick value representing the TWAP since the last lending update.\n     * @param self Observations storage struct\n     * @param newTick The current tick value.\n     * @param timeElapsedSinceUpdate The time elapsed since the last price update.\n     * @param timeElapsedSinceLendingUpdate The time elapsed since the last lending update.\n     * @return lendingStateTick The tick value representing the TWAP since the last lending update.\n     * @return currentCumulativeSum The current cumulative sum for the last updated timestamp.\n     */\n    function getLendingStateTick(\n        Observations storage self,\n        int56 newTick,\n        uint32 timeElapsedSinceUpdate,\n        uint32 timeElapsedSinceLendingUpdate,\n        bool tickAvailable\n    ) internal view returns (int16, int56) {\n        uint24 midTermIntervalConfig = self.midTermIntervalConfig; // gas savings\n        int56 currentCumulativeSum = getMidTermAtLastIndex(self, self.midTermIndex);\n\n        if (tickAvailable && timeElapsedSinceUpdate >= midTermIntervalConfig) {\n            unchecked {\n                // add currentTimeStamp * tick to cumulativeSum\n                currentCumulativeSum += int56(boundTick(self, int16(newTick))) * int56(uint56(timeElapsedSinceUpdate));\n            }\n        }\n\n        return (\n            (timeElapsedSinceLendingUpdate < midTermIntervalConfig)\n                ? self.lastTick\n                : calculateTickAverage(currentCumulativeSum, self.lendingCumulativeSum, timeElapsedSinceLendingUpdate),\n            currentCumulativeSum\n        );\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Updates the observation data with the new tick value and current timestamp.\n     * @dev This function updates both mid-term and long-term observation states based on the provided\n     *      new tick value and the current timestamp. It also updates the last recorded observation state.\n     * @param self The storage reference to the Observations struct.\n     * @param newTick The new tick value to be recorded.\n     * @param currentCumulativeTick The current cumulative tick sum.\n     */\n    function setObservationData(\n        Observations storage self,\n        int16 newTick,\n        int56 currentCumulativeTick,\n        uint256 currentMidTermIndex,\n        bool firstUpdate\n    ) private {\n        uint32 currentTimestamp = getCurrentTimestamp();\n        // Update the mid-term interval state.\n        self.midTermCumulativeSum[currentMidTermIndex] = currentCumulativeTick;\n        self.midTermTimeInterval[currentMidTermIndex] = currentTimestamp;\n\n        // set storage and memory to save gas.\n        uint256 nextIndex = self.midTermIndex = getNextIndex(currentMidTermIndex, MID_TERM_ARRAY_LENGTH);\n\n        if (!self.isMidTermBufferInitialized && nextIndex == 0) {\n            self.isMidTermBufferInitialized = true;\n        }\n\n        {\n            // Check if long-term interval should be updated.\n            uint24 currentLongTermConfig = self.longTermIntervalConfig;\n\n            uint32 duration;\n            unchecked {\n                duration = currentTimestamp\n                    - self.longTermTimeInterval[getLastIndex(self.longTermIndex, LONG_TERM_ARRAY_LAST_INDEX)];\n            }\n            if (duration >= currentLongTermConfig || firstUpdate) {\n                uint256 currentLongTermIndex = self.longTermIndex;\n\n                // Update the long-term interval state.\n                self.longTermCumulativeSum[currentLongTermIndex] = currentCumulativeTick;\n                self.longTermTimeInterval[currentLongTermIndex] = currentTimestamp;\n                // set to storage and memory to save gas.\n                nextIndex = self.longTermIndex = getNextIndex(currentLongTermIndex, LONG_TERM_ARRAY_LENGTH);\n\n                if (!self.isLongTermBufferInitialized && nextIndex == 0) {\n                    self.isLongTermBufferInitialized = true;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        // update last recorded observation state.\n        self.lastTick = newTick;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Computes the tick average based on the cumulative sum and duration.\n     * @param currentCumulativeSum The current cumulative sum of mid-term/long-term values.\n     * @param previousCumulativeSum The previous cumulative sum recorded for mid-term/long-term.\n     * @param bufferLength If the mid-term/long-term buffer is fully recorded, then the buffer length\n     *         equals the duration passed between the first and last recorded ticks, else it's same as\n     *         the mid-term/long-term buffer.\n     * @return tick The computed tick average for mid-term/long-term.\n     */\n    function calculateTickAverage(\n        int56 currentCumulativeSum,\n        int56 previousCumulativeSum,\n        uint256 bufferLength\n    ) private pure returns (int16 tick) {\n        unchecked {\n            // under flows desired\n            tick = int16((currentCumulativeSum - previousCumulativeSum) / int256(bufferLength));\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Gets the long-term buffer factor based on the available data in long-term array.\n     * @param self The observation struct where stored oracle array containing the tick observations.\n     * @param isLongTermBufferInitialized Boolean value which represents whether long-term buffer is filled or not.\n     * @return factor The amount of information we have in the long-term array.\n     */\n    function getLongTermBufferFactor(\n        Observations storage self,\n        bool isLongTermBufferInitialized\n    ) private view returns (uint256 factor) {\n        /**\n         * Factor, `F` is the amount of information we have in the long-term array.\n         * It's represented by the last filled index count in the long-term array.\n         */\n        factor = LONG_TERM_ARRAY_LAST_INDEX;\n        if (!isLongTermBufferInitialized) {\n            factor = self.longTermIndex - 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Adjusts the new tick value to ensure it stays within valid bounds. When we have less data, the outlier\n     *         factor is greater to allow for more flexibility to find the true price.\n     * @dev The function ensures that `newTick` stays within the bounds\n     *      determined by `lastTick` and a dynamically calculated factor.\n     * @param self The storage reference to `Observations`, which holds historical tick data.\n     * @param newTick The proposed new tick value to be adjusted within valid bounds.\n     * @return The adjusted tick value constrained within the allowable range.\n     */\n    function boundTick(Observations storage self, int16 newTick) internal view returns (int16) {\n        int256 outlierFactor = DEFAULT_TICK_DELTA_FACTOR;\n\n        bool isLongTermBufferInitialized = self.isLongTermBufferInitialized;\n\n        if (!isLongTermBufferInitialized) {\n            outlierFactor =\n                int256(LONG_TERM_ARRAY_LAST_INDEX - getLongTermBufferFactor(self, isLongTermBufferInitialized));\n        }\n        int16 lastTick = self.lastTick;\n        unchecked {\n            int256 maxTickDelta = outlierFactor * MAX_TICK_DELTA;\n            int256 minTickBound = lastTick - maxTickDelta;\n            int256 maxTickBound = lastTick + maxTickDelta;\n\n            if (newTick > maxTickBound) {\n                newTick = int16(maxTickBound);\n            } else if (newTick < minTickBound) {\n                newTick = int16(minTickBound);\n            }\n        }\n\n        return newTick;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the current block timestamp casted to uint32.\n     * @return The current block timestamp as a uint32 value.\n     */\n    function getCurrentTimestamp() internal view returns (uint32) {\n        unchecked {\n            // slither-disable-next-line weak-prng\n            return uint32(block.timestamp % Q32);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Average the mid-term tick and the new tick, rounding towards the `midTermTick`.\n     * @param midTermTick The midterm tick value\n     * @param newTick The new tick value\n     * @return The calculated average tick value\n     */\n    function calculateTickAverageTowardsMidTerm(int256 midTermTick, int256 newTick) internal pure returns (int16) {\n        int256 sum = midTermTick + newTick;\n\n        // If sum is even, the average is an integer\n        // If sum is odd, the average is a half-integer, so round towards `midTermTick`\n        return int16(sum % 2 == 0 ? sum / 2 : midTermTick > newTick ? (sum + 1) / 2 : (sum - 1) / 2);\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    "lib/core-v1/contracts/libraries/QuadraticSwapFees.sol": {
      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only\npragma solidity ^0.8.34;\n\nimport {Math} from '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol';\nimport {Convert} from 'contracts/libraries/Convert.sol';\n\n/**\n * @title QuadraticSwapFees\n * @author Will\n * @notice A library to calculate fees that grow with respect to price deviation from a reference point.\n *   This library relies on a reference reserve from the start of the block to determine what the overall\n *   growth in price has been in the current block.\n *\n * Fee Model Overview:\n *   The fee structure uses two distinct models depending on the magnitude of price deviation:\n *\n *   1. Quadratic Fee Model (for moderate price changes):\n *      - Used when price deviation is relatively small\n *      - Fee grows proportionally to the square of the price movement\n *      - This creates a smooth, incentive-aligned fee curve that discourages large single swaps\n *        while keeping fees reasonable for normal market activity\n *      - Key property: If one swap would pay fee `F`, splitting it into two equal swaps would pay\n *        two fees that sum to approximately `F`\n *\n *   2. Linear Fee Model (for extreme price changes):\n *      - Used when price deviation exceeds the quadratic threshold\n *      - Fee grows linearly beyond the threshold point, capping at `MAX_QUADRATIC_FEE_PERCENT`\n *      - This prevents fees from becoming prohibitively expensive for very large swaps while\n *        still maintaining strong economic disincentives against massive price manipulations\n *      - The linear growth provides a more predictable fee structure for extreme market conditions\n *\n * Why Two Models?\n *   - Quadratic fees alone would become so high that the invariant curve property of being monotonic, leading\n *     larger swaps amounts in would eventually lead to less assets out than smaller swaps in.\n *   - Linear fees alone wouldn't provide enough protection against price manipulation for moderate swaps\n *\n * Price Movement Behavior:\n *   - If the price moves away from the reference, and then back toward it, the fee is minimal until\n *     the price again crosses the starting reference price\n *   - Only the net price deviation from the start-of-block reference is charged\n */\nlibrary QuadraticSwapFees {\n    /**\n     * @notice Minimum fee is one tenth of a basis point.\n     */\n    uint256 public constant MIN_FEE_Q64 = 0x1999999999999999;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice 10000 bips per 100 percent in Q64.\n     */\n    uint256 public constant BIPS_Q64 = 0x27100000000000000000;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Max percent fee growing at a quadratic rate. After this the growths slows down.\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant MAX_QUADRATIC_FEE_PERCENT = 40;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice A scaler that controls how fast the fee grows, at 20, 9x price change will be\n     *   a 40% fee.\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant N = 20;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice A reserve multiplier used to determine the boundary at which we switch from quadratic to linear fee.\n     */\n    uint256 private constant RESERVE_MULTIPLIER = 2;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice the $$\\sqrt{price}$$ at which we switch from quadratic fee to a more linear fee.\n     *   ```math\n     *     \\frac{MAX\\_QUADRATIC\\_FEE\\_PERCENT + 2\\cdot N}{N}\n     *   ```\n     */\n    uint256 private constant LINEAR_START_REFERENCE_SCALER = 4;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice the fee at `LINEAR_START_REFERENCE_SCALER` in bips\n     */\n    uint256 private constant MAX_QUADRATIC_FEE_PERCENT_BIPS = 4000;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice $$N \\cdot 100 \\cdot Q64$$ or `N` times bips in one percent in Q64\n     */\n    uint256 private constant N_TIMES_BIPS_Q64_PER_PERCENT = 0x7d00000000000000000;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice 2 times Q64 or Q65\n     */\n    uint256 private constant TWO_Q64 = 0x20000000000000000;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice `MAX_QUADRATIC_FEE_PERCENT` in Q64, $$MAX\\_QUADRATIC\\_FEE\\_PERCENT \\cdot Q64$$\n     */\n    uint256 private constant MAX_QUADRATIC_FEE_Q64 = 0x280000000000000000;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Computes the swap fee based on the `input` amount and the pool's\n     *   `currentReserve` and `referenceReserve` for the input asset.\n     *\n     * Control flow by scenario:\n     *   - `currentReserve >= referenceReserve` (price already at/away from reference):\n     *     - If `input + RESERVE_MULTIPLIER * currentReserve < referenceReserve * LINEAR_START_REFERENCE_SCALER`:\n     *       Use the quadratic fee model (closer to reference, pre-threshold).\n     *     - Otherwise:\n     *       Use the linear fee model (farther from reference, post-threshold).\n     *   - `currentReserve < referenceReserve` (price at/moving back toward the start-of-block reference):\n     *     - If `input + currentReserve <= referenceReserve`:\n     *       Does not cross the reference. Only the global minimum fee will apply at the end.\n     *     - If `input + currentReserve > referenceReserve`:\n     *       Crosses the reference. The portion beyond the reference (`pastBy`) is charged using\n     *       either the quadratic or linear model depending on `pastBy`:\n     *         - `pastBy > RESERVE_MULTIPLIER * referenceReserve` → use linear fee model\n     *         - otherwise → use quadratic fee model\n     *       The resulting fee for the beyond-reference portion is then weighted by `pastBy / input`.\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *     \\begin{equation*}\n     *       f_\\phi(X_{in}) =\n     *         \\begin{cases}\n     *           n \\cdot \\frac{2(X_{0}-X_{R})+X_{in}}{X_{R}}\n     *             &\\text{if } X_0 \\ge X_R \\text{ \\& } X_{in} + 2 X_{0} \\le X_{R}\\left(\\frac{M_Q +\\ 2n}{n}\\right) \\\\\n     *\n     *           M_Q \\left( 2 - X_R \\frac{M_Q}{n\\left( X_{in} + 2 (X_0 - X_R) \\right)} \\right)\n     *             &\\text {if } X_0 \\ge X_R \\text{ \\& } X_{in} + 2X_{0} \\ge X_{R}\\left(\\frac{M_Q+\\ 2n}{n}\\right) \\\\\n     *\n     *           \\frac{n \\cdot \\left( \\frac{pastBy^2}{X_R} \\right)}{X_{in}}\n     *             &\\text{ if } X_0 + X_{in} \\gt X_R \\text{ \\& } X_{in} + 2 X_{0} \\le X_{R}\\left(\\frac{M_Q +\\ 2n}{n}\\right) \\\\\n     *\n     *           \\frac{M_Q \\left( 2 - X_R \\frac{M_Q}{n \\cdot pastBy} \\right) \\cdot pastBy}{X_{in}}\n     *             &\\text{ if } X_0 + X_{in} \\gt X_R \\text{ \\& } X_{in} + 2 X_{0} \\ge X_{R}\\left(\\frac{M_Q +\\ 2n}{n}\\right) \\\\\n     *\n     *           MinBips &\\text{ otherwise } X_0 + X_{in} \\le  X_R \\\\\n     *         \\end{cases}\n     *     \\end{equation*}\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   where:\n     *   - `X_in` is the `input` amount\n     *   - `X_0` is the `currentReserve`\n     *   - `X_R` is the `referenceReserve`\n     *   - `M_Q` is `MAX_QUADRATIC_FEE_PERCENT`\n     *   - `n` is `N`\n     *   - `pastBy` is the amount by which the price has moved past the `referenceReserve`\n     *   - `MinBips` is `MIN_FEE_Q64`\n     *\n     * @param input The input amount of the asset (units of the asset).\n     * @param currentReserve The current reserve of the input asset in the pool.\n     * @param referenceReserve The start-of-block reference reserve for the input asset.\n     * @return fee The swap fee in Q64 bips.\n     */\n    function calculateSwapFeeBipsQ64(\n        uint256 input,\n        uint256 currentReserve,\n        uint256 referenceReserve\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 fee) {\n        if (input == 0) return 0;\n\n        /**\n         * @dev All arithmetic operations within this `unchecked` block are safe from overflow/underflow.\n         *\n         * 1. `input + currentReserve` cannot overflow:\n         *    - The caller `AmmalgamPair` verifies reserves via `updateReserves(reserveAssets + amountIn)`\n         *    - Reserves are casted using `SafeCast` to `uint112` before being stored,\n         *      so `input + currentReserve <= type(uint112).max`\n         *\n         * 2. a) `LINEAR_START_REFERENCE_SCALER * referenceReserve` cannot overflow:\n         *    b) `RESERVE_MULTIPLIER * referenceReserve` cannot overflow:\n         *       - `referenceReserve` is bounded by `uint112`\n         *\n         * 3. `currentReserveAfterSwap + currentReserve` cannot overflow:\n         *    - `currentReserveAfterSwap = input + currentReserve` is bounded by `uint112`\n         *    - `currentReserve` is bounded by `uint112`\n         */\n        unchecked {\n            uint256 currentReserveAfterSwap = input + currentReserve;\n            if (currentReserve >= referenceReserve) {\n                if (currentReserveAfterSwap + currentReserve > referenceReserve * LINEAR_START_REFERENCE_SCALER) {\n                    fee = calculateLinearFeeBipsQ64(input, currentReserve, referenceReserve);\n                } else {\n                    fee = calculateQuadraticFeeBipsQ64(input, currentReserve, referenceReserve);\n                }\n            } else {\n                if (currentReserveAfterSwap > referenceReserve) {\n                    uint256 pastBy = currentReserveAfterSwap - referenceReserve;\n\n                    // similar inequality as above but treating `currentReserve == referenceReserve`\n                    if (pastBy > RESERVE_MULTIPLIER * referenceReserve) {\n                        fee = calculateLinearFeeBipsQ64(pastBy, referenceReserve, referenceReserve);\n                    } else {\n                        fee = calculateQuadraticFeeBipsQ64(pastBy, referenceReserve, referenceReserve);\n                    }\n\n                    // We weight the fee based on how far past the reference reserve\n                    fee = Convert.mulDiv(fee, pastBy, input, false);\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        fee = Math.max(fee, MIN_FEE_Q64);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the quadratic fee in Q64 bips.\n     * @dev The quadratic fee model charges fees proportional to the square of price movement.\n     * Refer to `calculateSwapFeeBipsQ64` NatSpec for the quadratic fee formula.\n     *\n     * @param input The input amount of the asset (units of the asset).\n     * @param currentReserve The current reserve of the input asset in the pool.\n     * @param referenceReserve The start-of-block reference reserve for the input asset.\n     * @return fee The quadratic fee in Q64 bips.\n     */\n    function calculateQuadraticFeeBipsQ64(\n        uint256 input,\n        uint256 currentReserve,\n        uint256 referenceReserve\n    ) private pure returns (uint256 fee) {\n        fee = Convert.mulDiv(\n            N_TIMES_BIPS_Q64_PER_PERCENT,\n            input + RESERVE_MULTIPLIER * (currentReserve - referenceReserve),\n            referenceReserve,\n            false\n        );\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the linear fee in Q64 bips.\n     * @dev The linear fee model charges fees that grow linearly (not quadratically) for extreme price deviations.\n     * Refer to `calculateSwapFeeBipsQ64` NatSpec for the linear fee formula.\n     *\n     * @param input The input amount of the asset (units of the asset).\n     * @param currentReserve The current reserve of the input asset in the pool.\n     * @param referenceReserve The start-of-block reference reserve for the input asset.\n     * @return fee The linear fee in Q64 bips.\n     */\n    function calculateLinearFeeBipsQ64(\n        uint256 input,\n        uint256 currentReserve,\n        uint256 referenceReserve\n    ) private pure returns (uint256 fee) {\n        fee = MAX_QUADRATIC_FEE_PERCENT_BIPS\n            * (\n                TWO_Q64\n                    - Convert.mulDiv(\n                        referenceReserve,\n                        MAX_QUADRATIC_FEE_Q64,\n                        N * (input + RESERVE_MULTIPLIER * (currentReserve - referenceReserve)),\n                        false\n                    )\n            );\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    "lib/core-v1/contracts/libraries/Validation.sol": {
      "content": "/// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only\npragma solidity ^0.8.34;\n\nimport {Math} from '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol';\n\nimport {\n    DEPOSIT_L,\n    DEPOSIT_X,\n    DEPOSIT_Y,\n    BORROW_L,\n    BORROW_X,\n    BORROW_Y\n} from 'contracts/interfaces/tokens/ITokenController.sol';\n\nimport {\n    Q72,\n    Q128,\n    LTVMAX_IN_MAG2,\n    ALLOWED_LIQUIDITY_LEVERAGE,\n    MAG2,\n    MINIMUM_LIQUIDITY\n} from 'contracts/libraries/constants.sol';\nimport {Convert} from 'contracts/libraries/Convert.sol';\nimport {TickMath} from 'contracts/libraries/TickMath.sol';\nimport {QuadraticSwapFees} from 'contracts/libraries/QuadraticSwapFees.sol';\n\nlibrary Validation {\n    uint256 internal constant MAX_BORROW_PERCENTAGE = 90;\n\n    struct InputParams {\n        uint256[6] userAssets;\n        int16 minTick;\n        int16 maxTick;\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMinInQ72;\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMaxInQ72;\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets;\n        uint256 reservesXAssets;\n        uint256 reservesYAssets;\n        bool hasBorrow;\n    }\n\n    struct CheckLtvParams {\n        uint256 netDepositedXinLAssets;\n        uint256 netDepositedYinLAssets;\n        uint256 netBorrowedXinLAssets;\n        uint256 netBorrowedYinLAssets;\n    }\n\n    struct VerifyMaxBorrowParams {\n        uint256 depositedAssets;\n        uint256 borrowedAssets;\n        uint256 reserve;\n        uint256 totalDepositedLAssets;\n        uint256 totalBorrowedLAssets;\n    }\n\n    error InsufficientLiquidity();\n    error AmmalgamCannotBorrowAgainstSameCollateral();\n    error AmmalgamMaxBorrowReached();\n    error AmmalgamDepositIsNotStrictlyBigger();\n    error AmmalgamLTV();\n    error AmmalgamMaxSlippage();\n    error AmmalgamTooMuchLeverage();\n    error AmmalgamTransferAmtExceedsBalance();\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Get the input parameters for the validation\n     * @dev hasBorrow is set to true here, because we assume that the caller has verified there is\n     *      a borrowed asset\n     * @param userAssets The user assets of the pool\n     * @param activeLiquidityAssets The current active liquidity assets of the pool\n     * @param reserveXAssets The reserve of the X asset\n     * @param reserveYAssets The reserve of the Y asset\n     * @param externalLiquidity The external liquidity of the pool\n     * @param minTick The min tick of the pool\n     * @param maxTick The max tick of the pool\n     * @return inputParams The input parameters for the validation\n     */\n    function getInputParams(\n        uint256[6] memory userAssets,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets,\n        uint256 reserveXAssets,\n        uint256 reserveYAssets,\n        uint256 externalLiquidity,\n        int16 minTick,\n        int16 maxTick\n    ) internal pure returns (Validation.InputParams memory inputParams) {\n        inputParams = Validation.InputParams({\n            userAssets: userAssets,\n            minTick: minTick,\n            maxTick: maxTick,\n            sqrtPriceMinInQ72: TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(minTick),\n            sqrtPriceMaxInQ72: TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(maxTick),\n            activeLiquidityAssets: activeLiquidityAssets + externalLiquidity,\n            reservesXAssets: reserveXAssets,\n            reservesYAssets: reserveYAssets,\n            hasBorrow: true\n        });\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Get the check LTV parameters for needed for `validateLTVAndLeverage()`\n     * @dev the sqrt prices are in the input params, but by passing them we allow for the ability\n     *      to switch them as needed in liquidation and other cases.\n     * @param userAssets User asset array\n     * @param sqrtPriceMinInQ72 The minimum sqrt price in Q72\n     * @param sqrtPriceMaxInQ72 The maximum sqrt price in Q72\n     */\n    function getCheckLtvParams(\n        uint256[6] memory userAssets,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMinInQ72,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMaxInQ72\n    ) internal pure returns (CheckLtvParams memory checkLtvParams) {\n        (checkLtvParams.netBorrowedXinLAssets, checkLtvParams.netBorrowedYinLAssets) =\n            getBorrowedInL(userAssets, sqrtPriceMinInQ72, sqrtPriceMaxInQ72);\n        (checkLtvParams.netDepositedXinLAssets, checkLtvParams.netDepositedYinLAssets) =\n            getDepositsInL(userAssets, sqrtPriceMinInQ72, sqrtPriceMaxInQ72);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Verifies that debt is backed by liquidity from an independent provider and\n     *   that users do not borrow the same underlying assets they supply as collateral.\n     * @param userAssets The account's assets by token type.\n     * @param activeLiquidityAssets The pair's active liquidity assets.\n     */\n    function validateBalanceAndLiqAndNotSameAssetsSuppliedAndBorrowed(\n        uint256[6] memory userAssets,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets\n    ) internal pure {\n        if (activeLiquidityAssets < userAssets[DEPOSIT_L] + MINIMUM_LIQUIDITY) revert InsufficientLiquidity();\n\n        verifyNotSameAssetsSuppliedAndBorrowed(\n            userAssets[DEPOSIT_X], userAssets[DEPOSIT_Y], userAssets[BORROW_X], userAssets[BORROW_Y]\n        );\n    }\n\n    function validateLTVAndLeverage(\n        CheckLtvParams memory checkLtvParams,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets\n    ) internal pure {\n        checkLtv(checkLtvParams, activeLiquidityAssets);\n        checkLeverage(checkLtvParams);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Added TokenType and uint256s for amount, balance from, and balance to\n     * to enable to pass a value for the current balance of a token to avoid one\n     * check of a balance that can be done from within a token.\n     */\n    function validateSolvency(\n        uint256[6] memory userAssets,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMinInQ72,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMaxInQ72,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets\n    ) external pure {\n        validateBalanceAndLiqAndNotSameAssetsSuppliedAndBorrowed(userAssets, activeLiquidityAssets);\n        CheckLtvParams memory checkLtvParams = getCheckLtvParams(userAssets, sqrtPriceMinInQ72, sqrtPriceMaxInQ72);\n        validateLTVAndLeverage(checkLtvParams, activeLiquidityAssets - userAssets[DEPOSIT_L]);\n    }\n\n    function verifyNotSameAssetsSuppliedAndBorrowed(\n        uint256 depositedXAssets,\n        uint256 depositedYAssets,\n        uint256 borrowedXAssets,\n        uint256 borrowedYAssets\n    ) internal pure {\n        if ((borrowedXAssets > 0 && depositedXAssets > 0) || (borrowedYAssets > 0 && depositedYAssets > 0)) {\n            revert AmmalgamCannotBorrowAgainstSameCollateral();\n        }\n    }\n\n    function verifyMaxBorrow(\n        VerifyMaxBorrowParams memory params\n    ) internal pure {\n        unchecked {\n            uint256 scaledBorrowedLiquidityAssets = Convert.mulDiv(\n                params.reserve,\n                params.totalBorrowedLAssets,\n                params.totalDepositedLAssets - params.totalBorrowedLAssets,\n                false\n            );\n\n            if (\n                Convert.mulDiv(params.reserve + scaledBorrowedLiquidityAssets, MAX_BORROW_PERCENTAGE, MAG2, false)\n                    + params.depositedAssets < scaledBorrowedLiquidityAssets + params.borrowedAssets\n            ) {\n                revert AmmalgamMaxBorrowReached();\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    function getDepositsInL(\n        uint256[6] memory userAssets,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMinInQ72,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMaxInQ72\n    ) private pure returns (uint256 netDepositedXinLAssets, uint256 netDepositedYinLAssets) {\n        netDepositedXinLAssets = netDepositedYinLAssets = userAssets[DEPOSIT_L];\n\n        if (0 < userAssets[DEPOSIT_X]) {\n            netDepositedXinLAssets += Validation.convertXToL(\n                userAssets[DEPOSIT_X],\n                sqrtPriceMaxInQ72, // max tick is applied on X for deposit\n                false\n            );\n        }\n        if (0 < userAssets[DEPOSIT_Y]) {\n            netDepositedYinLAssets += Validation.convertYToL(\n                userAssets[DEPOSIT_Y],\n                sqrtPriceMinInQ72, // min tick is applied on Y for deposit\n                false\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    function getBorrowedInL(\n        uint256[6] memory userAssets,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMinInQ72,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMaxInQ72\n    ) private pure returns (uint256 netBorrowedXinLAssets, uint256 netBorrowedYinLAssets) {\n        netBorrowedXinLAssets = netBorrowedYinLAssets = userAssets[BORROW_L];\n\n        if (userAssets[BORROW_X] > 0) {\n            netBorrowedXinLAssets += convertXToL(\n                userAssets[BORROW_X],\n                sqrtPriceMinInQ72, // min tick is applied on X for borrow\n                true\n            );\n        }\n        if (userAssets[BORROW_Y] > 0) {\n            netBorrowedYinLAssets += convertYToL(\n                userAssets[BORROW_Y],\n                sqrtPriceMaxInQ72, // max tick is applied on Y for borrow\n                true\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Convert X assets to L assets: L = x / sqrt(p)\n     * amountLAssets = amountInXAssets * Q72 / sqrtPriceInXInQ72\n     */\n    function convertXToL(\n        uint256 amountInXAssets,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceInXInQ72,\n        bool roundUp\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 amountLAssets) {\n        if (amountInXAssets == 0) return 0;\n        amountLAssets = Convert.mulDiv(amountInXAssets, Q72, sqrtPriceInXInQ72, roundUp);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Convert L assets to X assets: x = L * sqrt(p)\n     * amountXAssets = amount * sqrtPriceQ72 / Q72\n     */\n    function convertLToX(\n        uint256 amount,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceQ72,\n        bool roundUp\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 amountXAssets) {\n        if (amount == 0) return 0;\n        amountXAssets = Convert.mulDiv(amount, sqrtPriceQ72, Q72, roundUp);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Convert Y assets to L assets: L = y * sqrt(p)\n     * amountLAssets = amountInYAssets * sqrtPriceInXInQ72 / Q72\n     */\n    function convertYToL(\n        uint256 amountInYAssets,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceInXInQ72,\n        bool roundUp\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 amountInLAssets) {\n        if (amountInYAssets == 0) return 0;\n        amountInLAssets = Convert.mulDiv(amountInYAssets, sqrtPriceInXInQ72, Q72, roundUp);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * Convert L assets to Y assets: y = L / sqrt(p)\n     * amountYAssets = amount * Q72 / sqrtPriceQ72\n     */\n    function convertLToY(\n        uint256 amount,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceQ72,\n        bool roundUp\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 amountYAssets) {\n        if (amount == 0) return 0;\n        amountYAssets = Convert.mulDiv(amount, Q72, sqrtPriceQ72, roundUp);\n    }\n\n    function calcDebtAndCollateral(\n        CheckLtvParams memory checkLtvParams\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 debtLiquidityAssets, uint256 collateralLiquidityAssets, bool netDebtX) {\n        bool xDepositIsStrictlyBigger = checkLtvParams.netDepositedXinLAssets > checkLtvParams.netBorrowedXinLAssets;\n        bool yDepositIsStrictlyBigger = checkLtvParams.netDepositedYinLAssets > checkLtvParams.netBorrowedYinLAssets;\n\n        if (!xDepositIsStrictlyBigger && !yDepositIsStrictlyBigger) {\n            unchecked {\n                debtLiquidityAssets = checkLtvParams.netBorrowedXinLAssets - checkLtvParams.netDepositedXinLAssets\n                    + (checkLtvParams.netBorrowedYinLAssets - checkLtvParams.netDepositedYinLAssets);\n            }\n        } else if (!xDepositIsStrictlyBigger) {\n            unchecked {\n                debtLiquidityAssets = checkLtvParams.netBorrowedXinLAssets - checkLtvParams.netDepositedXinLAssets;\n                collateralLiquidityAssets = checkLtvParams.netDepositedYinLAssets - checkLtvParams.netBorrowedYinLAssets;\n            }\n            netDebtX = true;\n        } else if (!yDepositIsStrictlyBigger) {\n            unchecked {\n                debtLiquidityAssets = checkLtvParams.netBorrowedYinLAssets - checkLtvParams.netDepositedYinLAssets;\n                collateralLiquidityAssets = checkLtvParams.netDepositedXinLAssets - checkLtvParams.netBorrowedXinLAssets;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    function checkLtv(\n        CheckLtvParams memory checkLtvParams,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets\n    ) private pure returns (uint256 debtLiquidityAssets, uint256 collateralLiquidityAssets) {\n        if (checkLtvParams.netBorrowedXinLAssets == 0 && checkLtvParams.netBorrowedYinLAssets == 0) return (0, 0);\n\n        (debtLiquidityAssets, collateralLiquidityAssets,) = calcDebtAndCollateral(checkLtvParams);\n        if (collateralLiquidityAssets == 0 && debtLiquidityAssets > 0) {\n            revert AmmalgamDepositIsNotStrictlyBigger();\n        }\n\n        // Exclude current user liquidity as liquidator may sell collateral after liquidation in\n        // which case the slippage would not included deposited L.\n        // underflow has been checked at 'Ammalgam: Insufficient liquidity'.\n        unchecked {\n            debtLiquidityAssets = increaseForSlippage(debtLiquidityAssets, activeLiquidityAssets);\n\n            if (collateralLiquidityAssets * LTVMAX_IN_MAG2 < debtLiquidityAssets * MAG2) revert AmmalgamLTV();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Increases an L-denominated debt amount by the constant-product slippage needed\n     *   to buy that debt against the pool's active liquidity.\n     *\n     *   Let `D` be `debtLiquidityAssets` and `L` be `activeLiquidityAssets`. The function\n     *   reverts when `D >= L`; otherwise it returns:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   D_{in}=\\left\\lceil\\frac{L\\cdot D}{L-D}\\right\\rceil\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   This is the amount of L assets that must enter the pool to remove `D` L assets\n     *   of debt from the opposite reserve under the constant-product approximation.\n     *\n     * @param debtLiquidityAssets The amount of debt with units of L that will need to be purchased in case of liquidation.\n     * @param activeLiquidityAssets The amount of liquidity in the pool available to swap against.\n     */\n    function increaseForSlippage(\n        uint256 debtLiquidityAssets,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        if (debtLiquidityAssets >= activeLiquidityAssets) {\n            revert AmmalgamMaxSlippage();\n        }\n        return Math.ceilDiv(activeLiquidityAssets * debtLiquidityAssets, (activeLiquidityAssets - debtLiquidityAssets));\n    }\n\n    function checkLeverage(\n        CheckLtvParams memory checkLtvParams\n    ) private pure {\n        unchecked {\n            uint256 totalNetDeposits = checkLtvParams.netDepositedXinLAssets + checkLtvParams.netDepositedYinLAssets;\n            uint256 totalNetDebts = checkLtvParams.netBorrowedXinLAssets + checkLtvParams.netBorrowedYinLAssets;\n\n            if (totalNetDebts > 0) {\n                if (\n                    totalNetDeposits < totalNetDebts\n                        || (totalNetDeposits - totalNetDebts) * ALLOWED_LIQUIDITY_LEVERAGE < totalNetDeposits\n                ) {\n                    revert AmmalgamTooMuchLeverage();\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },

      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only\npragma solidity ^0.8.34;\n\nimport {Math} from '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol';\n\nimport {Q56, Q72, MAG2, BUFFER, BUFFER_NUMERATOR} from 'contracts/libraries/constants.sol';\nimport {ROUNDING_UP} from 'contracts/interfaces/tokens/ITokenController.sol';\n\nlibrary Convert {\n    function toLiquidityAssets(\n        uint256 liquidityShares,\n        uint256 reservesAssets,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets,\n        uint256 depositLiquidityAssets,\n        uint256 depositLiquidityShares\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        // This calculation is derived from the original formula:\n        // amountAssets = amountShares * (depositLiquidityAssets / depositLiquidityShares) * reservesAssets / activeLiquidityAssets.\n        return Convert.mulDiv(\n            Convert.mulDiv(liquidityShares, reservesAssets, activeLiquidityAssets, false),\n            depositLiquidityAssets,\n            depositLiquidityShares,\n            false\n        );\n    }\n\n    function toAssets(\n        uint256 shares,\n        uint256 totalAssets,\n        uint256 totalShares,\n        bool roundingUp\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 _assets) {\n        if (totalShares == 0) {\n            return shares; // If no shares, assets are equal to shares.\n        }\n        return mulDiv(shares, totalAssets, totalShares, roundingUp);\n    }\n\n    function toShares(\n        uint256 assets,\n        uint256 totalAssets,\n        uint256 totalShares,\n        bool roundingUp\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 _shares) {\n        if (totalAssets == 0) {\n            return assets; // If no assets, shares are equal to assets.\n        }\n        return mulDiv(assets, totalShares, totalAssets, roundingUp);\n    }\n\n    function mulDiv(uint256 x, uint256 y, uint256 z, bool roundingUp) internal pure returns (uint256 result) {\n        result = x * y;\n        result = roundingUp ? Math.ceilDiv(result, z) : result / z;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice helper method to calculate balance adjustment for missing assets\n     * @dev In the depleted case the adjusted reserve is `(reserve - missing) * bufferNumerator`,\n     *      matching the `BUFFER_NUMERATOR` scaling applied by `calculateBalanceAfterFees` so the\n     *      K comparison stays division-free.\n     *      For updateObservation, different scaled `buffer` and `bufferNumerator` values\n     *      are supplied so the adjusted reserve reflects observation-specific logic.\n     * @param reserve the starting reserve\n     * @param missing the missing assets, zero if deposits > borrows of X or Y\n     * @param buffer  Scaling factor applied to the reserve for the depletion comparison.\n     * @param bufferNumerator  Scaling factor applied to the missing amount for the comparison\n     *                         and for computing the depleted-case adjusted reserve.\n     * @return reserveAdjustment The adjusted reserve value used for swap or updateObservation\n     *                           depends on the buffer, bufferNumerator to be passed in.\n     */\n    function calculateReserveAdjustmentsForMissingAssets(\n        uint256 reserve,\n        uint256 missing,\n        uint256 buffer,\n        uint256 bufferNumerator\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 reserveAdjustment) {\n        reserveAdjustment = reserve * buffer < missing * bufferNumerator\n            ? (reserve - missing) * bufferNumerator // depleted case\n            : reserve; // not depleted case\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Depletion-adjusted active liquidity, `sqrt` of the adjusted-reserve product using the\n     *  swap K-check buffer. Shared by interest accrual and `TokenController.calculateActiveLiquidityAssets`\n     *  so every active-liquidity reader uses one basis.\n     * @param reserveXAssets reserve X used for the active-liquidity calculation.\n     * @param reserveYAssets reserve Y used for the active-liquidity calculation.\n     * @param missingXAssets missing X assets, zero if deposits > borrows of X.\n     * @param missingYAssets missing Y assets, zero if deposits > borrows of Y.\n     * @return The depletion-adjusted active liquidity.\n     */\n    function depletionAdjustedActiveLiquidity(\n        uint256 reserveXAssets,\n        uint256 reserveYAssets,\n        uint256 missingXAssets,\n        uint256 missingYAssets\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.sqrt(\n            calculateReserveAdjustmentsForMissingAssets(reserveXAssets, missingXAssets, BUFFER, BUFFER_NUMERATOR)\n                * calculateReserveAdjustmentsForMissingAssets(reserveYAssets, missingYAssets, BUFFER, BUFFER_NUMERATOR)\n        );\n    }\n}\n"

'@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol';\nimport {SafeCast} from '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/SafeCast.sol';\n\nimport {IFactoryCallback} from ZERO_ADDRESS,\n    INTEREST_PERIOD_FOR_SWAP,\n    MAG2\n} from 'contracts/libraries/constants.sol';\nimport {InitializablePair} from 'contracts/proxy/PairBeaconProxy.sol';\nimport {ISaturationAndGeometricTWAPState} from 'contracts/interfaces/ISaturationAndGeometricTWAPState.sol';\nimport {Validation} from 'contracts/libraries/Validation.sol';\n\n/**\n * @dev Wrapper of the ERC20 tokens that has some functionality similar to the ERC1155.\n */\ncontract TokenController is InitializablePair, ITokenController {\n    // These can not be constants as they are defined in initialize()\n    // slither-disable-start constable-states\n    IERC20 private tokenX;\n    IERC20 private tokenY;\n    IAmmalgamERC20 private _tokenDepositL;\n    IAmmalgamERC20 private _tokenDepositX;\n    IAmmalgamERC20 private _tokenDepositY;\n    IAmmalgamERC20 private _tokenBorrowL;\n    IAmmalgamERC20 private _tokenBorrowX;\n    IAmmalgamERC20 private _tokenBorrowY;\n\n    // State is initialized in initialize()\n    // slither-disable-start uninitialized-state\n    IFactoryCallback internal factory;\n    ISaturationAndGeometricTWAPState internal saturationAndGeometricTWAPState;\n    // slither-disable-end uninitialized-state\n    // slither-disable-end constable-states\n    uint112[6] private allShares;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice The first position in this array is never stored because it is instead computed as\n     *         $\\sqrt{reserveX\\cdot reserveY}+borrowedLAssets$. We keep this storage spot because\n     *         we heavily use DEPOSIT_L, DEPOSIT_X, DEPOSIT_Y, BORROW_L, BORROW_X, BORROW_Y to look\n     *         up positions throughout the code and didn't want to change all those references.\n     */\n    uint112[6] private allAssets;\n\n    uint112 private reserveXAssets;\n    uint112 private reserveYAssets;\n    uint32 internal lastUpdateTimestamp;\n    uint112 internal referenceReserveX;\n    uint112 internal referenceReserveY;\n    uint32 internal lastLendingTimestamp;\n\n    uint112 public override externalLiquidity;\n    uint112 public override fragileLiquidityShares;\n    mapping(address => uint256) internal userFragileLiquidityShares;\n\n    uint112 internal transient totalDepositLAssets;\n    uint112 internal transient totalDepositXAssets;\n    uint112 internal transient totalDepositYAssets;\n    uint112 internal transient totalBorrowLAssets;\n    uint112 internal transient totalBorrowXAssets;\n    uint112 internal transient totalBorrowYAssets;\n    uint112 internal transient activeLiquidityAssets;\n\n    error Forbidden();\n\n    function _initialize() internal virtual override {\n        IAmmalgamERC20[6] memory tokenData;\n        factory = IFactoryCallback(msg.sender);\n\n        // We are calling a trusted party, the factory creating the pair contract.\n        // slither-disable-next-line reentrancy-benign\n        (tokenX, tokenY, tokenData) = factory.generateTokensWithinFactory();\n\n        _tokenDepositL = tokenData[DEPOSIT_L];\n        _tokenDepositX = tokenData[DEPOSIT_X];\n        _tokenDepositY = tokenData[DEPOSIT_Y];\n        _tokenBorrowL = tokenData[BORROW_L];\n        _tokenBorrowX = tokenData[BORROW_X];\n        _tokenBorrowY = tokenData[BORROW_Y];\n\n        saturationAndGeometricTWAPState = ISaturationAndGeometricTWAPState(factory.saturationAndGeometricTWAPState());\n    }\n\n    modifier onlyFeeToSetter() {\n        _onlyFeeToSetter();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    function _onlyFeeToSetter() private view {\n        if (msg.sender != factory.feeToSetter()) {\n            revert Forbidden();\n        }\n    }\n\n    function underlyingTokens() public view virtual override returns (IERC20, IERC20) {\n        return (tokenX, tokenY);\n    }\n\n    function updateAssets(uint256 tokenType, uint112 assets) internal {\n        if (tokenType > DEPOSIT_L) allAssets[tokenType] = assets;\n\n        // totalDepositLAssets == 0 => no transient vars set yet\n        // slither-disable-next-line incorrect-equality\n        if (totalDepositLAssets == 0) return;\n\n        if (tokenType == DEPOSIT_L) {\n            totalDepositLAssets = assets;\n        } else if (tokenType == DEPOSIT_X) {\n            totalDepositXAssets = assets;\n        } else if (tokenType == DEPOSIT_Y) {\n            totalDepositYAssets = assets;\n        } else if (tokenType == BORROW_L) {\n            totalBorrowLAssets = assets;\n        } else if (tokenType == BORROW_X) {\n            totalBorrowXAssets = assets;\n        } else if (tokenType == BORROW_Y) {\n            totalBorrowYAssets = assets;\n        }\n    }\n\n    function updateExternalLiquidity(\n        uint112 _externalLiquidity\n    ) external virtual onlyFeeToSetter {\n        accrueSaturationPenaltiesAndInterest(ZERO_ADDRESS, DEFAULT_INTEREST_PERIOD);\n        (, uint256 _activeLiquidityAssets) = getDepositAndActiveLiquidityAssets();\n        // slither-disable-next-line uninitialized-local\n        Validation.InputParams memory inputParams;\n\n        inputParams.activeLiquidityAssets = _activeLiquidityAssets + _externalLiquidity;\n        inputParams.activeLiquidityAssets -= fragileLiquidityAssets(inputParams.activeLiquidityAssets);\n        // slither-disable-next-line reentrancy-benign,reentrancy-events\n        saturationAndGeometricTWAPState.update(inputParams, ZERO_ADDRESS, false);\n        externalLiquidity = _externalLiquidity;\n        emit UpdateExternalLiquidity(_externalLiquidity);\n    }\n\n    function mintId(uint256 tokenType, address sender, address to, uint256 assets, uint256 shares_) internal {\n        uint112 mintedShares = SafeCast.toUint112(shares_);\n\n        allShares[tokenType] += mintedShares;\n        uint112 updatedAssets = SafeCast.toUint112(rawTotalAssets(tokenType) + assets);\n        updateAssets(tokenType, updatedAssets);\n\n        // slither-disable-next-line reentrancy-no-eth,reentrancy-benign,reentrancy-events\n        tokens(tokenType).ownerMint(sender, to, assets, shares_);\n\n        if (tokenType == DEPOSIT_L) {\n            updateFragileLiquidity(to);\n        }\n    }\n\n    function burnId(uint256 tokenType, address sender, address receiver, uint256 assets, uint256 shares_) internal {\n        // Burn tokens first, this will ensure user does not try to repay or burn more than they own.\n        // slither-disable-next-line reentrancy-no-eth,reentrancy-benign,reentrancy-events\n        tokens(tokenType).ownerBurn(sender, receiver, assets, shares_);\n        allShares[tokenType] -= SafeCast.toUint112(shares_);\n\n        uint112 burnAssets = SafeCast.toUint112(assets);\n\n        // We use Math.max() because when there is only one owner of the current assets and they\n        // reduce their entire balance, this may underflow due to rounding up in prior\n        // calculations. If the final change amount slightly exceed the remaining assets in the\n        // pool, math max puts the asset balance at zero.\n        updateAssets(tokenType, uint112(Math.max(rawTotalAssets(tokenType), burnAssets) - burnAssets));\n    }\n\n    function tokens(\n        uint256 tokenType\n    ) public view virtual override returns (IAmmalgamERC20) {\n        // slither-disable-next-line reentrancy-no-eth,reentrancy-benign,reentrancy-events\n        return [_tokenDepositL, _tokenDepositX, _tokenDepositY, _tokenBorrowL, _tokenBorrowX, _tokenBorrowY][tokenType];\n    }\n\n    function balanceOf(address account, uint256 tokenType) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        // slither-disable-next-line reentrancy-no-eth,reentrancy-benign,reentrancy-events,calls-loop\n        return tokens(tokenType).balanceOf(account);\n    }\n\n    function totalShares(\n        uint256 tokenType\n    ) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        return allShares[tokenType];\n    }\n\n    function rawTotalAssets(\n        uint256 tokenType\n    ) internal view returns (uint256 assetAmount) {\n        if (tokenType == DEPOSIT_L) {\n            (assetAmount,) = getDepositAndActiveLiquidityAssets();\n        } else {\n            assetAmount = allAssets[tokenType];\n        }\n    }\n\n    function getRawReserves() internal view returns (uint112 _reserveXAssets, uint112 _reserveYAssets) {\n        _reserveXAssets = reserveXAssets;\n        _reserveYAssets = reserveYAssets;\n    }\n\n    function getReserves()\n        public\n        view\n        virtual\n        returns (uint112 _reserveXAssets, uint112 _reserveYAssets, uint32 _lastUpdateTimestamp)\n    {\n        _lastUpdateTimestamp = lastUpdateTimestamp;\n\n        uint32 deltaUpdateTimestamp;\n        // underflow is desired\n        unchecked {\n            deltaUpdateTimestamp = GeometricTWAP.getCurrentTimestamp() - _lastUpdateTimestamp;\n        }\n\n        if (deltaUpdateTimestamp > INTEREST_PERIOD_FOR_SWAP) {\n            (, _reserveXAssets, _reserveYAssets,) = computeAssetsState();\n        } else {\n            _reserveXAssets = reserveXAssets;\n            _reserveYAssets = reserveYAssets;\n        }\n    }\n\n    function referenceReserves() external view virtual returns (uint112, uint112) {\n        uint32 deltaUpdateTimestamp;\n        unchecked {\n            deltaUpdateTimestamp = GeometricTWAP.getCurrentTimestamp() - lastUpdateTimestamp;\n        }\n        if (deltaUpdateTimestamp < saturationAndGeometricTWAPState.midTermIntervalConfig()) {\n            return (referenceReserveX, referenceReserveY);\n        }\n\n        // We always use raw reserves to compute reference reserves, because they represent the last actual price state.\n        return getUpdatedReferenceReserves(TickMath.getTickFromReserves(reserveXAssets, reserveYAssets));\n    }\n\n    function totalAssetsAndShares(\n        bool withInterest\n    ) public view virtual returns (uint112[6] memory _allAssets, uint112[6] memory _allShares) {\n        // return stored values if no interest is to be applied\n        if (!withInterest) {\n            _allAssets = allAssets;\n            (uint256 totalLAssets,) = getDepositAndActiveLiquidityAssets();\n            _allAssets[DEPOSIT_L] = uint112(totalLAssets);\n            return (_allAssets, allShares);\n        }\n\n        uint256[3] memory protocolFees;\n        uint112 _reserveXAssets;\n        uint112 _reserveYAssets;\n        (_allAssets, _reserveXAssets, _reserveYAssets, protocolFees) = computeAssetsState();\n\n        _allShares = allShares;\n\n        // Mint protocol fees only to deposit tokens.\n        // Convert each fee amount to shares (before updating assets) to preserve ratio accuracy.\n        // Then add the fee amount to the corresponding asset balance.\n        for (uint256 i; i < FIRST_DEBT_TOKEN; i++) {\n            // it's safe down cast here because protocolFees < interest\n            // and interest <= type(uint112).max - max(depositedAssets, borrowedAssets)\n            // due to the check in computeInterestAssetsGivenRate()\n            // DEPOSIT_L already counts the fee in _allAssets[i] via BORROW_L, so divide by the pre-fee total.\n            _allShares[i] += uint112(\n                Convert.toShares(\n                    protocolFees[i],\n                    i == DEPOSIT_L ? _allAssets[i] - uint112(protocolFees[i]) : _allAssets[i],\n                    _allShares[i],\n                    ROUNDING_UP\n                )\n            );\n            // For deposited liquidity, we calculate growth using\n            // $$\\sqrt{reserveX * reserveY} + borrowedLAssets$$, We add protocol fees to borrowed L\n            // in computeAssetsState() -> accrueInterestWithAssets() and due to the dependence of\n            // borrowed L to compute deposited L, we would double count them if we added them here.\n            if (i > DEPOSIT_L) _allAssets[i] += uint112(protocolFees[i]);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Computes fragile liquidity and validates it can be removed from active liquidity.\n     * @param _activeLiquidityAssets The active liquidity available before the fragile liquidity decrement.\n     */\n    function fragileLiquidityAssets(\n        uint256 _activeLiquidityAssets\n    ) internal view returns (uint256 _fragileLiquidityAssets) {\n        _fragileLiquidityAssets = Convert.toAssets(\n            fragileLiquidityShares, rawTotalAssets(DEPOSIT_L), totalShares(DEPOSIT_L), ROUNDING_UP\n        ) * FRAGILE_LIQUIDITY_DECREMENT_PERCENTAGE / MAG2;\n\n        if (_fragileLiquidityAssets > _activeLiquidityAssets) {\n            revert FragileLiquidityExceedsActiveLiquidity();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice used to update fragileLiquidityShares that have a borrow of x or y against them\n     * @dev  We then need to increase fragile liquidity when\n     *   1. a new borrow of x or y is made when none existed and the user has l deposits.\n     *   2. l deposits are minted to a user with existing borrows of x or y.\n     *   3. l deposits are transferred to a user with no existing l and but borrows of x or y.\n     *   4. debt of x or y is transferred to a user with l shares but no debt existing x or y debt.\n     *   Fragile liquidity then needs to be decreased when\n     *   1. all borrows of x and y are repaid for a user with l deposits.\n     *   2. l deposits are burned from a user with existing borrows of x or y.\n     *   3. l deposits are transferred from a user with borrows of x or y to a user with no borrow\n     *      of x or y.\n     *   4. last debt of x or y is transferred from a user with l deposits to a user with no l\n     *      deposits.\n     * @param user the address to update fragile liquidity for.\n     */\n    function updateFragileLiquidity(\n        address user\n    ) internal {\n        uint256 priorFragileShares = userFragileLiquidityShares[user];\n        uint256 balanceLShares = balanceOf(user, DEPOSIT_L);\n        bool debtOfXOrY = balanceOf(user, BORROW_X) > 0 || balanceOf(user, BORROW_Y) > 0;\n\n        if (balanceLShares > priorFragileShares && debtOfXOrY) {\n            // adding\n\n            userFragileLiquidityShares[user] = balanceLShares;\n            fragileLiquidityShares += uint112(balanceLShares - priorFragileShares);\n        } else if (priorFragileShares > balanceLShares || (!debtOfXOrY && priorFragileShares > 0)) {\n            // removing\n\n            uint256 newBalanceLShares = debtOfXOrY ? balanceLShares : 0;\n\n            userFragileLiquidityShares[user] = newBalanceLShares;\n            fragileLiquidityShares -= uint112(priorFragileShares - newBalanceLShares);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Recalculates current total assets, reserves, and protocol fees, accounting for elapsed time and interest.\n     *\n     * @dev Core logic for interest accrual and state updates (used by `totalAssetsAndShares` when `withInterest` is true):\n     *      1. Fetches raw reserves before computing interest.\n     *      2. If `totalDepositLAssets` is not 0, returns transient asset values immediately (no interest to accrue).\n     *      3. Calculates time elapsed since last update (`deltaUpdateTimestamp`) and last lending state check (`deltaLendingTimestamp`).\n     *      4. If no time has elapsed since last lending check (`deltaLendingTimestamp == 0`), returns stored values without recalculation.\n     *      5. Otherwise:\n     *         - Computes the current market tick via `getTickFromReserves()` and bounds it to valid ranges.\n     *         - Determines active lending state tick and saturation percentage using `getLendingStateTick()`.\n     *         - Calls `Interest.accrueInterestWithAssets()` to calculate interest, update asset values, and compute protocol fees.\n     *         - Adds LP-earned interest portions to X and Y reserves.\n     *\n     * @return _allAssets Array of six `uint112` values: Recalculated total assets for each of the 6 Ammalgam token types (post-interest).\n     * @return _reserveXAssets Reserve balance for Asset X, updated with LP-earned interest.\n     * @return _reserveYAssets Reserve balance for Asset Y, updated with LP-earned interest.\n     * @return protocolFees Array of three `uint256` values: Accumulated protocol fees for DEPOSIT_L, DEPOSIT_X, and DEPOSIT_Y (from interest accrual).\n     */\n    function computeAssetsState()\n        internal\n        view\n        returns (\n            uint112[6] memory _allAssets,\n            uint112 _reserveXAssets,\n            uint112 _reserveYAssets,\n            uint256[3] memory protocolFees\n        )\n    {\n        (_reserveXAssets, _reserveYAssets) = getRawReserves();\n\n        if (totalDepositLAssets != 0) {\n            return (\n                [\n                    totalDepositLAssets,\n                    totalDepositXAssets,\n                    totalDepositYAssets,\n                    totalBorrowLAssets,\n                    totalBorrowXAssets,\n                    totalBorrowYAssets\n                ],\n                _reserveXAssets,\n                _reserveYAssets,\n                protocolFees\n            );\n        }\n\n        uint32 currentTimestamp = GeometricTWAP.getCurrentTimestamp();\n        uint32 deltaUpdateTimestamp;\n        uint32 deltaLendingTimestamp;\n        unchecked {\n            deltaUpdateTimestamp = currentTimestamp - lastUpdateTimestamp;\n            deltaLendingTimestamp = currentTimestamp - lastLendingTimestamp;\n        }\n\n        _allAssets = allAssets;\n\n        // Set active liquidity assets for needed calculations in interest accrual and transient\n        // storage\n        (uint256 depositLAssets,) = getDepositAndActiveLiquidityAssets();\n        _allAssets[DEPOSIT_L] = uint112(depositLAssets);\n\n        if (deltaLendingTimestamp == 0) return (_allAssets, _reserveXAssets, _reserveYAssets, protocolFees);\n\n        (int16 lendingStateTick, uint256 satPercentageInWads) = saturationAndGeometricTWAPState.getLendingStateTick(\n            TickMath.getTickFromReserves(_reserveXAssets, _reserveYAssets), deltaUpdateTimestamp, deltaLendingTimestamp\n        );\n        uint256 interestXPortionForLP;\n        uint256 interestYPortionForLP;\n        (_allAssets, interestXPortionForLP, interestYPortionForLP, protocolFees) = Interest.accrueInterestWithAssets(\n            _allAssets,\n            Interest.AccrueInterestParams({\n                duration: deltaLendingTimestamp,\n                lendingStateTick: lendingStateTick,\n                shares: allShares,\n                satPercentageInWads: satPercentageInWads,\n                reserveXAssets: _reserveXAssets,\n                reserveYAssets: _reserveYAssets\n            })\n        );\n\n        // cast is safe which is assured by accrueInterestWithAssets\n        _reserveXAssets += uint112(interestXPortionForLP);\n        _reserveYAssets += uint112(interestYPortionForLP);\n    }\n\n    function accrueSaturationPenaltiesAndInterest(\n        address affectedAccount,\n        uint256 minimumTimeBeforeUpdate\n    )\n        internal\n        returns (uint256 _reserveXAssets, uint256 _reserveYAssets, uint256 balanceXAssets, uint256 balanceYAssets)\n    {\n        (_reserveXAssets, _reserveYAssets) = getRawReserves();\n        if (_reserveXAssets > 0 && _reserveYAssets > 0) {\n            uint32 currentTimestamp = GeometricTWAP.getCurrentTimestamp();\n            uint32 deltaUpdateTimestamp;\n            unchecked {\n                deltaUpdateTimestamp = currentTimestamp - lastUpdateTimestamp;\n            }\n\n            updateObservation(_reserveXAssets, _reserveYAssets, currentTimestamp, deltaUpdateTimestamp);\n\n            // Enter the lending checkpoint once `deltaLendingTimestamp >= minimumTimeBeforeUpdate`.\n            // `minimumTimeBeforeUpdate` is DEFAULT_INTEREST_PERIOD (0) for every caller except swap, so\n            // it normally enters on any elapsed time; swap passes INTEREST_PERIOD_FOR_SWAP (~1 day) to\n            // throttle accrual to at most once per day.\n            uint32 deltaLendingTimestamp;\n            unchecked {\n                deltaLendingTimestamp = currentTimestamp - lastLendingTimestamp;\n            }\n            if (minimumTimeBeforeUpdate <= deltaLendingTimestamp) {\n                // penalties\n                mintPenalties(affectedAccount, deltaLendingTimestamp);\n\n                // slither-disable-next-line incorrect-equality\n                if (deltaLendingTimestamp > 0) {\n                    // in the update, we mint protocol fees and update transient storage for allAssets\n                    (_reserveXAssets, _reserveYAssets) = updateTokenController(\n                        currentTimestamp, deltaUpdateTimestamp, deltaLendingTimestamp, _reserveXAssets, _reserveYAssets\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        (balanceXAssets, balanceYAssets) = getNetBalances(_reserveXAssets, _reserveYAssets);\n    }\n\n    // slither-disable-next-line naming-convention\n    function updateObservation(\n        uint256 _reserveXAssets,\n        uint256 _reserveYAssets,\n        uint32 currentTimestamp,\n        uint32 deltaUpdateTimestamp\n    ) private {\n        if (0 < deltaUpdateTimestamp && 0 < _reserveXAssets && 0 < _reserveYAssets) {\n            (uint256 missingX, uint256 missingY) = missingAssets();\n\n            int16 newTick = TickMath.getTickFromReserves(\n                Convert.calculateReserveAdjustmentsForMissingAssets(\n                    _reserveXAssets, missingX, BUFFER_OBS, BUFFER_OBS_NUMERATOR\n
