),\n                Convert.calculateReserveAdjustmentsForMissingAssets(\n                    _reserveYAssets, missingY, BUFFER_OBS, BUFFER_OBS_NUMERATOR\n                )\n            );\n\n            // Call to trusted contract holding some pair state.\n            // slither-disable-next-line reentrancy-benign,reentrancy-no-eth\n            if (saturationAndGeometricTWAPState.recordObservation(newTick, deltaUpdateTimestamp)) {\n                lastUpdateTimestamp = currentTimestamp;\n                updateReferenceReserve(newTick);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    function mintPenalties(address account, uint32 deltaLendingTimestamp) internal {\n        if (account != ZERO_ADDRESS || deltaLendingTimestamp > 0) {\n            // add penalty before interest because penalty state existed for the duration\n            // mint DL and BL for pair to the amount of penalty [LAssets] since the previous state update in total\n            uint256 allAssetsDepositL = rawTotalAssets(DEPOSIT_L);\n            uint256 allAssetsBorrowL = rawTotalAssets(BORROW_L);\n            uint256 allSharesBorrowL = totalShares(BORROW_L);\n\n            // slither-disable-next-line reentrancy-no-eth,reentrancy-benign,reentrancy-events External contract is under our control\n            (uint256 totalPenaltyInBorrowLShares, uint256 accountPenaltyInBorrowLShares) =\n            saturationAndGeometricTWAPState.accruePenalties(\n                account,\n                externalLiquidity,\n                deltaLendingTimestamp,\n                allAssetsDepositL,\n                allAssetsBorrowL,\n                allSharesBorrowL,\n                fragileLiquidityAssets(allAssetsDepositL + externalLiquidity - allAssetsBorrowL)\n            );\n\n            if (totalPenaltyInBorrowLShares > 0) {\n                // defensive check transient state should not be set for this path.\n                // slither-disable-next-line incorrect-equality\n                require(totalDepositLAssets == 0, 'TS');\n                uint256 totalPenaltyInLAssets =\n                    Convert.toAssets(totalPenaltyInBorrowLShares, allAssetsBorrowL, allSharesBorrowL, ROUNDING_UP);\n\n                // mint BL for penalty not minted to any specific account\n                // DL is derived from sqrt(reserveX * reserveY) + BL, so updating BL automatically\n                // updates DL.\n                mintId(BORROW_L, address(this), address(this), totalPenaltyInLAssets, totalPenaltyInBorrowLShares);\n            }\n\n            // update the account, we use the min in case rounding up exceeds the amount of shares\n            // if there where only one account in penalty that receives the whole penalty.\n            if (0 < accountPenaltyInBorrowLShares) {\n                tokens(BORROW_L).ownerTransfer(\n                    address(this),\n                    account,\n                    Math.min(accountPenaltyInBorrowLShares, _tokenBorrowL.balanceOf(address(this)))\n                );\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    function getAssets(\n        uint112[6] memory _totalAssets,\n        uint112[6] memory _totalShares,\n        address toCheck\n    ) internal view returns (uint256[6] memory userAssets) {\n        for (uint256 i; i < TOKEN_COUNT; i++) {\n            uint256 currentShares = balanceOf(toCheck, i);\n            if (0 < currentShares) {\n                // FIRST_DEBT_TOKEN <= i <=> rounding up for borrow tokens\n                userAssets[i] = Convert.toAssets(currentShares, _totalAssets[i], _totalShares[i], FIRST_DEBT_TOKEN <= i);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    function updateTokenController(\n        uint32 currentTimestamp,\n        uint32 deltaUpdateTimestamp,\n        uint32 deltaLendingTimestamp,\n        uint256 _reserveXAssets,\n        uint256 _reserveYAssets\n    ) internal returns (uint256 updatedReservesX, uint256 updatedReservesY) {\n        // slither-disable-next-line reentrancy-benign External contract is under our control\n        (int16 lendingStateTick, uint256 satPercentageInWads) = saturationAndGeometricTWAPState\n            .getLendingStateTickAndCheckpoint(deltaUpdateTimestamp, deltaLendingTimestamp);\n\n        lastLendingTimestamp = currentTimestamp;\n\n        (uint256 interestXForLP, uint256 interestYForLP, uint256[3] memory protocolFeeAssets) = Interest\n            .accrueInterestAndUpdateReservesWithAssets(\n            allAssets,\n            Interest.AccrueInterestParams({\n                duration: deltaLendingTimestamp,\n                lendingStateTick: lendingStateTick,\n                shares: allShares,\n                satPercentageInWads: satPercentageInWads,\n                reserveXAssets: _reserveXAssets,\n                reserveYAssets: _reserveYAssets\n            })\n        );\n\n        updatedReservesX = _reserveXAssets + interestXForLP;\n        updatedReservesY = _reserveYAssets + interestYForLP;\n\n        updateReserves(updatedReservesX, updatedReservesY);\n\n        address feeTo = factory.feeTo();\n\n        // DEPOSIT_L fee is already in its total via BORROW_L so we back it out to compute shares to mint.\n        // X and Y fees are not included yet.\n        mintProtocolFees(DEPOSIT_L, feeTo, protocolFeeAssets[DEPOSIT_L], true);\n        mintProtocolFees(DEPOSIT_X, feeTo, protocolFeeAssets[DEPOSIT_X], false);\n        mintProtocolFees(DEPOSIT_Y, feeTo, protocolFeeAssets[DEPOSIT_Y], false);\n\n        (uint256 _totalDepositLAssets, uint256 _activeLiquidityAssets) = getDepositAndActiveLiquidityAssets();\n        totalDepositLAssets = uint112(_totalDepositLAssets);\n        activeLiquidityAssets = uint112(_activeLiquidityAssets);\n        totalDepositXAssets = uint112(rawTotalAssets(DEPOSIT_X));\n        totalDepositYAssets = uint112(rawTotalAssets(DEPOSIT_Y));\n        totalBorrowLAssets = uint112(rawTotalAssets(BORROW_L));\n        totalBorrowXAssets = uint112(rawTotalAssets(BORROW_X));\n        totalBorrowYAssets = uint112(rawTotalAssets(BORROW_Y));\n    }\n\n    function updateReferenceReserve(\n        int256 newTick\n    ) internal {\n        (referenceReserveX, referenceReserveY) = getUpdatedReferenceReserves(newTick);\n    }\n\n    function mintProtocolFees(\n        uint256 tokenType,\n        address feeTo,\n        uint256 protocolFee,\n        bool feeIncludedInTotalAssets\n    ) internal {\n        if (protocolFee > 0) {\n            // Back the fee out of the denominator when it is already counted in rawTotalAssets(tokenType).\n            uint256 totalDepositedAssets = rawTotalAssets(tokenType);\n            if (feeIncludedInTotalAssets) {\n                totalDepositedAssets -= protocolFee;\n            }\n            mintId(\n                tokenType,\n                address(this),\n                feeTo,\n                protocolFee,\n                Convert.toShares(protocolFee, totalDepositedAssets, totalShares(tokenType), ROUNDING_UP)\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    function updateReserves(uint256 newReserveXAssets, uint256 newReserveYAssets) internal {\n        (uint112 _castedXAssets, uint112 _castedYAssets) = _castReserves(newReserveXAssets, newReserveYAssets);\n\n        reserveXAssets = _castedXAssets;\n        reserveYAssets = _castedYAssets;\n\n        // Update cached active liquidity if transient state is set\n        // slither-disable-next-line incorrect-equality\n        if (totalDepositLAssets != 0) {\n            uint112 newActiveLiquidity = uint112(calculateActiveLiquidityAssets(newReserveXAssets, newReserveYAssets));\n            activeLiquidityAssets = newActiveLiquidity;\n\n            updateAssets(DEPOSIT_L, newActiveLiquidity + totalBorrowLAssets);\n        }\n\n        // slither-disable-next-line reentrancy-events\n        emit Sync(_castedXAssets, _castedYAssets);\n    }\n\n    function updateReservesAndReference(\n        uint256 _reserveXAssets,\n        uint256 _reserveYAssets,\n        uint256 newReserveXAssets,\n        uint256 newReserveYAssets\n    ) internal {\n        updateReserves(newReserveXAssets, newReserveYAssets);\n        (referenceReserveX, referenceReserveY) = _castReserves(\n            Convert.mulDiv(referenceReserveX, newReserveXAssets, _reserveXAssets, false),\n            Convert.mulDiv(referenceReserveY, newReserveYAssets, _reserveYAssets, false)\n        );\n    }\n\n    function _castReserves(uint256 _reserveXAssets, uint256 _reserveYAssets) internal pure returns (uint112, uint112) {\n        return (SafeCast.toUint112(_reserveXAssets), SafeCast.toUint112(_reserveYAssets));\n    }\n\n    // slither-disable-next-line naming-convention\n    function getNetBalances(\n        uint256 _reserveXAssets,\n        uint256 _reserveYAssets\n    ) internal view returns (uint256, uint256) {\n        (IERC20 _tokenX, IERC20 _tokenY) = underlyingTokens();\n\n        return (\n            _tokenX.balanceOf(address(this)) + rawTotalAssets(BORROW_X) - rawTotalAssets(DEPOSIT_X) - _reserveXAssets,\n            _tokenY.balanceOf(address(this)) + rawTotalAssets(BORROW_Y) - rawTotalAssets(DEPOSIT_Y) - _reserveYAssets\n        );\n    }\n\n    function missingAssets() internal view returns (uint112 missingXAssets, uint112 missingYAssets) {\n        uint256 depositXAssets = rawTotalAssets(DEPOSIT_X);\n        uint256 depositYAssets = rawTotalAssets(DEPOSIT_Y);\n        uint256 borrowXAssets = rawTotalAssets(BORROW_X);\n        uint256 borrowYAssets = rawTotalAssets(BORROW_Y);\n        // no need to safe cast.\n        // 0 <= borrow <= uint112.max\n        // 0 <= deposit <= uint112.max\n        // -uint256.max <= -deposit <= 0\n        // -uint256.max <= borrow - deposit <= uint112.max\n        missingXAssets = uint112(Math.max(borrowXAssets, depositXAssets) - depositXAssets);\n        missingYAssets = uint112(Math.max(borrowYAssets, depositYAssets) - depositYAssets);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Active liquidity from reserves, using the same depletion adjustment as the swap\n     *  K-check in `calculateReserveAdjustmentsForMissingAssets`. Caching raw `sqrt(X*Y)` is\n     *  non-monotonic across depletion cycles, letting saturation-tree leaves register above the\n     *  post-recovery `maxLeaf` and bricking the pair with `MaxTrancheOverSaturated()`.\n     * @param _reserveXAssets The reserve X used for the active-liquidity calculation.\n     * @param _reserveYAssets The reserve Y used for the active-liquidity calculation.\n     * @return The depletion-adjusted active liquidity, i.e. sqrt of the adjusted-reserve product.\n     */\n    function calculateActiveLiquidityAssets(\n        uint256 _reserveXAssets,\n        uint256 _reserveYAssets\n    ) internal view returns (uint256) {\n        (uint112 _missingXAssets, uint112 _missingYAssets) = missingAssets();\n        return\n            Convert.depletionAdjustedActiveLiquidity(_reserveXAssets, _reserveYAssets, _missingXAssets, _missingYAssets);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Get the deposit, and active liquidity assets.\n     * @return depositLiquidityAssets The deposit liquidity assets.\n     * @return currentActiveLiquidityAssets The current active liquidity assets.\n     */\n    function getDepositAndActiveLiquidityAssets()\n        internal\n        view\n        returns (uint256 depositLiquidityAssets, uint256 currentActiveLiquidityAssets)\n    {\n        // Return cached values if transient state is set\n        // slither-disable-next-line incorrect-equality\n        if (totalDepositLAssets != 0) {\n            return (totalDepositLAssets, activeLiquidityAssets);\n        }\n\n        currentActiveLiquidityAssets = calculateActiveLiquidityAssets(uint256(reserveXAssets), uint256(reserveYAssets));\n\n        depositLiquidityAssets = currentActiveLiquidityAssets + allAssets[BORROW_L];\n    }\n\n    function burnBadDebt(address borrower, uint256 tokenType, uint256 reserve) internal {\n        uint256 badDebtShares = tokens(tokenType).balanceOf(borrower);\n        // round down means the commons debt and commons deposit is 1 unit larger\n        uint256 badDebtAssets =\n            Convert.toAssets(badDebtShares, rawTotalAssets(tokenType), totalShares(tokenType), !ROUNDING_UP);\n\n        burnId(tokenType, address(this), borrower, badDebtAssets, badDebtShares);\n        if (tokenType == BORROW_L) {\n            // distribute the loss to the pool. This call will update transient storage for any\n            // interactions after the current one within the same transaction. Typically we don't\n            // back out burned liquidity from the deposits since depositL = sqrt(reserveX *\n            // reserveY) + borrowedL, and burning of borrowed L also requires the replenishment of\n            // the reserves leaving deposit L unchanged. With bad debt the reserves do not get\n            // replenished and thus we do need to back out bad debt.\n            updateAssets(DEPOSIT_L, SafeCast.toUint112(rawTotalAssets(DEPOSIT_L) - badDebtAssets));\n        } else {\n            uint256 totalAssetsWithReserves = rawTotalAssets(tokenType - FIRST_DEBT_TOKEN) + reserve;\n            uint256 burnReserves = Convert.mulDiv(badDebtAssets, reserve, totalAssetsWithReserves, false);\n\n            if (tokenType == BORROW_X) {\n                reserveXAssets -= SafeCast.toUint112(burnReserves);\n                updateAssets(DEPOSIT_X, SafeCast.toUint112(rawTotalAssets(DEPOSIT_X) - (badDebtAssets - burnReserves)));\n            } else {\n                reserveYAssets -= SafeCast.toUint112(burnReserves);\n                updateAssets(DEPOSIT_Y, SafeCast.toUint112(rawTotalAssets(DEPOSIT_Y) - (badDebtAssets - burnReserves)));\n            }\n            // Reserves changed, so cached active liquidity must be recalculated if transient\n            // state is set.\n            // slither-disable-next-line incorrect-equality\n            if (totalDepositLAssets != 0) {\n                uint112 newActiveLiquidity =\n                    uint112(calculateActiveLiquidityAssets(uint256(reserveXAssets), uint256(reserveYAssets)));\n                activeLiquidityAssets = newActiveLiquidity;\n                uint256 newDepositL = newActiveLiquidity + allAssets[BORROW_L];\n                updateAssets(DEPOSIT_L, SafeCast.toUint112(newDepositL));\n            }\n        }\n\n        emit BurnBadDebt(borrower, tokenType, badDebtAssets, badDebtShares);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Get the updated reference reserves based on the `newTick`.\n     * @param newTick The current tick.\n     * @return _referenceReserveX The updated reference reserve X.\n     * @return _referenceReserveY The updated reference reserve Y.\n     */\n    function getUpdatedReferenceReserves(\n        int256 newTick\n    ) internal view returns (uint112, uint112) {\n        int256 midTermTick = saturationAndGeometricTWAPState.getObservedMidTermTick();\n        (uint256 _refReserveX, uint256 _refReserveY) = Interest.getReservesAtTick(\n            rawTotalAssets(DEPOSIT_L) - rawTotalAssets(BORROW_L),\n            GeometricTWAP.calculateTickAverageTowardsMidTerm(midTermTick, newTick)\n        );\n        return _castReserves(_refReserveX, _refReserveY);\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    "lib/core-v1/contracts/libraries/Liquidation.sol": {
      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only\npragma solidity ^0.8.34;\n\nimport {Math} from '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol';\n\nimport {\n    BIPS,\n    MAG1,\n    MAG2,\n    ALLOWED_LIQUIDITY_LEVERAGE,\n    ALLOWED_LIQUIDITY_LEVERAGE_MINUS_ONE\n} from 'contracts/libraries/constants.sol';\nimport {Convert} from 'contracts/libraries/Convert.sol';\nimport {PartialLiquidations} from 'contracts/libraries/PartialLiquidations.sol';\nimport {Saturation} from 'contracts/libraries/Saturation.sol';\nimport {TickMath} from 'contracts/libraries/TickMath.sol';\nimport {Validation} from 'contracts/libraries/Validation.sol';\nimport {ISaturationAndGeometricTWAPState} from 'contracts/interfaces/ISaturationAndGeometricTWAPState.sol';\nimport {\n    DEPOSIT_L,\n    DEPOSIT_X,\n    DEPOSIT_Y,\n    BORROW_L,\n    BORROW_X,\n    BORROW_Y\n} from 'contracts/interfaces/tokens/ITokenController.sol';\nimport {ROUNDING_UP} from 'contracts/interfaces/tokens/ITokenController.sol';\n\nlibrary Liquidation {\n    // constants\n\n    uint256 internal constant START_NEGATIVE_PREMIUM_LTV_BIPS = 6000; // == 0.6\n    uint256 private constant START_PREMIUM_LTV_BIPS = 7500; // == 0.75\n    uint256 private constant NEGATIVE_PREMIUM_SLOPE_IN_BIPS = 66_667; // 20/3\n    uint256 private constant NEGATIVE_PREMIUM_INTERCEPT_IN_BIPS = 40_000; // == -4 (subtracted since stored positively)\n    uint256 private constant POSITIVE_PREMIUM_SLOPE_IN_BIPS = 7408; // == 20/27\n    uint256 private constant POSITIVE_PREMIUM_INTERCEPT_IN_BIPS = 4444; // == 4/9\n\n    /**\n     * @notice This factor brings a leveraged position just below the leverage liquidation threshold\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant LEVERAGE_LIQUIDATION_BREAK_EVEN_FACTOR_MAG2 = 10; // 0.1 MAG2 version\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Leverage liquidation factor derived from max allowed leverage scaled by the break-even factor\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant LEVERAGE_LIQUIDATION_FACTOR =\n        ALLOWED_LIQUIDITY_LEVERAGE * MAG2 / LEVERAGE_LIQUIDATION_BREAK_EVEN_FACTOR_MAG2;\n    uint256 internal constant MAX_PREMIUM_IN_BIPS = 11_111; // == 10 * BIPS / 9\n\n    uint256 internal constant HARD = 0;\n    uint256 internal constant SATURATION = 1;\n    uint256 internal constant LEVERAGE = 2;\n\n    // errors\n\n    error LiquidationPremiumTooHigh();\n    error LiquidationZeroPremium();\n    error NotEnoughRepaidForLiquidation();\n    error TooMuchDepositToTransferForLeverageLiquidation();\n    error LiquidationMutation();\n\n    function verifyHardLiquidation(\n        ISaturationAndGeometricTWAPState saturationState,\n        address pairAddress,\n        Validation.InputParams memory inputParams,\n        uint256[6] memory proposedLiquidation,\n        uint256 fragileLiquidityAssets,\n        address borrower\n    ) external view returns (uint256[6] memory partialLiquidation, bool badDebt) {\n        {\n            (uint256 netRepaidLAssets, uint256 netSeizedLAssets, bool netDebtX) = calculateNetDebtAndSeizedDeposits(\n                proposedLiquidation, inputParams.sqrtPriceMinInQ72, inputParams.sqrtPriceMaxInQ72\n            );\n\n            Saturation.Account memory account = saturationState.getAccount(pairAddress, netDebtX, borrower);\n\n            partialLiquidation = PartialLiquidations.calculatePartialLiquidation(\n                account.satPairPerTranche,\n                account.lastTranche,\n                inputParams.userAssets,\n                inputParams.activeLiquidityAssets - fragileLiquidityAssets,\n                netRepaidLAssets,\n                netSeizedLAssets,\n                netDebtX\n            );\n        }\n\n        uint256 maxAllowedPremiumBips = calcHardMaxPremiumInBips(\n            partialLiquidation,\n            inputParams.activeLiquidityAssets,\n            inputParams.sqrtPriceMinInQ72,\n            inputParams.sqrtPriceMaxInQ72\n        );\n\n        // Premium exceeds max before bad debt and all assets seized.\n        badDebt = maxAllowedPremiumBips > MAX_PREMIUM_IN_BIPS\n            && (\n                partialLiquidation[DEPOSIT_L] == inputParams.userAssets[DEPOSIT_L]\n                    && partialLiquidation[DEPOSIT_X] == inputParams.userAssets[DEPOSIT_X]\n                    && partialLiquidation[DEPOSIT_Y] == inputParams.userAssets[DEPOSIT_Y]\n            );\n\n        if (!badDebt) {\n            // Not bad debt.\n            // Debt repaid must be equal to or greater than the partial liquidation amount.\n            // Deposits seized will be less than or equal the partial liquidation amount.\n            // Verifies the repaid amount of each asset to cover the partial liquidation.\n            if (\n                proposedLiquidation[BORROW_L] < partialLiquidation[BORROW_L]\n                    || proposedLiquidation[BORROW_X] < partialLiquidation[BORROW_X]\n                    || proposedLiquidation[BORROW_Y] < partialLiquidation[BORROW_Y]\n            ) {\n                revert NotEnoughRepaidForLiquidation();\n            }\n            // Verified that proposed deposits do not exceed partial liquidation allowance.\n            if (\n                proposedLiquidation[DEPOSIT_L] > partialLiquidation[DEPOSIT_L]\n                    || proposedLiquidation[DEPOSIT_X] > partialLiquidation[DEPOSIT_X]\n                    || proposedLiquidation[DEPOSIT_Y] > partialLiquidation[DEPOSIT_Y]\n            ) {\n                revert LiquidationMutation();\n            }\n            // keep the repaid amounts from the partial liquidation and use the proposed deposits.\n            partialLiquidation[DEPOSIT_L] = proposedLiquidation[DEPOSIT_L];\n            partialLiquidation[DEPOSIT_X] = proposedLiquidation[DEPOSIT_X];\n            partialLiquidation[DEPOSIT_Y] = proposedLiquidation[DEPOSIT_Y];\n        } else {\n            // potential bad debt case.\n            // Debt repaid will be less than the partial liquidation amount, deposits will be equal\n            // to the partial liquidation amount.\n            // Verify that liquidator does not mutate by not seizing enough of one asset and more\n            // of another.\n            // Do not allow a liquidator to skip any borrow leg held by the borrower.\n            // Bad debt burns the borrower's full token balance after this validation.\n            if (\n                (\n                    proposedLiquidation[BORROW_L] > partialLiquidation[BORROW_L]\n                        || proposedLiquidation[BORROW_X] > partialLiquidation[BORROW_X]\n                        || proposedLiquidation[BORROW_Y] > partialLiquidation[BORROW_Y]\n                ) || (proposedLiquidation[BORROW_L] == 0 && partialLiquidation[BORROW_L] > 0)\n                    || (proposedLiquidation[BORROW_X] == 0 && partialLiquidation[BORROW_X] > 0)\n                    || (proposedLiquidation[BORROW_Y] == 0 && partialLiquidation[BORROW_Y] > 0)\n            ) {\n                revert LiquidationMutation();\n            }\n            // keep the deposit amount from the partial liquidation and use the proposed repaid amounts.\n            partialLiquidation[BORROW_L] = proposedLiquidation[BORROW_L];\n            partialLiquidation[BORROW_X] = proposedLiquidation[BORROW_X];\n            partialLiquidation[BORROW_Y] = proposedLiquidation[BORROW_Y];\n        }\n\n        (uint256 netDebtRepaidInLAssets, uint256 netDepositsSeizedInLAssets,) = calculateNetDebtAndSeizedDeposits(\n            partialLiquidation, inputParams.sqrtPriceMinInQ72, inputParams.sqrtPriceMaxInQ72\n        );\n\n        checkHardPremiums(netDebtRepaidInLAssets, netDepositsSeizedInLAssets, maxAllowedPremiumBips);\n    }\n\n    function checkHardPremiums(\n        uint256 repaidDebtInL,\n        uint256 seizedCollateralValueInL,\n        uint256 maxAllowedPremiumBips\n    ) internal pure {\n        uint256 premiumInBips = calcHardPremiumInBips(repaidDebtInL, seizedCollateralValueInL);\n\n        if (maxAllowedPremiumBips < premiumInBips) revert LiquidationPremiumTooHigh();\n    }\n\n    function calculateNetDebtAndSeizedDeposits(\n        uint256[6] memory proposedLiquidation,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMinInQ72,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMaxInQ72\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 netDebtInLAssets, uint256 netCollateralInLAssets, bool netDebtX) {\n        // swap min and max\n        Validation.CheckLtvParams memory checkLtvParams =\n            Validation.getCheckLtvParams(proposedLiquidation, sqrtPriceMaxInQ72, sqrtPriceMinInQ72);\n        (netDebtInLAssets, netCollateralInLAssets, netDebtX) = Validation.calcDebtAndCollateral(checkLtvParams);\n    }\n\n    function checkSaturationPremiums(\n        ISaturationAndGeometricTWAPState saturationAndGeometricTWAPState,\n        Validation.InputParams memory inputParams,\n        address borrower\n    ) external view returns (uint256 seizeLAssets, uint256 seizeXAssets, uint256 seizeYAssets) {\n        uint256 premiumInBips = calcSaturationMaxPremiumInBips(saturationAndGeometricTWAPState, inputParams, borrower);\n\n        if (premiumInBips == 0) {\n            revert LiquidationZeroPremium();\n        }\n\n        (seizeLAssets, seizeXAssets, seizeYAssets) = calcSaturationSeizedAssets(\n            inputParams.userAssets[DEPOSIT_L],\n            inputParams.userAssets[DEPOSIT_X],\n            inputParams.userAssets[DEPOSIT_Y],\n            premiumInBips\n        );\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculate weighted leverage liquidation repayments and seized deposits.\n     * @dev The first three return indices are seized deposits, the last three are required\n     *   repayments, and all values are in each token's native units. The formula for the premium\n     *   is calculated with the average net borrow of X and Y $$B$$ and the net deposit of X and Y\n     *   $$D$$ and a scaler $$S$$ that sets the pace at which the premium increased, in code we\n     *   call this `LEVERAGE_LIQUIDATION_BREAK_EVEN_FACTOR_MAG2`, and allowed leverage $$AL$$,\n     *   `ALLOWED_LIQUIDITY_LEVERAGE`:\n     *   ```math\n     *   premium = \\begin{cases}\n     *     S \\left(\n     *       \\frac{B}{D}\n     *       - \\frac{AL - 1}{AL}\n     *     \\right)\n     *     \\text { if } \\frac{B}{D} >\n     *       \\frac{AL - 1}{AL} \\\\\n     *     0 \\text { otherwise }\n     *   \\end{cases}\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   This can be visualized [here](https://www.desmos.com/calculator/1cd55f1yhz).\n     *\n     *   The premium is a percentage of the total deposit. If the premium is low enough, then we\n     *   we attempt to deleverage the position such that the premium and closed part of the\n     *   position leaves it under the leveraged threshold. If this is not possible, then all of the\n     *   users deposit will be transferred to the liquidator and there will be bad debt.\n     *\n     *   Note that the de leveraging relies on the min and max tick to be equal, so the result may\n     *   not be a valid amount of leverage using a min and max price as is done in the Validation\n     *   library.\n     *\n     * @param inputParams The params representing the position of the borrower.\n     * @return leveragedLiquidationParams Array indexed by DEPOSIT_L, DEPOSIT_X, DEPOSIT_Y,\n     *   BORROW_L, BORROW_X, BORROW_Y.\n     * @return badDebt Whether the leverage liquidation leaves bad debt to burn.\n     */\n    function liquidateLeverageCalcDeltaAndPremium(\n        Validation.InputParams memory inputParams\n    ) external pure returns (uint256[6] memory leveragedLiquidationParams, bool badDebt) {\n        uint256 netDepositInLAssets;\n        uint256 netBorrowInLAssets;\n\n        // we use the averageSqrtPrice for conversion between L and X/Y assets\n        uint256 averageSqrtPrice = Math.sqrt(inputParams.sqrtPriceMinInQ72 * inputParams.sqrtPriceMaxInQ72);\n\n        // overestimates borrow and underestimates deposit as we do in validation\n        Validation.CheckLtvParams memory checkLtvParams =\n            Validation.getCheckLtvParams(inputParams.userAssets, averageSqrtPrice, averageSqrtPrice);\n\n        {\n            // We average the two since both are in L assets.\n            netDepositInLAssets = (checkLtvParams.netDepositedXinLAssets + checkLtvParams.netDepositedYinLAssets) / 2;\n            netBorrowInLAssets = (checkLtvParams.netBorrowedXinLAssets + checkLtvParams.netBorrowedYinLAssets) / 2;\n        }\n\n        if (\n            // guarantee that premium > 0;\n            ALLOWED_LIQUIDITY_LEVERAGE * netBorrowInLAssets\n                > ALLOWED_LIQUIDITY_LEVERAGE_MINUS_ONE * netDepositInLAssets + LEVERAGE_LIQUIDATION_FACTOR\n        ) {\n            uint256 premiumValueInLAssets = (\n                ALLOWED_LIQUIDITY_LEVERAGE * netBorrowInLAssets\n                    - ALLOWED_LIQUIDITY_LEVERAGE_MINUS_ONE * netDepositInLAssets\n            ) / LEVERAGE_LIQUIDATION_FACTOR;\n\n            uint256 totalRepayInLAssets;\n            uint256 remainingDepositAfterPremium;\n            if (netDepositInLAssets < premiumValueInLAssets) {\n                // extreme, unlikely, case when the premium is greater than all deposits\n                premiumValueInLAssets = netDepositInLAssets;\n            } else {\n                // start close assets as the remaining deposits after premium.\n                remainingDepositAfterPremium = netDepositInLAssets - premiumValueInLAssets;\n            }\n\n            if (netBorrowInLAssets < remainingDepositAfterPremium) {\n                // the position can be de leveraged to meet leverage requirements.\n                totalRepayInLAssets = remainingDepositAfterPremium\n                    - ALLOWED_LIQUIDITY_LEVERAGE * (remainingDepositAfterPremium - netBorrowInLAssets);\n            } else {\n                totalRepayInLAssets = remainingDepositAfterPremium;\n                badDebt = true;\n            }\n\n            uint256 totalSeizedInLAssets = totalRepayInLAssets + premiumValueInLAssets;\n\n            leveragedLiquidationParams[DEPOSIT_L] = calculateLeverageLiquidationAsset(\n                inputParams.userAssets[DEPOSIT_L], totalSeizedInLAssets, netDepositInLAssets, !ROUNDING_UP\n            );\n            leveragedLiquidationParams[DEPOSIT_X] = calculateLeverageLiquidationAsset(\n                inputParams.userAssets[DEPOSIT_X], totalSeizedInLAssets, netDepositInLAssets, !ROUNDING_UP\n            );\n            leveragedLiquidationParams[DEPOSIT_Y] = calculateLeverageLiquidationAsset(\n                inputParams.userAssets[DEPOSIT_Y], totalSeizedInLAssets, netDepositInLAssets, !ROUNDING_UP\n            );\n            leveragedLiquidationParams[BORROW_L] = calculateLeverageLiquidationAsset(\n                inputParams.userAssets[BORROW_L], totalRepayInLAssets, netBorrowInLAssets, ROUNDING_UP\n            );\n            leveragedLiquidationParams[BORROW_X] = calculateLeverageLiquidationAsset(\n                inputParams.userAssets[BORROW_X], totalRepayInLAssets, netBorrowInLAssets, ROUNDING_UP\n            );\n            leveragedLiquidationParams[BORROW_Y] = calculateLeverageLiquidationAsset(\n                inputParams.userAssets[BORROW_Y], totalRepayInLAssets, netBorrowInLAssets, ROUNDING_UP\n            );\n        } else {\n            revert LiquidationZeroPremium();\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculate the amount of an asset to be liquidated in a leverage liquidation.\n     * @dev we use the min so amounts don't exceed balances\n     * @param userAsset The amount of the asset held by the user.\n     * @param totalShareLAsset The portion getting seized or repaid in L assets, or fraction of the total borrowed l or deposited l respectively.\n     * @param totalLAsset The total borrowed l or deposited l respectively.\n     * @param rounding Whether to round up the result, we round up for the borrow legs to make sure enough is repaid to cover the seized deposits.\n     */\n    function calculateLeverageLiquidationAsset(\n        uint256 userAsset,\n        uint256 totalShareLAsset,\n        uint256 totalLAsset,\n        bool rounding\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        return Math.min(\n            userAsset,\n            Math.mulDiv(userAsset, totalShareLAsset, totalLAsset, rounding ? Math.Rounding.Ceil : Math.Rounding.Floor)\n        );\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculate the maximum premium the liquidator may receive given the LTV of the borrower.\n     * @dev We min the result to favor the borrower.\n     * @return maxPremiumInBips The max premium allowed to be received by the liquidator.\n     */\n    function calcHardMaxPremiumInBips(\n        uint256[6] memory validatedLiquidation,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMinInQ72,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceMaxInQ72\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 maxPremiumInBips) {\n        // Calculate max premium before repay, swap min and max\n        Validation.CheckLtvParams memory checkLtvParams =\n            Validation.getCheckLtvParams(validatedLiquidation, sqrtPriceMaxInQ72, sqrtPriceMinInQ72);\n\n        (uint256 netDebtInLAssets, uint256 netCollateralInLAssets,) = Validation.calcDebtAndCollateral(checkLtvParams);\n\n        netDebtInLAssets = Validation.increaseForSlippage(netDebtInLAssets, activeLiquidityAssets);\n\n        maxPremiumInBips = convertLtvToPremium(\n            0 < netCollateralInLAssets ? Convert.mulDiv(netDebtInLAssets, BIPS, netCollateralInLAssets, false) : 0\n        );\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculate the premium being afforded to the liquidator given the repay and depositToTransfer amounts.\n     * @dev We use prices to maximize the `premiumInBips` to favor the borrower\n     * @param repaidDebtInL The amount of debt being repaid in L assets.\n     * @param seizedCollateralValueInL The value of the collateral being seized in L assets.\n     * @return premiumInBips The premium being received by the liquidator.\n     */\n    function calcHardPremiumInBips(\n        uint256 repaidDebtInL,\n        uint256 seizedCollateralValueInL\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 premiumInBips) {\n        // If nothing is being or can be liquidated, premium is infinite, we will revert\n        if (repaidDebtInL == 0) {\n            return type(uint256).max;\n        }\n\n        // Calculate premium in bips\n        unchecked {\n            premiumInBips = Convert.mulDiv(seizedCollateralValueInL, BIPS, repaidDebtInL, ROUNDING_UP);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the maximum premium the liquidator should receive based on borrower LTV.\n     *   `ltvBips` and the returned premium are both denominated in bips. Let `l` be\n     *   `ltvBips`, `a` and `b` be the two LTV thresholds, `m` be a slope, and `c` be an\n     *   intercept. The negative-premium segment uses `m_-` and `c_-`; the positive-premium\n     *   segment uses `m_+` and `c_+`.\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   P(l)=\n     *   \\begin{cases}\n     *     0\n     *       & l \\le a \\\\\n     *     \\left\\lfloor\\frac{m_{-}\\cdot l}{BIPS}\\right\\rfloor-c_{-}\n     *       & a < l < b \\\\\n     *     \\left\\lfloor\\frac{m_{+}\\cdot l}{BIPS}\\right\\rfloor+c_{+}\n     *       & b \\le l\n     *   \\end{cases}\n     *   ```\n     * @dev internal for testing only\n     * @param ltvBips LTV of the borrower.\n     * @return maxPremiumInBips The maximum premium for the liquidator.\n     */\n    function convertLtvToPremium(\n        uint256 ltvBips\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 maxPremiumInBips) {\n        if (ltvBips > START_NEGATIVE_PREMIUM_LTV_BIPS) {\n            if (ltvBips < START_PREMIUM_LTV_BIPS) {\n                // negative premium <=> maxPremiumInBips < 1\n                // linear function going thru (START_NEGATIVE_PREMIUM_LTV_BIPS, 0) and (START_PREMIUM_LTV_BIPS, 1)\n                maxPremiumInBips = Convert.mulDiv(NEGATIVE_PREMIUM_SLOPE_IN_BIPS, ltvBips, BIPS, false)\n                    - NEGATIVE_PREMIUM_INTERCEPT_IN_BIPS;\n            } else {\n                // positive premium <=> 1 <= maxPremiumInBips\n                // linear function going thru (START_PREMIUM_LTV_BIPS, 1) and (0.9, 1/0.9)\n                maxPremiumInBips = Convert.mulDiv(POSITIVE_PREMIUM_SLOPE_IN_BIPS, ltvBips, BIPS, false)\n                    + POSITIVE_PREMIUM_INTERCEPT_IN_BIPS;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    // saturation liquidation\n\n    function calcSaturationSeizedAssets(\n        uint256 depositedLAssets,\n        uint256 depositedXAssets,\n        uint256 depositedYAssets,\n        uint256 premiumInBips\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 seizedLAssets, uint256 seizedXAssets, uint256 seizedYAssets) {\n        seizedLAssets = depositedLAssets * premiumInBips / BIPS;\n        seizedXAssets = depositedXAssets * premiumInBips / BIPS;\n        seizedYAssets = depositedYAssets * premiumInBips / BIPS;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculate the max premium the saturation liquidator can receive given position of\n     *         `account`.\n     * @param saturationAndGeometricTWAPState The contract containing the saturation state.\n     * @param inputParams The params containing the position of `account`.\n     * @param account The account of the borrower.\n     * @return maxPremiumBips The max premium for the liquidator.\n     */\n    function calcSaturationMaxPremiumInBips(\n        ISaturationAndGeometricTWAPState saturationAndGeometricTWAPState,\n        Validation.InputParams memory inputParams,\n        address account\n    ) internal view returns (uint256 maxPremiumBips) {\n        // calculate ratio of new sat vs old sat\n        (uint256 netXLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72, uint256 netYLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72) =\n            Saturation.calcLiqSqrtPriceQ72(inputParams.userAssets);\n\n        // use max of netX vs netY\n        uint256 ratioBips;\n        if (0 < netXLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72 || 0 < netYLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72) {\n            ratioBips = saturationAndGeometricTWAPState.calcSatChangeRatioBips(\n                inputParams, netXLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72, netYLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72, address(this), account\n            );\n        }\n\n        // if the saturation has decreased, no saturation liquidation (maxPremium == 0)\n        if (ratioBips < BIPS) return 0;\n\n        // calculate premium\n        unchecked {\n            maxPremiumBips = (ratioBips - BIPS) / MAG1;\n        }\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    "lib/core-v1/contracts/libraries/Saturation.sol": {
      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only\npragma solidity ^0.8.34;\n\nimport {Math} from '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol';\nimport {SafeCast} from '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/SafeCast.sol';\nimport {BitLib} from '@mangrovedao/mangrove-core/lib/core/BitLib.sol';\nimport {MathLib, WAD} from '@morpho-org/morpho-blue/src/libraries/MathLib.sol';\n\nimport {Convert} from 'contracts/libraries/Convert.sol';\nimport {Uint16Set} from 'contracts/libraries/Uint16Set.sol';\nimport {TickMath} from 'contracts/libraries/TickMath.sol';\nimport {\n    B_IN_Q72,\n    BIPS,\n    EXPECTED_SATURATION_LTV_MAG2,\n    MAG1,\n    MAG2,\n    MAG4,\n    MAG6,\n    MAX_SATURATION_RATIO_IN_MAG2,\n    MINIMUM_LIQUIDITY,\n    Q16,\n    Q32,\n    Q56,\n    Q72,\n    Q88,\n    Q112,\n    Q128,\n    Q144,\n    Q200,\n    TRANCHE_B_IN_Q72,\n    TRANCHE_B_MINUS_ONE_IN_Q72,\n    SAT_PERCENTAGE_DELTA_DEFAULT_WAD,\n    SAT_PERCENTAGE_DELTA_4_WAD,\n    SAT_PERCENTAGE_DELTA_5_WAD,\n    SAT_PERCENTAGE_DELTA_6_WAD,\n    SAT_PERCENTAGE_DELTA_7_WAD,\n    ZERO_ADDRESS,\n    MAX_SATURATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD,\n    MAX_UTILIZATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD,\n    LIQUIDITY_INTEREST_RATE_MAGNIFICATION,\n    SECONDS_IN_YEAR\n} from 'contracts/libraries/constants.sol';\nimport {Liquidation} from 'contracts/libraries/Liquidation.sol';\nimport {Validation} from 'contracts/libraries/Validation.sol';\nimport {\n    DEPOSIT_L,\n    DEPOSIT_X,\n    DEPOSIT_Y,\n    BORROW_L,\n    BORROW_X,\n    BORROW_Y,\n    ROUNDING_UP\n} from 'contracts/interfaces/tokens/ITokenController.sol';\nimport {Interest} from 'contracts/libraries/Interest.sol';\n\n/**\n * @title   A lib to maintain the saturation of all the positions\n * @author  imi@1m1.io\n * @author  Will duelingGalois@protonmail.com\n * @notice  Saturation, or sat, is defined as the net borrow. In theory, we would want to divide net\n *  borrow by the total liquidity; in practice, we keep the net borrow only in the tree. The unit\n *  of sat is relative to active liquidity assets, or the amount of L deposited less the amount\n *  borrowed.\n *\n *  When we determine how much a swap moves the price, or square root price, we can define our\n *  equation using ticks, or tranches (25 ticks), where for some base $b$, the square root price\n *  is $b^t$ for some tick $t$. Alternatively for a larger base $B = b^{25}$ we can define the\n *  square root price as $B^T$ for some tranche $T$. Using the square root price, we can define the\n *  amount of x or y in each tranche as:\n *  ```math\n *  \\begin{align*}\n *    x =  L \\cdot B^{T_0} - L \\cdot B^{T_1} \\\\\n *    y = \\frac{L}{ B^{T_1}} - \\frac{L}{B^{T_0}}\n *  \\end{align*}\n *  ```\n *  where liquidity is $L = \\sqrt{reserveX \\cdot reserveY}$. If we want to know how much debt of x\n *  or y can be liquidated within one tick, we can solve these equations for L and then the amount\n *  of x and y are considered the debt we would like to see if it could be liquidated in one tick.\n *  If saturation with respect to our starting $L$ is smaller, that amount of debt can be\n *  liquidated in one swap in the given ticks. Otherwise it is too big and can not. Note that we\n *  assume $$t_1 \\text{ and } t_0 \\in \\mathbb{Z}$$ and $$t_0 + 1 = t_1$$. Then our definition of\n *  saturation relative to L is as follows,\n *\n *  ```math\n *    \\begin{equation}\n *      saturationRelativeToL =\n *        \\begin{cases}\n *          \\frac{ debtX }{ b^{ t_1 } } \\left( \\frac{ b }{ b - 1 } \\right) \\\\\n *          debtY \\cdot b^{ t_0 } \\cdot \\left( \\frac{ b }{ b - 1 } \\right)\n *        \\end{cases}\n *    \\end{equation}\n *   ```\n *  Saturation is kept in a tree, starting with a root, levels and leafs. We keep 2 trees, one for\n *  net X borrows, another for net Y borrows. The price is always the price of Y in units of X.\n *  Mostly, the code works with the sqrt of price. A net X borrow refers to a position that if\n *  liquidated would cause the price to become smaller; the opposite for net Y positions. Ticks are\n *  along the price dimension and int16. Tranches are 25 ticks, stored as int16.\n *\n *  Leafs (uint16) split the sat, which is uint112, into intervals. From left to right, the leafs\n *  of the tree cover the sat space in increasing order. Each account with a position has a price\n *  at which its LTV would reach LTVMAX, which is its liquidation (=liq) price.\n *\n *  To place a debt into the appropriate tranche, we think of each debt and its respective\n *  collateral as a series of sums, where each item in the series fits in one tranche. Using\n *  formulas above, we determine the number of ticks a debt would cross if liquidated. This is\n *  considered the span of the liquidation. Using this value we then determine the start and end\n *  points of the liquidation, where the start would be closer to the prices, on the right of the\n *  end for net debt of x and on the left of the end for net debt of Y.\n *\n *  Once we have the liquidation start, end, and span, we begin to place the debt, one tranche at a\n *  time moving towards the price. In this process we compare the prior recorded saturation and\n *  allow the insertion up to some max, set at 90% or the configuration set by the user.\n *\n *  A Tranche contains multiple accounts and thus a total sat. The tranche's sat assigns it to a\n *  leaf. Each leaf can contain multiple tranches and thus has a total actual sat whilst\n *  representing a specific sat per tranche range. Leafs and thus tranches and thus accounts above\n *  a certain sat threshold are considered over saturated. These accounts are penalized for being\n *  in an over saturated tranche. Each account, tranche and leaf has a total penalty that needs to\n *  be repaid to close the position fully. Sat is distributed over multiple tranches, in case a\n *  single tranche does not have enough available sat left. Sat is kept cumulatively in the tree,\n *  meaning a node contains the sum of the sat of its parents. Updating a sat at the  bottom of the\n *  tree requires updating all parents. Penalty is kept as a path sum, in uints of LAssets, meaning\n *  the penalty of an account is the sum of the penalties of all its parents. Updating the penalty\n *  for a range of leafs only requires updating the appropriate parent. Position (=pos) refers to\n *  the relative index of a child within its parent. Index refers to the index of a node in within\n *  its level\n *\n *  The formula for allocating saturation is derived from,\n *  ```math\n *  \\begin{align*}\n *    X = L \\cdot \\left( b^{t_e} - b^{t_s} \\right) \\\\\n *    Y = L \\cdot \\left( \\frac{1}{b^{t_s}} - \\frac{1}{b^{t_e}} \\right)\n *  \\end{align*}\n *  ```\n *\n *  for the start and end of liquidation $$t_s$$ and $$t_e$$ respectively. When we consider our\n *  buckets of `TICKS_PER_TRANCHE` we can rewrite this as a series where each boundary of each\n *  tranche $$T_i$$ where $$T_0 = t_e \\bmod \\mathrm{TICKS\\_PER\\_TRANCHE}$$ for a net debt of X and\n *  $$T_0 = (-t_e) \\bmod \\mathrm{TICKS\\_PER\\_TRANCHE}$$ for a net debt of Y and\n *  $$T_i = T_{i-1} + \\mathrm{TICKS\\_PER\\_TRANCHE}$$ for each subsequent tranche and\n *  $$B= b^{\\mathrm{TICKS\\_PER\\_TRANCHE}}$$. Thus we can rewrite the equations as:\n *\n *  ```math\n *  \\begin{align*}\n *    X &=\n *      L \\left(b^{T_1} - b^{t_e} \\right)\n *      + L \\left( b^{T_2} - b^{T_1}\\right)\n *      + ...\n *      + L \\left( b^{T_n} - b^{T_{n-1}}\\right)\n *      + L \\left(b^{t_s}-b^{T_n}  \\right)\n *    \\\\\n *\n *    \\Large\\frac{X}{b^{t_e}(B-1)} &=\n *      \\Large L \\left(\n *        \\frac{B \\cdot b^{t_e-T_0} - 1}{B-1}\n *        + \\frac{ \\sum_{i=1}^{n-1} B^{i} }{ b^{t_e-T_0} }\n *        + \\frac{B^{n} \\left(\n *  \\frac{b^{t_s}}{B^{n} \\cdot  b^{T_0}}-1 \\right) }{ b^{t_e-T_0}(B-1)}\n *        \\right)\n *  \\end{align*}\n *  ```\n *\n *  We then define the left side of this equation as total saturation $T_{sat}$ or newSaturation as\n *  we call it in the parameter passed in. Saturation is relative to the saturation in one tranche.\n *  The right side of the equation defines the saturation in each tranche $s_i$, starting at the\n *  furthest point from the tranche and moving forward.\n *\n *  ```math\n *  \\begin{align*}\n *    T_{sat} &=\n *      s_0\n *      + \\frac{\\sum_{i=1}^{n-1} s_i \\cdot B^{i}}{b^{t_e-T_0}}\n *      + \\frac{B^n \\cdot  s_n}{b^{t_e-T_0}}\n *    \\\\\n *\n *    \\frac{(T_{sat} - s_0)b^{t_e-T_0}}{B} - s_1 &=\n *      \\left(\\sum_{i=2}^{n-1} s_i \\cdot B^{i-1} \\right)\n *      + B^{n-1} \\cdot s_n\n *    \\\\\n *\n *   \\frac{\\frac{(T_{sat} - s_0)b^{t_e-T_0}}{B} - s_1 }{ B } -s_2 &=\n *      \\left(\\sum_{i=2}^{n-1} s_i \\cdot B^{i-2} \\right)\n *      + B^{n-2} \\cdot s_n\n *  \\end{align*}\n *  ```\n *\n *  When calculating the case for Y, the result is almost identical, except our definition for\n *  $T_{sat}$ requires us to multiply by $$b^{t_e}$$ rather than divide.\n *\n *  The above shows the logic applied in this function. We can allocate saturation across each\n *  tranche until the total remaining saturation is depleted. We allow less than the ideal\n *  saturation to be consumed if there is less available. Extra saturation is then carried forward\n *  to tranches closer to the price, requiring part of the position to be liquidated sooner as\n *  needed based on the available liquidity.\n *\n *  Two critical nuances of this algorithm is that we reduce by a factor of $$B$$ after each\n *  iteration and we multiply one time by $$b^{t_e - T_0}$$ after we allocate $$s_0$$ one time. The\n *  reduction of $$B$$ each iteration reflects the increase in the size of each tranche relative to\n *  a unit of X or Y as you move from one tranche to the next towards the price. The one time\n *  multiplication of $$b^{t_e - T_0}$$ is an adjustment for the offset of the start of liquidation\n *  relative to the start of the second tranche to minimize the impact of the reduction by $$B$$\n *  since the first portion of saturation does not use an entire tranche.\n *\n *  If saturation reaches the minOrMaxTick, we revert as the position is already reaching the limit\n *  of our probable price range and may require immediate liquidation if opened.\n */\nlibrary Saturation {\n    // constants\n\n    /**\n     * @notice time budget added to sat before adding it to the tree; compensates for the fact that\n     * the liq price moves closer to the current price over time.\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant SATURATION_TIME_BUFFER_IN_MAG2 = 101;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice percentage of max sat per tranche where penalization begins\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant START_SATURATION_PENALTY_RATIO_IN_MAG2 = 85;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice maximum initial saturation percentage when adding a new position\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant MAX_INITIAL_SATURATION_MAG2 = 90;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice $$\\mathrm{EXPECTED\\_SATURATION\\_LTV\\_MAG2} \\cdot \\mathrm{SATURATION\\_TIME\\_BUFFER\\_IN\\_MAG2}^{2}$$,\n     * a constant used in calculations.\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant EXPECTED_SATURATION_LTV_MAG2_TIMES_SAT_BUFFER_SQUARED = 867_085;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice $$\\mathrm{EXPECTED\\_SATURATION\\_LTV\\_MAG2} + 100$$, a constant used in calculations.\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant EXPECTED_SATURATION_LTV_PLUS_ONE_MAG2 = 185;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Slope for calculating premium when resetting saturation for straddle positions\n     *         where $$L^2 < X \\cdot Y$$ transitions to $$L^2 > X \\cdot Y$$. Applied to\n     *         $$(L^{2} - X \\cdot Y) / (X \\cdot Y)$$ to produce `premiumBips`. Matches the\n     *         Desmos coefficient $$\\frac{BIPS}{10} \\cdot 100 = 100000$$. At\n     *         $$L^{2} = 1.02 \\cdot X \\cdot Y$$ the raw premium evaluates to\n     *         `MAX_SAT_RESET_FOR_STRADDLE_PREMIUM_BIPS`; past that point the cap engages.\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant SAT_RESET_FOR_STRADDLE_SLOPE_BIPS = 100_000;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Maximum premium when resetting saturation for zero-to-positive straddle positions.\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant MAX_SAT_RESET_FOR_STRADDLE_PREMIUM_BIPS = 2000;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice a constant used to change the log base from the tick math base to the saturation to\n     * leaf base.\n     */\n    uint256 private constant SAT_CHANGE_OF_BASE_Q128 = 0xa39713406ef781154a9e682c2331a7c03;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice a constant used to shift when changing the base from tick math base to the\n     * saturation leaf base.\n     */\n    uint256 private constant SAT_CHANGE_OF_BASE_TIMES_SHIFT = 0xb3f2fb93ad437464387b0c308d1d05537;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice tick offset added to ensure leaf calculation starts from 0 at the lowest leaf\n     */\n    int16 private constant TICK_OFFSET = 1112;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice the lowest possible saturation is always in penalty\n     * $$MAX\\_ASSETS \\cdot \\mathrm{START\\_SATURATION\\_PENALTY\\_RATIO\\_IN\\_MAG2} / \\mathrm{TICKS\\_PER\\_TRANCHE}$$\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant LOWEST_POSSIBLE_IN_PENALTY = 0xd9999999999999999999999999999999;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice the minimum liquidity to reach the possibility of being in penalty.\n     * $$MINIMUM\\_LIQUIDITY \\cdot \\mathrm{START\\_SATURATION\\_PENALTY\\_RATIO\\_IN\\_MAG2} / \\mathrm{TICKS\\_PER\\_TRANCHE}$$\n     */\n    uint256 private constant MIN_LIQ_TO_REACH_PENALTY = 850;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Constant number one as an int type. Used for rounding or iterating direction.\n     */\n    int256 private constant INT_ONE = 1;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Constant number negative one. Used for rounding or iterating direction.\n     */\n    int256 private constant INT_NEGATIVE_ONE = -1;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Constant number zero as an int type. Used for rounding or iterating direction.\n     */\n    int256 private constant INT_ZERO = 0;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Tree leafs are on level LEVELS_WITHOUT_LEAFS; root is level 0\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant LEVELS_WITHOUT_LEAFS = 3;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice for convenience, since used a lot, ==LEVELS_WITHOUT_LEAFS - 1\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant LOWEST_LEVEL_INDEX = 2;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice $$2^{\\mathrm{LEAFS\\_IN\\_BITS}}$$\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant LEAFS = 4096;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice $$2^4$$\n     */\n    uint256 internal constant CHILDREN_PER_NODE = 16;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice $$2^{2 \\cdot 4}$$\n     */\n    uint256 private constant CHILDREN_AT_THIRD_LEVEL = 256;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice $$b = \\frac{2^9}{2^9-1}$$ is the base for ticks, then the tranche base is\n     * $$B = b^{\\mathrm{TICKS\\_PER\\_TRANCHE}}$$, int only to not need casting below, equals TICKS_PER_TRANCHE\n     */\n    int256 internal constant TICKS_PER_TRANCHE = 25;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice for convenience, used to determine max sat per tranche to not cross in liq swap:\n     * $$\\frac{B}{B-1}$$\n     */\n    uint256 constant TRANCHE_BASE_OVER_BASE_MINUS_ONE_Q72 = 0x5a19b9039a07efd7b39;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice `TickMath.MIN_TICK / TICKS_PER_TRANCHE - 1;` // -1 to floor\n     */\n    int256 internal constant MIN_TRANCHE = -795;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice `TickMath.MAX_TICK / TICKS_PER_TRANCHE;`\n     */\n    int256 internal constant MAX_TRANCHE = 794;\n\n    //\n    /**\n     * @notice constants for bit reading and writing in nodes.\n     * `type(uint256).max >> (TOTAL_BITS - FIELD_BITS);`\n     */\n    uint256 private constant FIELD_NODE_MASK = 0xffff;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Buffer space (in tranches) allowed above the highest used tranche before hitting\n     * maxLeaf limit\n     */\n    uint8 internal constant SATURATION_MAX_BUFFER_TRANCHES = 3;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Twenty-five percent magnitude of two.\n     */\n    uint256 private constant QUARTER_OF_MAG2 = 25;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Twenty-five percent minus one magnitude of two.\n     */\n    uint256 private constant QUARTER_MINUS_ONE = 24;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice quarters per tranche.\n     */\n    uint256 private constant NUMBER_OF_QUARTERS = 4;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice $$2 \\cdot 2^{72}$$, used in saturation formula.\n     */\n    uint256 private constant TWO_Q72 = 0x2000000000000000000;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice $$4 \\cdot 2^{128}$$, needed in quadratic formula is saturation.\n     */\n    uint256 private constant FOUR_Q144 = 0x4000000000000000000000000000000000000;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice $$MAG4 \\cdot Q72$$ constant needed in formula.\n     */\n    uint256 private constant MAG4_TIMES_Q72 = 0x2710000000000000000000;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice $$b^2 \\cdot Q72 - 1$$ used to round up results of `TickMath.getTickAtPrice()`.\n     */\n    uint256 private constant B_SQUARED_Q72_MINUS_ONE = 0x10100c08050301c1008;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice A large number that will not overflow when multiplied by `B_SQUARED_Q72_MINUS_ONE`\n     * $$\\left\\lfloor \\frac{ 2^{ 256 } }{ B\\_SQUARED\\_Q72\\_MINUS\\_ONE } \\right\\rfloor$$\n     */\n    uint256 private constant Q183 = 0x8000000000000000000000000000000000000000000000;\n\n    /**\n     * @notice $$\\mathrm{TICKS\\_PER\\_TRANCHE} \\cdot MAG2$$ used for calculating available liquidity.\n     */\n    uint256 private constant TICKS_PER_TRANCHE_MAG2 = 2500;\n\n    // errors\n\n    /**\n     * @notice if the largest sat in the trees is too large\n     */\n    error MaxTrancheOverSaturated();\n\n    /**\n     * @notice raised if the start of liquidation would occur on the wrong side of the min or max\n     *  tick price from the GeometricTWAP.\n     */\n    error LiquidationPassesMinOrMaxTick();\n\n    /**\n     * @notice raised if the the available saturation is not sufficient to keep the start of\n     *  liquidation from reaching the wrong side of the min or max tick price from the\n     *  GeometricTWAP.\n     */\n    error SaturationReachesMinOrMaxTick();\n\n    // storage structs\n\n    /**\n     * @notice final structure containing all the storage data\n     */\n    struct SaturationStruct {\n        // the tree containing sat and penalties for netX sat\n        Tree netXTree;\n        // the tree containing sat and penalties for netY sat\n        Tree netYTree;\n        uint16 maxLeaf;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice the main storage type of tree struct within the `SaturationStruct`.\n     */\n    struct Tree {\n        // is this tree netX xor not\n        bool netX;\n        // highest leaf that contains a tranche/account in the tree, useful to quickly decide whether the entire tree is over saturated\n        uint16 highestSetLeaf;\n        // nodes per level, each node contains a bit field of size of the number of its children\n        // and a uint112 saturation\n        uint128 totalSatInLAssets;\n        uint256[][LEVELS_WITHOUT_LEAFS] nodes;\n        // last level of nodes is kept as leafs\n        Leaf[LEAFS] leafs;\n        // which leaf does a tranche belong to\n        mapping(int16 => uint16) trancheToLeaf;\n        // sat per tranche\n        mapping(int16 => SaturationPair) trancheToSaturation;\n        // data per account\n        mapping(address => Account) accountData;\n        // cumulative penalty adjustment per tranche, bridging leaf penalty gaps when tranche moves\n        mapping(int16 => int256) tranchePenaltyAdjustment;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice a leaf contains multiple tranches and contains the total sat and penalty for the leaf\n     */\n    struct Leaf {\n        // set of tranches in a leaf\n        Uint16Set.Set tranches;\n        // sum of sat of each tranche in this leaf\n        SaturationPair leafSatPair;\n        // penalty for the leaf\n        uint256 penaltyInBorrowLSharesPerSatInQ72;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice basic data per account associated with an address stored in the `Tree` struct in a\n     * map as the value associated with the owners address as the key.\n     */\n    struct Account {\n        //does account exist, needed as accountToTranche has default value 0 and tranche 0 is ok\n        bool exists;\n        // tranche that account belongs to\n        int16 lastTranche;\n        // penalty of account\n        uint112 penaltyInBorrowLShares;\n        // sat per tranche starting at `tranche` and running in the direction dictated by\n        // netX/netY; netX trees have us distributing sat over increasing tranches, netY over\n        // decreasing tranches, in both cases, towards the current price\n        SaturationPair[] satPairPerTranche;\n        // penalty per sat per tranche starting at `tranche` and running in the direction dictated\n        // by netX/netY; netX trees have us distributing sat over increasing tranches, netY over\n        // decreasing tranches, in both cases, towards the current price\n        uint256[] treePenaltyAtOnsetInBorrowLSharesPerSatInQ72PerTranche;\n    }\n\n    // memory structs\n\n    /**\n     * @notice used in memory to avoid stack overflow in `calcLiqSqrtPriceQ72()`.\n     */\n    struct CalcLiqSqrtPriceHandleAllABCNonZeroStruct {\n        int256 netLInMAG2;\n        int256 netXInMAG2;\n        int256 netYInMAG2;\n        uint256 netYAbsInMAG2;\n        uint256 borrowedXAssets;\n        uint256 borrowedYAssets;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice used in memory to avoid stack overflow in `addSatToTranche()`.\n     */\n    struct AddSatToTrancheStateUpdatesStruct {\n        int256 tranche;\n        uint256 newLeaf;\n        SaturationPair oldTrancheSaturation;\n        SaturationPair newTrancheSaturation;\n        SaturationPair satAvailableToAdd;\n        uint256 targetCapacityRelativeToLAssets;\n        address account;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice a pair of saturation values used and stored throughout this library.\n     */\n    struct SaturationPair {\n        // the value of a debt in units of L assets at a given liquidation price.\n        uint128 satInLAssets;\n        // the amount of active liquidity assets, L, that the swap required to liquidate the debt\n        // would consume.\n        uint128 satRelativeToL;\n    }\n\n    // init functions\n\n    /**\n     * @notice  initializes the satStruct, allocating storage for all nodes\n     * @dev     initCheck can be removed once the tree structure is fixed\n     * @param   satStruct contains the entire sat data\n     */\n    function initializeSaturationStruct(\n        SaturationStruct storage satStruct\n    ) internal {\n        // init nodes in storage\n        initTree(satStruct.netXTree);\n        // init nodes in storage\n        initTree(satStruct.netYTree);\n        // set 1 of the tree to netX, the other stays netY by default\n        satStruct.netXTree.netX = true;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice  init the nodes of the tree\n     * @param   tree that is being read from or written to\n     */\n    function initTree(\n        Tree storage tree\n    ) internal {\n        tree.nodes[0] = new uint256[](1);\n        tree.nodes[1] = new uint256[](CHILDREN_PER_NODE);\n        tree.nodes[2] = new uint256[](CHILDREN_AT_THIRD_LEVEL);\n    }\n\n    // update functions\n\n    /**\n     * @notice  update the borrow position of an account and potentially check (and revert) if the\n     * resulting sat is too high\n     * @dev     run accruePenalties before running this function\n     * @param   satStruct  main data struct\n     * @param   inputParams  contains the position and pair params, like account borrows/deposits,\n     * current price and active liquidity\n     * @param   account  for which is position is being updated\n     */\n    function update(\n        SaturationStruct storage satStruct,\n        Validation.InputParams memory inputParams,\n        address account,\n        uint256 userSaturationRatioMAG2,\n        bool skipMinOrMaxTickCheck\n    ) internal {\n        if (account != ZERO_ADDRESS) {\n            SaturationPair memory zeroSat = SaturationPair({satRelativeToL: 0, satInLAssets: 0});\n\n            // if the account has no borrow position, it could be getting repaid\n            if (!inputParams.hasBorrow) {\n                updateTreeGivenAccountTrancheAndSat(\n                    satStruct.netXTree,\n                    zeroSat,\n                    account,\n                    0,\n                    inputParams.activeLiquidityAssets,\n                    0,\n                    userSaturationRatioMAG2\n                );\n\n                updateTreeGivenAccountTrancheAndSat(\n                    satStruct.netYTree,\n                    zeroSat,\n                    account,\n                    0,\n                    inputParams.activeLiquidityAssets,\n                    0,\n                    userSaturationRatioMAG2\n                );\n            } else {\n                // calc the netX and netY prices where the position would reach LTVMAX\n                (uint256 netXLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72, uint256 netYLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72) =\n                    calcLiqSqrtPriceQ72(inputParams.userAssets);\n\n                // if a netX exists, update the netX tree\n                if (0 < netXLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72) {\n                    (SaturationPair memory saturation, int256 endOfLiquidationInTicks, int256 minTick) =\n                    calcLastTickAndSaturation(\n                        inputParams,\n                        netXLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72,\n                        netYLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72,\n                        userSaturationRatioMAG2,\n                        true,\n                        skipMinOrMaxTickCheck\n                    );\n\n                    updateTreeGivenAccountTrancheAndSat(\n                        satStruct.netXTree,\n                        saturation,\n                        account,\n                        endOfLiquidationInTicks,\n                        inputParams.activeLiquidityAssets,\n                        minTick,\n                        userSaturationRatioMAG2\n                    );\n                } else if (satStruct.netXTree.accountData[account].exists) {\n                    updateTreeGivenAccountTrancheAndSat(\n                        satStruct.netXTree,\n                        zeroSat,\n                        account,\n                        0,\n                        inputParams.activeLiquidityAssets,\n                        0,\n                        userSaturationRatioMAG2\n                    );\n                }\n\n                // if a netY exists, update the netY tree\n                if (0 < netYLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72) {\n                    (SaturationPair memory saturation, int256 endOfLiquidationInTicks, int256 maxTick) =\n                    calcLastTickAndSaturation(\n                        inputParams,\n                        netXLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72,\n                        netYLiqSqrtPriceInXInQ72,\n                        userSaturationRatioMAG2,\n                        false,\n                        skipMinOrMaxTickCheck\n                    );\n\n                    updateTreeGivenAccountTrancheAndSat(\n                        satStruct.netYTree,\n                        saturation,\n                        account,\n                        endOfLiquidationInTicks,\n                        inputParams.activeLiquidityAssets,\n                        maxTick,\n                        userSaturationRatioMAG2\n                    );\n                } else if (satStruct.netYTree.accountData[account].exists) {\n                    updateTreeGivenAccountTrancheAndSat(\n                        satStruct.netYTree,\n                        zeroSat,\n                        account,\n                        0,\n                        inputParams.activeLiquidityAssets,\n                        0,\n                        userSaturationRatioMAG2\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        uint256 maxLeaf = satToLeaf(inputParams.activeLiquidityAssets);\n        // check whether the max sat is too high\n        if (\n            maxLeaf\n                < Math.max(satStruct.netXTree.highestSetLeaf, satStruct.netYTree.highestSetLeaf)\n                    + SATURATION_MAX_BUFFER_TRANCHES\n        ) {\n            revert MaxTrancheOverSaturated();\n        }\n\n        satStruct.maxLeaf = uint16(maxLeaf);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice  internal update that removes the account from the tree (if it exists) from its prev\n     * position and adds it to its new position\n     * @param   tree that is being read from or written to\n     * @param   newSaturation  the new sat of the account, in units of LAssets (absolute) and\n     * relative to active liquidity\n     * @param   account  whos position is being considered\n     * @param   newEndOfLiquidationInTicks the new tranche of the account in mag2.\n     * @param   activeLiquidityInLAssets  of the pair\n     */\n    function updateTreeGivenAccountTrancheAndSat(\n        Tree storage tree,\n        SaturationPair memory newSaturation,\n        address account,\n        int256 newEndOfLiquidationInTicks,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityInLAssets,\n        int256 minOrMaxTick,\n        uint256 userSaturationRatioMAG2\n    ) internal {\n        // flag whether the highest sat needs updating\n        bool highestSetLeafRemoved;\n        bool highestSetLeafAdded;\n\n        // if account exists at all, remove from the tree\n        if (tree.accountData[account].exists) {\n            highestSetLeafRemoved = removeSatFromTranche(tree, account);\n        }\n\n        // if the account has any sat, add to the tree\n        if (0 < newSaturation.satRelativeToL) {\n            highestSetLeafAdded = addSatToTranche(\n                tree,\n                account,\n                newEndOfLiquidationInTicks,\n                newSaturation,\n                activeLiquidityInLAssets,\n                userSaturationRatioMAG2,\n                minOrMaxTick\n            );\n        }\n\n        // update highestSetLeaf\n        if (highestSetLeafRemoved && !highestSetLeafAdded) {\n            unchecked {\n                tree.highestSetLeaf =\n                    uint16(findHighestSetLeafUpwards(tree, LOWEST_LEVEL_INDEX, tree.highestSetLeaf / CHILDREN_PER_NODE));\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice  remove sat from tree, for each tranche in a loop that could hold sat for the account\n     * @param   tree that is being read from or written to\n     * @param   account whose position is being considered\n     * @return  highestSetLeafRemoved  flag indicating whether we removed sat from the highest leaf\n     * xor not\n     */\n    function removeSatFromTranche(Tree storage tree, address account) internal returns (bool highestSetLeafRemoved) {\n        // beginning tranche\n        int256 tranche = tree.accountData[account].lastTranche;\n        uint256 satArrayLength = tree.accountData[account].satPairPerTranche.length;\n        // loop through each tranche that could contain sat, we cannot short circuit as we might have added sat to the last tranche\n        for (uint256 trancheIndex = 0; trancheIndex < satArrayLength; trancheIndex++) {\n            // if we have reached the edges of price, we are definitely done\n            if (MAX_TRANCHE < tranche || tranche < MIN_TRANCHE) break;\n\n            SaturationPair memory oldAccountSaturationInTranche =\n                tree.accountData[account].satPairPerTranche[trancheIndex];\n            // if the account had no sat in this tranche, move to next tranche\n            if (0 < oldAccountSaturationInTranche.satRelativeToL) {\n                // remember old leaf before state update\n                uint256 oldLeaf = tree.trancheToLeaf[int16(tranche)];\n\n                // update sat, fields and penalties for leafs, parents\n                removeSatFromTrancheStateUpdates(tree, oldAccountSaturationInTranche, tranche, oldLeaf);\n\n                uint256 highestSetLeaf = tree.highestSetLeaf;\n                bool isLeafEmpty = Uint16Set.count(tree.leafs[highestSetLeaf].tranches) == 0;\n                if (oldLeaf == highestSetLeaf && isLeafEmpty) {\n                    highestSetLeafRemoved = true;\n                }\n            }\n\n            // move to next tranche\n            unchecked {\n                tranche += trancheDirection(tree.netX);\n            }\n        }\n        // we have removed the account from the tree and update the state of the account\n        delete tree.accountData[account];\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice  depending on old and new leaf of the tranche, update the sats, fields and penalties\n     * of the tree\n     * @param   tree that is being read from or written to\n     * @param   oldAccountSaturationInTranche account sat\n     * @param   tranche  under consideration\n     * @param   oldLeaf where tranche was located before this sat removal\n     */\n    function removeSatFromTrancheStateUpdates(\n        Tree storage tree,\n        SaturationPair memory oldAccountSaturationInTranche,\n        int256 tranche,\n        uint256 oldLeaf\n    ) internal {\n        // old sat of tranche (both absolute and relative)\n        SaturationPair memory oldTrancheSaturation = tree.trancheToSaturation[int16(tranche)];\n\n        // tranche sat decreases by removed account sat, account can not be greater than tranche of\n        // accounts\n        SaturationPair memory newTrancheSaturation;\n        unchecked {\n            newTrancheSaturation.satRelativeToL =\n                oldTrancheSaturation.satRelativeToL - oldAccountSaturationInTranche.satRelativeToL;\n            newTrancheSaturation.satInLAssets =\n                oldTrancheSaturation.satInLAssets - oldAccountSaturationInTranche.satInLAssets;\n        }\n\n        // Use relative saturation for satToLeaf calculation\n        uint256 newLeaf = satToLeaf(newTrancheSaturation.satRelativeToL);\n\n        // update both absolute and relative saturation\n        tree.trancheToSaturation[int16(tranche)] = newTrancheSaturation;\n\n        if (newTrancheSaturation.satRelativeToL == 0) {\n            // case remove tranche from tree. Reset the per-tranche penalty adjustment so a\n            // future account joining this tranche key starts a fresh lifecycle. Required to\n            // maintain the invariant `leafPen + adjustment >= 0` — see `getEffectivePenalty`.\n            removeTrancheToLeaf(tree, oldTrancheSaturation, tranche, oldLeaf);\n            delete tree.tranchePenaltyAdjustment[int16(tranche)];\n        } else if (newLeaf < oldLeaf) {\n            // case change to lower leaf, since we are removing sat\n            addSatToTrancheStateUpdatesHigherLeaf(\n                tree, tranche, oldTrancheSaturation, newTrancheSaturation, oldLeaf, newLeaf\n            );\n        } else {\n            // case change to same leaf, oldLeaf == newLeaf, less updating needed\n\n            // decrease leaf sat (both absolute and relative)\n            tree.leafs[newLeaf].leafSatPair.satInLAssets -= oldAccountSaturationInTranche.satInLAssets;\n            tree.leafs[newLeaf].leafSatPair.satRelativeToL -= oldAccountSaturationInTranche.satRelativeToL;\n            unchecked {\n                // update sat up the tree (use absolute saturation)\n                addSatUpTheTree(tree, oldAccountSaturationInTranche.satInLAssets, false);\n                // penalty offset stays the same\n            }\n        }\n        // case oldLeaf < newLeaf does not exist\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice  add sat to tree, for each tranche in a loop as needed. we add to each tranche as\n     *          much as it can bear.\n     *          Saturation Distribution Logic\n     *\n     *          This function distributes debt across multiple tranches, maintaining two types of\n     *          saturation:\n     *          1. satInLAssets: The absolute debt amount in L assets (should remain constant total)\n     *          2. satRelativeToL: The relative saturation that depends on the tranche's price level\n     *\n     *          As we move between tranches (different price levels), the same absolute debt\n     *          translates to different relative saturations due to the price-dependent formula.\n     *\n     *          conceptually satInLAssets should not be scaled as it represents actual debt that\n     *          doesn't change with price.\n     *\n     *          The formula applied here, derived in the introduction, is,\n     *          ```math\n     *          \\begin{align*}\n     *            T_{sat} &=\n     *              s_0\n     *              + \\frac{\\sum_{i=1}^{n-1} s_i \\cdot B^{i}}{b^{t_e-T_0}}\n     *              + \\frac{B^n \\cdot  s_n}{b^{t_e-T_0}}\n     *            \\\\\n     *\n     *            \\frac{(T_{sat} - s_0)b^{t_e-T_0}}{B} - s_1 &=\n     *              \\left(\\sum_{i=2}^{n-1} s_i \\cdot B^{i-1} \\right)\n     *              + B^{n-1} \\cdot s_n\n     *            \\\\\n     *\n     *           \\frac{\\frac{(T_{sat} - s_0)b^{t_e-T_0}}{B} - s_1 }{ B } -s_2 &=\n     *              \\left(\\sum_{i=2}^{n-1} s_i \\cdot B^{i-2} \\right)\n     *              + B^{n-2} \\cdot s_n\n     *          \\end{align*}\n     *          ```\n     * @param   tree that is being read from or written to\n     * @param   account whose position is being considered\n     * @param   newEndOfLiquidationInTicks the new tranche of the account location in MAG2\n     * @param   newSaturation the new sat of the account, in units of LAssets (absolute) and\n     *   relative to active liquidity\n     * @param   activeLiquidityInLAssets of the pair\n     * @return  highestSetLeafAdded flag indicating whether we removed sat from the highest leaf\n     *   xor not\n     */\n    function addSatToTranche(\n        Tree storage tree,\n        address account,\n        int256 newEndOfLiquidationInTicks,\n        SaturationPair memory newSaturation,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityInLAssets,\n        uint256 userSaturationRatioMAG2,\n        int256 minOrMaxTick\n    ) internal returns (bool highestSetLeafAdded) {\n        bool netDebtX = tree.netX;\n        // `nextTranche` starts as the last tranche and moves forward to the first.\n        (uint256 usableTicks, int256 nextTranche) = getUsableTicksAndLastTranche(newEndOfLiquidationInTicks, netDebtX);\n\n        usableTicks = restrictUsableTicksForMinOrMaxTick(usableTicks, nextTranche, minOrMaxTick, netDebtX);\n\n        tree.accountData[account].lastTranche = int16(nextTranche);\n\n        uint256 endOfLiquidationAdjustmentQ72 =\n            calculateEndOfLiquidationAdjustment(newEndOfLiquidationInTicks, netDebtX);\n\n        // keep adding sat to tranches as long as more needs adding\n        while (0 < newSaturation.satRelativeToL) {\n            if (\n                (netDebtX && nextTranche * TICKS_PER_TRANCHE >= minOrMaxTick)\n                    || (!netDebtX && (nextTranche + 1) * TICKS_PER_TRANCHE <= minOrMaxTick)\n            ) {\n                revert SaturationReachesMinOrMaxTick();\n            }\n\n            // if we have reached the edges of price, we are definitely done\n            if (MAX_TRANCHE < nextTranche || nextTranche < MIN_TRANCHE) break;\n\n            // convenience struct to avoid 'stack too deep'\n            AddSatToTrancheStateUpdatesStruct memory addSatToTrancheStateUpdatesParams =\n            getAddSatToTrancheStateUpdatesParams(\n                tree,\n                account,\n                nextTranche,\n                newSaturation,\n                activeLiquidityInLAssets,\n                userSaturationRatioMAG2,\n                usableTicks\n            );\n\n            // if we have nothing to add to this tranche (it is full), move to the next\n            if (0 < addSatToTrancheStateUpdatesParams.satAvailableToAdd.satRelativeToL) {\n                // update the sat per tranche\n                tree.accountData[account].satPairPerTranche.push(\n                    SaturationPair({\n                        satInLAssets: addSatToTrancheStateUpdatesParams.satAvailableToAdd.satInLAssets,\n                        satRelativeToL: addSatToTrancheStateUpdatesParams.satAvailableToAdd.satRelativeToL\n                    })\n                );\n                // update sat, fields and penalties for leafs, parents\n                tree.accountData[account].treePenaltyAtOnsetInBorrowLSharesPerSatInQ72PerTranche.push(\n                    addSatToTrancheStateUpdates(tree, addSatToTrancheStateUpdatesParams)\n                );\n                // if we have a new highest leaf, we set this to true so the caller knows the\n                // highestSetLeaf needs updating.\n                if (tree.highestSetLeaf < addSatToTrancheStateUpdatesParams.newLeaf) {\n                    tree.highestSetLeaf = uint16(addSatToTrancheStateUpdatesParams.newLeaf);\n                    highestSetLeafAdded = true;\n                }\n            } else {\n                // Keep one array slot per traversed tranche so index-to-tranche mapping\n                // (lastTranche + i * direction) remains aligned for removal/penalty loops.\n                tree.accountData[account].satPairPerTranche.push(SaturationPair({satInLAssets: 0, satRelativeToL: 0}));\n                tree.accountData[account].treePenaltyAtOnsetInBorrowLSharesPerSatInQ72PerTranche.push(0);\n            }\n\n            unchecked {\n                // we have less to add for the next tranches\n                newSaturation.satRelativeToL -= addSatToTrancheStateUpdatesParams.satAvailableToAdd.satRelativeToL;\n                newSaturation.satInLAssets -= addSatToTrancheStateUpdatesParams.satAvailableToAdd.satInLAssets;\n\n                if (newSaturation.satRelativeToL > 0) {\n                    // decrease only relative saturation by a factor of B (absolute debt doesn't\n                    // change with price) safe to cast since it makes the value smaller.\n                    newSaturation.satRelativeToL = uint128(\n                        Convert.mulDiv(\n                            newSaturation.satRelativeToL, endOfLiquidationAdjustmentQ72, TRANCHE_B_IN_Q72, false\n                        )\n                    );\n\n
