    "lib/core-v1/contracts/libraries/TickMath.sol": {
      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-2.0-or-later\npragma solidity ^0.8.34;\n\nimport {Convert} from 'contracts/libraries/Convert.sol';\nimport {Q128} from 'contracts/libraries/constants.sol';\n\n/**\n * @title Math library for computing sqrt prices from ticks and vice versa\n * @notice Computes sqrt price for ticks of size $B=(1-2^{-9})^{-1}$ as fixed point Q72 numbers.\n * Supports prices between $2^{-112}$ and $2^{112}-1$.\n */\nlibrary TickMath {\n    error PriceOutOfBounds();\n    error TickOutOfBounds();\n\n    // big(2)^64 / log2(B) - 1; / 2 since the input is price but ticks map to sqrt price; -1 since without is an odd number\n    int256 private constant BASE_CHANGE_CONSTANT_IN_Q128 = 0xb145b7be86780ae93f;\n    int256 private constant TICK_LOW_ERROR_CORRECTION_IN_Q128 = 0x1f6d22eefc342687357a94df44b0dbf;\n    int256 private constant TICK_HI_ERROR_CORRECTION_IN_Q128 = 0xb33c8bdbc23c5eaf1cd8140681512562;\n\n    uint256 internal constant MIN_PRICE_IN_Q128 = 0x10000;\n    uint256 internal constant MAX_PRICE_IN_Q128 = 0xffffffffffffffffffffffffffff00000000000000000000000000000000;\n\n    // getSqrtPriceAtTick(MIN_TICK) == MIN_SQRT_PRICE_IN_Q72 < getSqrtPriceAtTick(MIN_TICK + 1)\n    // getSqrtPriceAtTick(MAX_TICK - 1) < MAX_SQRT_PRICE_IN_Q72 == getSqrtPriceAtTick(MAX_TICK)\n    int16 internal constant MIN_TICK = -0x4d8f; // -19855\n    int16 internal constant MAX_TICK = 0x4d8e; // -MIN_TICK - 1; // 19854\n    uint256 internal constant MIN_SQRT_PRICE_IN_Q72 = 0xffc0;\n    uint256 internal constant MAX_SQRT_PRICE_IN_Q72 = 0xffc00ffc00ffc00ffc00ffc00ffc00ff;\n\n    // sqrtPrice = B^tick\n    function getSqrtPriceAtTick(\n        int16 tick\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 sqrtPriceInQ72) {\n        // Handle edge case where we round up tick by 1.\n        if (tick == MAX_TICK + 1) return MAX_SQRT_PRICE_IN_Q72;\n        if (tick < MIN_TICK || MAX_TICK + 1 < tick) revert TickOutOfBounds();\n\n        int256 intTick = int256(tick);\n        uint256 absTick = uint256(intTick < 0 ? -intTick : intTick);\n\n        sqrtPriceInQ72 = applyMultiplications(absTick) >> 56; // 72 == 128 - 56\n        if (0 < tick) sqrtPriceInQ72 = type(uint144).max / sqrtPriceInQ72; // 2 * 72 == 144\n    }\n\n    // tick = log in basis B of priceInQ128\n    function getTickAtPrice(\n        uint256 priceInQ128\n    ) internal pure returns (int16) {\n        if (priceInQ128 < MIN_PRICE_IN_Q128 || MAX_PRICE_IN_Q128 < priceInQ128) revert PriceOutOfBounds();\n\n        uint256 p = priceInQ128;\n        uint256 msb = 0;\n\n        assembly {\n            let f := shl(7, gt(p, 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFF))\n            msb := or(msb, f)\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            let f := shl(6, gt(p, 0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFF))\n            msb := or(msb, f)\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            let f := shl(5, gt(p, 0xFFFFFFFF))\n            msb := or(msb, f)\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            let f := shl(4, gt(p, 0xFFFF))\n            msb := or(msb, f)\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            let f := shl(3, gt(p, 0xFF))\n            msb := or(msb, f)\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            let f := shl(2, gt(p, 0xF))\n            msb := or(msb, f)\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            let f := shl(1, gt(p, 0x3))\n            msb := or(msb, f)\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            let f := gt(p, 0x1)\n            msb := or(msb, f)\n        }\n\n        int256 logBase2;\n        unchecked {\n            if (msb >= 128) p = priceInQ128 >> (msb - 127);\n            else p = priceInQ128 << (127 - msb);\n            logBase2 = (int256(msb) - 128) << 64;\n        }\n\n        assembly {\n            p := shr(127, mul(p, p))\n            let f := shr(128, p)\n            logBase2 := or(logBase2, shl(63, f))\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            p := shr(127, mul(p, p))\n            let f := shr(128, p)\n            logBase2 := or(logBase2, shl(62, f))\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            p := shr(127, mul(p, p))\n            let f := shr(128, p)\n            logBase2 := or(logBase2, shl(61, f))\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            p := shr(127, mul(p, p))\n            let f := shr(128, p)\n            logBase2 := or(logBase2, shl(60, f))\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            p := shr(127, mul(p, p))\n            let f := shr(128, p)\n            logBase2 := or(logBase2, shl(59, f))\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            p := shr(127, mul(p, p))\n            let f := shr(128, p)\n            logBase2 := or(logBase2, shl(58, f))\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            p := shr(127, mul(p, p))\n            let f := shr(128, p)\n            logBase2 := or(logBase2, shl(57, f))\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            p := shr(127, mul(p, p))\n            let f := shr(128, p)\n            logBase2 := or(logBase2, shl(56, f))\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n        assembly {\n            p := shr(127, mul(p, p))\n            let f := shr(128, p)\n            logBase2 := or(logBase2, shl(55, f))\n            p := shr(f, p)\n        }\n\n        unchecked {\n            int256 logB = BASE_CHANGE_CONSTANT_IN_Q128 * logBase2;\n            int16 tickLow = int16((logB - TICK_LOW_ERROR_CORRECTION_IN_Q128) >> 128);\n            int16 tickHi = int16((logB + TICK_HI_ERROR_CORRECTION_IN_Q128) >> 128);\n            if (tickLow == tickHi) return tickLow;\n            if (getPriceAtTick(tickHi) <= priceInQ128) return tickHi;\n            return tickLow;\n        }\n    }\n\n    function getPriceAtTick(\n        int16 tick\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 priceInQ128) {\n        if (tick == MAX_TICK + 1) return MAX_PRICE_IN_Q128;\n        if (tick < MIN_TICK || MAX_TICK + 1 < tick) revert TickOutOfBounds();\n\n        int256 intTick = tick;\n        uint256 absTick = uint256(intTick < 0 ? -intTick : intTick);\n\n        // apply the same algorithm as in getSqrtPriceAtTick using tick * 2 and adding a round.\n        absTick *= 2;\n        priceInQ128 = applyMultiplications(absTick);\n        if (absTick & 0x8000 != 0) priceInQ128 = (priceInQ128 * 0xbef94ed7e) >> 128;\n\n        if (0 < tick) priceInQ128 = type(uint256).max / priceInQ128;\n    }\n\n    function applyMultiplications(\n        uint256 absTick\n    ) private pure returns (uint256 valueInQ128) {\n        unchecked {\n            valueInQ128 = absTick & 0x1 != 0 ? 0xff800000000000000000000000000000 : 0x100000000000000000000000000000000;\n            if (absTick & 0x2 != 0) valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0xff004000000000000000000000000000) >> 128;\n            if (absTick & 0x4 != 0) valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0xfe017f80100000000000000000000000) >> 128;\n            if (absTick & 0x8 != 0) valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0xfc06f9045e406ff00100000000000000) >> 128;\n            if (absTick & 0x10 != 0) {\n                valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0xf81dba7137fcc6d22fafcfde71ae81e0) >> 128;\n            }\n            if (absTick & 0x20 != 0) {\n                valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0xf0799caf21e927ea1252fa7400a1d886) >> 128;\n            }\n            if (absTick & 0x40 != 0) {\n                valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0xe1e43f8ddd0922622788b108788fc191) >> 128;\n            }\n            if (absTick & 0x80 != 0) {\n                valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0xc7530338a302e81d8229a7f1f67fa265) >> 128;\n            }\n            if (absTick & 0x100 != 0) {\n                valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0x9b3229ed2432991a2e021bb106f5feb6) >> 128;\n            }\n            if (absTick & 0x200 != 0) {\n                valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0x5e15c89991553a6dc1c8a8a0931572d2) >> 128;\n            }\n            if (absTick & 0x400 != 0) {\n                valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0x2294012b4d1cbe1865fe254cef6e40bc) >> 128;\n            }\n            if (absTick & 0x800 != 0) {\n                valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0x4aba5e0da8e29a77fabca56a012ae25) >> 128;\n            }\n            if (absTick & 0x1000 != 0) {\n                valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0x15d0460cb40a7356d32b6966397c03) >> 128;\n            }\n            if (absTick & 0x2000 != 0) valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0x1dbd4effd593afec2694414e4f6) >> 128;\n            if (absTick & 0x4000 != 0) valueInQ128 = (valueInQ128 * 0x3746fe3b485b7be710a06) >> 128;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Get the new tick based on the current reserves.\n     * @param reserveXAssets The current reserve X assets.\n     * @param reserveYAssets The current reserve Y assets.\n     * @return newTick The current tick.\n     */\n    function getTickFromReserves(uint256 reserveXAssets, uint256 reserveYAssets) internal pure returns (int16) {\n        return getTickAtPrice(Convert.mulDiv(reserveXAssets, Q128, reserveYAssets, false));\n    }\n}\n"

    "lib/core-v1/contracts/libraries/Interest.sol": {
      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only\npragma solidity ^0.8.34;\n\nimport {Math} from '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol';\nimport {MathLib, WAD} from '@morpho-org/morpho-blue/src/libraries/MathLib.sol';\n\nimport {TickMath} from 'contracts/libraries/TickMath.sol';\n\nimport {\n    ITokenController,\n    DEPOSIT_L,\n    DEPOSIT_X,\n    DEPOSIT_Y,\n    BORROW_L,\n    BORROW_X,\n    BORROW_Y,\n    FIRST_DEBT_TOKEN,\n    TOKEN_COUNT,\n    ROUNDING_UP\n} from 'contracts/interfaces/tokens/ITokenController.sol';\nimport {\n    B_IN_Q72,\n    Q72,\n    Q128,\n    LIQUIDITY_INTEREST_RATE_MAGNIFICATION,\n    MAX_SATURATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD,\n    MAX_UTILIZATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD,\n    SECONDS_IN_YEAR\n} from 'contracts/libraries/constants.sol';\nimport {Convert} from 'contracts/libraries/Convert.sol';\n\n/**\n * @title Interest Library\n * @notice This library is used for calculating and accruing interest.\n * @dev many calculations are unchecked because we asset values are stored as uint112. We also limit\n *      the max amount amount of interest to ensure that it can not overflow when added to the\n *      current assets.\n *\n */\nlibrary Interest {\n    using MathLib for uint256;\n    using MathLib for uint112;\n\n    struct AccrueInterestParams {\n        uint256 duration;\n        int16 lendingStateTick;\n        uint112[6] shares;\n        uint256 satPercentageInWads;\n        uint256 reserveXAssets;\n        uint256 reserveYAssets;\n    }\n\n    uint256 internal constant OPTIMAL_UTILIZATION = 0.8e18; //  80%\n    uint256 internal constant DANGER_UTILIZATION = 0.925e18; // 92.5%\n    uint256 internal constant SLOPE1 = 0.1e18;\n    uint256 internal constant SLOPE2 = 2e18;\n    uint256 internal constant SLOPE3 = 20e18;\n    uint256 internal constant BASE_OPTIMAL_UTILIZATION = 0.08e18; // 8%\n    uint256 internal constant BASE_DANGER_UTILIZATION = 0.33e18; // 33%\n\n    uint256 internal constant LENDING_FEE_RATE = 10;\n    uint256 private constant MAX_UINT112 = type(uint112).max;\n    uint256 private constant LAST_DEPOSIT = 2; // FIRST_DEBT_TOKEN - 1;\n\n    uint256 private constant NO_RESERVES_FOR_L = 0;\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Maximum percentage for the penalty saturation allowed.\n     * This is used to prevent excessive penalties in case of high utilization.\n     */\n    uint256 private constant PENALTY_SATURATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD = 0.85e18; // 85%\n\n    /**\n     * @dev `MAX_SATURATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD` - `PENALTY_SATURATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD`\n     */\n    uint256 private constant SATURATION_PENALTY_BUFFER_IN_WAD = 0.1e18;\n\n    function accrueInterestAndUpdateReservesWithAssets(\n        uint112[6] storage assets,\n        AccrueInterestParams memory accrueInterestParams\n    ) external returns (uint256 interestXForLP, uint256 interestYForLP, uint256[3] memory protocolFeeAssets) {\n        if (accrueInterestParams.duration > 0) {\n            uint112[6] memory newAssets;\n            (newAssets, interestXForLP, interestYForLP, protocolFeeAssets) =\n                accrueInterestWithAssets(assets, accrueInterestParams);\n            for (uint256 i = DEPOSIT_X; i < TOKEN_COUNT; i++) {\n                assets[i] = newAssets[i];\n            }\n            emit ITokenController.InterestAccrued(\n                accrueInterestParams.reserveXAssets + interestXForLP,\n                accrueInterestParams.reserveYAssets + interestYForLP,\n                newAssets[DEPOSIT_X],\n                newAssets[DEPOSIT_Y],\n                newAssets[BORROW_L],\n                newAssets[BORROW_X],\n                newAssets[BORROW_Y]\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice we approximate the reserves based on an average tick value since the last lending\n     *         state update.\n     * @dev this will never return values greater than uint112 max when used correctly. The reserve\n     *      values are underestimated due to a tick being an approximate price. We use a smaller\n     *      value when multiplying and a larger when dividing to ensure that we do not overflow.\n     * @param activeLiquidityAssets active L where $\\sqrt{reserveX\\cdot reserveY}=L$\n     * @param lendingStateTick Average tick value since last lending state update.\n     * @return reserveXAssets approximate average reserve since last lending state update.\n     * @return reserveYAssets approximate average reserve since last lending state update.\n     */\n    function getReservesAtTick(\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets,\n        int16 lendingStateTick\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 reserveXAssets, uint256 reserveYAssets) {\n        // calculate reserves at lending state tick\n        uint256 sqrtPriceAtLendingStateTickMinInQ72 = TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(lendingStateTick);\n        uint256 sqrtPriceAtLendingStateTickMaxInQ72 =\n            Convert.mulDiv(sqrtPriceAtLendingStateTickMinInQ72, B_IN_Q72, Q72, true);\n\n        unchecked {\n            // x = L * sqrt(p)\n            reserveXAssets = Convert.mulDiv(activeLiquidityAssets, sqrtPriceAtLendingStateTickMinInQ72, Q72, false);\n\n            // y = L / sqrt(p)\n            reserveYAssets = Convert.mulDiv(activeLiquidityAssets, Q72, sqrtPriceAtLendingStateTickMaxInQ72, false);\n        }\n\n        return (Math.max(reserveXAssets, 1), Math.max(reserveYAssets, 1));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Reserve tokens borrowed out beyond the single-sided deposit, i.e. `max(borrow, deposit) - deposit`.\n     * @param startingAssets Asset balances indexed by token type.\n     * @return missingXAssets Reserve X borrowed beyond the X deposit.\n     * @return missingYAssets Reserve Y borrowed beyond the Y deposit.\n     */\n    function missingAssetsInReserve(\n        uint112[6] memory startingAssets\n    ) private pure returns (uint256 missingXAssets, uint256 missingYAssets) {\n        missingXAssets = Math.max(startingAssets[BORROW_X], startingAssets[DEPOSIT_X]) - startingAssets[DEPOSIT_X];\n        missingYAssets = Math.max(startingAssets[BORROW_Y], startingAssets[DEPOSIT_Y]) - startingAssets[DEPOSIT_Y];\n    }\n\n    function getUtilizationsInWads(\n        uint112[6] memory startingAssets,\n        uint256 reservesXAssets,\n        uint256 reservesYAssets,\n        uint256 satPercentageInWads\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256[3] memory utilizationInWads) {\n        uint256 missingAssetsXInL;\n        uint256 missingAssetsYInL;\n\n        unchecked {\n            // Calculate missing assets in a block scope to reduce stack depth\n            {\n                uint256 activeLiquidity = startingAssets[DEPOSIT_L] - startingAssets[BORROW_L];\n                (uint256 missingXAssets, uint256 missingYAssets) = missingAssetsInReserve(startingAssets);\n\n                // overflow not possible on multiplication, and underflow caught by conditions\n                if (missingXAssets > 0) {\n                    missingAssetsXInL = Math.ceilDiv(missingXAssets * activeLiquidity, reservesXAssets);\n                }\n                if (missingYAssets > 0) {\n                    missingAssetsYInL = Math.ceilDiv(missingYAssets * activeLiquidity, reservesYAssets);\n                }\n            }\n\n            // Calculate utilizations in separate block scope\n            {\n                utilizationInWads = [\n                    mutateUtilizationForSaturation(\n                        getUtilizationInWads(\n                            startingAssets[BORROW_L] + Math.max(missingAssetsXInL, missingAssetsYInL),\n                            startingAssets[DEPOSIT_L]\n                        ),\n                        satPercentageInWads\n                    ),\n                    getUtilizationInWads(startingAssets[BORROW_X], startingAssets[DEPOSIT_X] + reservesXAssets),\n                    getUtilizationInWads(startingAssets[BORROW_Y], startingAssets[DEPOSIT_Y] + reservesYAssets)\n                ];\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    function accrueInterestWithAssets(\n        uint112[6] memory assets,\n        AccrueInterestParams memory params\n    )\n        public\n        pure\n        returns (\n            uint112[6] memory newAssets,\n            uint256 interestXPortionForLP,\n            uint256 interestYPortionForLP,\n            uint256[3] memory protocolFeeAssets\n        )\n    {\n        uint112[6] memory startingAssets = assets;\n        uint256[3] memory interestAssetSet;\n        {\n            uint256 activeLiquidityAssets;\n            {\n                (uint256 missingXAssets, uint256 missingYAssets) = missingAssetsInReserve(startingAssets);\n                activeLiquidityAssets = Convert.depletionAdjustedActiveLiquidity(\n                    params.reserveXAssets, params.reserveYAssets, missingXAssets, missingYAssets\n                );\n            }\n\n            startingAssets[DEPOSIT_L] = uint112(activeLiquidityAssets + startingAssets[BORROW_L]);\n\n            (uint256 averageReservesX, uint256 averageReservesY) =\n                getReservesAtTick(activeLiquidityAssets, params.lendingStateTick);\n\n            {\n                uint256[3] memory utilizationsInWads = getUtilizationsInWads(\n                    startingAssets, averageReservesX, averageReservesY, params.satPercentageInWads\n                );\n\n                // for loop overhead not worth it for three loops.\n                interestAssetSet = [\n                    // Magnify interest on liquidity by 5x what x and y rates for the same utilization.\n                    LIQUIDITY_INTEREST_RATE_MAGNIFICATION\n                        * computeInterestAssets(\n                            params.duration,\n                            utilizationsInWads[DEPOSIT_L],\n                            NO_RESERVES_FOR_L,\n                            startingAssets[BORROW_L],\n                            startingAssets[DEPOSIT_L]\n                        ),\n                    computeInterestAssets(\n                        params.duration,\n                        utilizationsInWads[DEPOSIT_X],\n                        params.reserveXAssets,\n                        startingAssets[BORROW_X],\n                        startingAssets[DEPOSIT_X]\n                    ),\n                    computeInterestAssets(\n                        params.duration,\n                        utilizationsInWads[DEPOSIT_Y],\n                        params.reserveYAssets,\n                        startingAssets[BORROW_Y],\n                        startingAssets[DEPOSIT_Y]\n                    )\n                ];\n            }\n\n            unchecked {\n                protocolFeeAssets = [\n                    Convert.mulDiv(interestAssetSet[DEPOSIT_L], LENDING_FEE_RATE, 100, ROUNDING_UP),\n                    Convert.mulDiv(interestAssetSet[DEPOSIT_X], LENDING_FEE_RATE, 100, ROUNDING_UP),\n                    Convert.mulDiv(interestAssetSet[DEPOSIT_Y], LENDING_FEE_RATE, 100, ROUNDING_UP)\n                ];\n\n                interestAssetSet = [\n                    interestAssetSet[DEPOSIT_L] - protocolFeeAssets[DEPOSIT_L],\n                    interestAssetSet[DEPOSIT_X] - protocolFeeAssets[DEPOSIT_X],\n                    interestAssetSet[DEPOSIT_Y] - protocolFeeAssets[DEPOSIT_Y]\n                ];\n\n                interestXPortionForLP = Convert.mulDiv(\n                    interestAssetSet[DEPOSIT_X], averageReservesX, startingAssets[DEPOSIT_X] + averageReservesX, false\n                );\n                interestYPortionForLP = Convert.mulDiv(\n                    interestAssetSet[DEPOSIT_Y], averageReservesY, startingAssets[DEPOSIT_Y] + averageReservesY, false\n                );\n            }\n        }\n\n        // Skip DEPOSIT_L in the loop - it will be calculated after based on new reserves and BORROW_L\n        for (uint256 i = DEPOSIT_X; i < TOKEN_COUNT; i++) {\n            uint256 interestPortionForLP;\n            uint256 protocolFees;\n            uint256 shortArrayIndex = i % FIRST_DEBT_TOKEN;\n\n            if (i == DEPOSIT_X) {\n                interestPortionForLP = interestXPortionForLP;\n            } else if (i == DEPOSIT_Y) {\n                interestPortionForLP = interestYPortionForLP;\n            } else if (i > LAST_DEPOSIT) {\n                // add protocol fees to all borrows.\n                protocolFees = protocolFeeAssets[shortArrayIndex];\n            }\n            newAssets[i] = addInterestToAssets(\n                startingAssets[i],\n                interestAssetSet[shortArrayIndex]\n                // Back out lp interest being attributed to reserves\n                - interestPortionForLP\n                // add protocol fees to all borrows.\n                + protocolFees\n            );\n        }\n\n        // Calculate DEPOSIT_L last from the depletion adjusted active liquidity assets.\n        // newReserves include LP interest portions that will be added to reserves.\n        uint256 newReserveX = params.reserveXAssets + interestXPortionForLP;\n        uint256 newReserveY = params.reserveYAssets + interestYPortionForLP;\n        {\n            uint256 depositXWithFees = uint256(newAssets[DEPOSIT_X]) + protocolFeeAssets[DEPOSIT_X];\n            uint256 depositYWithFees = uint256(newAssets[DEPOSIT_Y]) + protocolFeeAssets[DEPOSIT_Y];\n            uint256 missingXAssets = Math.max(uint256(newAssets[BORROW_X]), depositXWithFees) - depositXWithFees;\n            uint256 missingYAssets = Math.max(uint256(newAssets[BORROW_Y]), depositYWithFees) - depositYWithFees;\n\n            newAssets[DEPOSIT_L] = uint112(\n                Convert.depletionAdjustedActiveLiquidity(newReserveX, newReserveY, missingXAssets, missingYAssets)\n                    + newAssets[BORROW_L]\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    function getUtilizationInWads(\n        uint256 totalBorrowedAssets,\n        uint256 totalDepositedAssets\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 utilization) {\n        if (totalDepositedAssets > 0) {\n            // assets are both 112 and will not overflow.\n            unchecked {\n                // assets are uint112, cant overflow.\n                utilization = Math.ceilDiv(totalBorrowedAssets * WAD, totalDepositedAssets);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Adjusts utilization based on saturation to calculate interest penalties\n     * @dev When saturation exceeds `PENALTY_SATURATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD`, utilization is increased\n     *      to apply higher interest rates as a penalty for high saturation\n     * @param utilization Current utilization of `L`, `X`, or `Y` assets\n     * @param maxSatInWads Saturation utilization\n     * @return The adjusted utilization value\n     */\n    function mutateUtilizationForSaturation(\n        uint256 utilization,\n        uint256 maxSatInWads\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        // Early return, if saturation is above or below defined threshold, or utilization is greater than max utilization.\n        if (utilization >= MAX_UTILIZATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD || maxSatInWads <= PENALTY_SATURATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD) {\n            return utilization;\n        } else if (maxSatInWads >= MAX_SATURATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD) {\n            return MAX_UTILIZATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD;\n        }\n\n        // Calculate adjustment based on formula:\n        // min(max((maxSatInWads - MAX_SATURATION_PERCENT) * (MAX_UTILIZATION - utilization) /\n        //     (MAX_SATURATION_PERCENT - PENALTY_SATURATION_PERCENT) + MAX_UTILIZATION, utilization), MAX_UTILIZATION)\n        uint256 adjustedUtilization = MAX_UTILIZATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD\n            - Convert.mulDiv(\n                MAX_SATURATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD - maxSatInWads,\n                MAX_UTILIZATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD - utilization,\n                SATURATION_PENALTY_BUFFER_IN_WAD,\n                false\n            );\n\n        return Math.min(Math.max(adjustedUtilization, utilization), MAX_UTILIZATION_PERCENT_IN_WAD);\n    }\n\n    function computeInterestAssets(\n        uint256 duration,\n        uint256 utilization,\n        uint256 reserveAssets,\n        uint256 borrowedAssets,\n        uint256 depositedAssets\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        uint256 baseRateInWads = getAnnualInterestRatePerSecondInWads(utilization);\n\n        return computeInterestAssetsGivenRate(\n            duration, borrowedAssets, Math.max(depositedAssets, reserveAssets), baseRateInWads\n        );\n    }\n\n    function computeInterestAssetsGivenRate(\n        uint256 duration,\n        uint256 borrowedAssets,\n        uint256 maxDepositedOrReserveAssets,\n        uint256 rateInWads\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256) {\n        // max amount of interest that can accrue is uint112 max to prevent overflows\n        // this means that once an asset hits the max, interest will no longer accrue.\n        unchecked {\n            return Math.min(\n                Convert.mulDiv(MathLib.wTaylorCompounded(rateInWads, duration), borrowedAssets, WAD, false),\n                type(uint112).max - Math.max(maxDepositedOrReserveAssets, borrowedAssets)\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    function addInterestToAssets(uint256 prevAssets, uint256 interest) internal pure returns (uint112) {\n        // safe down cast because interest <= type(uint112).max - max(depositedAssets, borrowedAssets) due to the check in computeInterestAssetsGivenRate\n        unchecked {\n            return uint112(prevAssets + interest);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Gets the annual interest rate for a given utilization\n     * @dev Same as getAnnualInterestRatePerSecondInWads but without dividing by SECONDS_IN_YEAR\n     * @param utilizationInWads The utilization rate in WADs\n     * @return interestRate The annual interest rate in WADs\n     */\n    function getAnnualInterestRatePerSecondInWads(\n        uint256 utilizationInWads\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 interestRate) {\n        if (utilizationInWads <= OPTIMAL_UTILIZATION) {\n            interestRate = utilizationInWads.wMulDown(SLOPE1);\n        } else if (utilizationInWads <= DANGER_UTILIZATION) {\n            interestRate = (utilizationInWads - OPTIMAL_UTILIZATION).wMulDown(SLOPE2) + BASE_OPTIMAL_UTILIZATION;\n        } else {\n            interestRate = (utilizationInWads - DANGER_UTILIZATION).wMulDown(SLOPE3) + BASE_DANGER_UTILIZATION;\n        }\n        interestRate /= SECONDS_IN_YEAR;\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    "lib/core-v1/contracts/libraries/PartialLiquidations.sol": {
      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only\npragma solidity ^0.8.34;\n\nimport {Math} from '@openzeppelin/contracts/utils/math/Math.sol';\n\nimport {\n    BIPS,\n    EXPECTED_SATURATION_LTV_MAG2,\n    MAG2,\n    MAX_SATURATION_RATIO_IN_MAG2,\n    TRANCHE_B_IN_Q72,\n    TRANCHE_B_MINUS_ONE_IN_Q72,\n    Q72,\n    Q144\n} from 'contracts/libraries/constants.sol';\nimport {ISaturationAndGeometricTWAPState} from 'contracts/interfaces/ISaturationAndGeometricTWAPState.sol';\nimport {\n    DEPOSIT_L,\n    DEPOSIT_X,\n    DEPOSIT_Y,\n    BORROW_L,\n    BORROW_X,\n    BORROW_Y\n} from 'contracts/interfaces/tokens/ITokenController.sol';\nimport {Convert} from 'contracts/libraries/Convert.sol';\nimport {Saturation} from 'contracts/libraries/Saturation.sol';\nimport {TickMath} from 'contracts/libraries/TickMath.sol';\nimport {Validation} from 'contracts/libraries/Validation.sol';\n\n/**\n * @title PartialLiquidations\n * @notice We allow liquidations to happen in parts when the position is spread across more than\n *   one tranche. These partial liquidations allow the liquidator to specify how many tranches they\n *   are liquidating based on how much they are repaying. Smaller slices start with the tranche range\n *   closest to the current price and move outward, so the slice reaches expected LTV before the full\n *   position on the same debt side does. In most cases, one tranche will be liquidated at one time,\n *   but in some cases, a sliver of liquidity in the first tranche may not be worth the cost to\n *   liquidate until it and the next tranche have become profitable to liquidate.\n *\n *   Once we determine the number of tranches to liquidate, we build the liquidated slice from the\n *   debt side first. L uses a weight because both deposit and borrow L can be included together;\n *   the opposing side is then solved directly so the slice lands on the expected liquidation LTV.\n *\n *   A partial liquidation splits the signed position into the liquidated slice and the remaining\n *   position:\n *\n *  ```math\n *   P_0=P_\\Delta+P_1,\n *   \\qquad\n *   P_0=[L_0,X_0,Y_0],\n *   \\qquad\n *   P_\\Delta=[L_\\Delta,X_\\Delta,Y_\\Delta]\n *   ```\n *\n *   The slice must preserve component signs, must not exceed the original component magnitudes, and\n *   must have exact expected liquidation LTV at the slice square-root price `s_delta`. The whole\n *   position and the remaining position do not need to have the same LTV as the liquidated slice.\n *\n *   Signed components are net deposits minus borrows:\n *\n *   ```math\n *   \\begin{align}\n *     L_0 &= depositL - borrowL \\\\\n *     X_0 &= depositX - borrowX \\\\\n *     Y_0 &= depositY - borrowY\n *   \\end{align}\n *   ```\n *\n *   The slice is constructed from the debt-side component first. Throughout this library, `s` is\n *   a square-root price, not a price. At square-root price `s`, X is measured in L as `X / s` and\n *   Y is measured in L as `Y * s`.\n *\n *   Let `k` be the unscaled expected liquidation LTV:\n *\n *   ```math\n *   k=EXPECTED\\_SATURATION\\_LTV\n *   ```\n *\n *   In code, `k` is represented by `EXPECTED_SATURATION_LTV_MAG2 / MAG2`.\n *\n *   In signed slice notation, repaid debt components are negative and seized collateral components\n *   are positive. For net debt X, the exact-LTV slice condition is:\n *\n *   ```math\n *   k=\\dfrac{-X_\\Delta/s_\\Delta-L_\\Delta}{L_\\Delta+Y_\\Delta s_\\Delta}\n *   ```\n *\n *   The net debt Y equations are symmetric:\n *\n *   ```math\n *   k=\\dfrac{-Y_\\Delta s_\\Delta-L_\\Delta}{L_\\Delta+X_\\Delta/s_\\Delta}\n *   ```\n *\n */\nlibrary PartialLiquidations {\n    int256 internal constant MAG2_INT = int256(MAG2);\n\n    struct LoopMemoryState {\n        uint256 satArrayLength;\n        uint256 partialSatInLAssets;\n        uint256 includedTranches;\n        uint256 partialSatLimit;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the partial liquidation slice for the tranches covered by the repayment.\n     * @dev Returns the full user asset array when every tranche is included. Returns the zero array\n     *   when no L-denominated debt is repaid. The tranche count is chosen from absolute\n     *   `satInLAssets`, while the slice geometry uses the corresponding relative saturation values.\n     * @param satPairPerTranche Saturation entries for the borrower, ordered from the end of\n     *   liquidation toward the start of liquidation.\n     * @param lastTranche Last tranche occupied by the borrower in the liquidation direction.\n     * @param userAssets Borrower assets in `[depositL, depositX, depositY, borrowL, borrowX, borrowY]` order.\n     * @param activeLiquidityAssets Active liquidity used to scale tranche saturation.\n     * @param netRepaidLAssets Net debt repaid by the liquidator, denominated in L.\n     * @param netSeizedLAssets Net collateral seized by the liquidator, denominated in L.\n     * @param netDebtX Whether the liquidation repays net X debt. If false, it repays net Y debt.\n     * @return liquidation Component-bounded asset amounts to remove from the borrower.\n     */\n    function calculatePartialLiquidation(\n        Saturation.SaturationPair[] memory satPairPerTranche,\n        int16 lastTranche,\n        uint256[6] memory userAssets,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets,\n        uint256 netRepaidLAssets,\n        uint256 netSeizedLAssets,\n        bool netDebtX\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256[6] memory liquidation) {\n        if (netRepaidLAssets > 0) {\n            uint256 partialSaturation;\n            uint256 totalSaturation;\n            uint256 remainingTranches;\n\n            {\n                LoopMemoryState memory loopMem = LoopMemoryState({\n                    satArrayLength: satPairPerTranche.length,\n                    partialSatInLAssets: 0,\n                    includedTranches: 0,\n                    partialSatLimit: Math.max(netRepaidLAssets, netSeizedLAssets * EXPECTED_SATURATION_LTV_MAG2 / MAG2)\n                });\n\n                uint256 bFactor = Q72;\n\n                for (uint256 i = loopMem.satArrayLength; i > 0; --i) {\n                    Saturation.SaturationPair memory borrowerTranche = satPairPerTranche[i - 1];\n                    uint256 satInRelativeLAssets = borrowerTranche.satRelativeToL * Q72 / bFactor;\n                    bFactor = bFactor * TRANCHE_B_IN_Q72 / Q72;\n\n                    if (loopMem.partialSatLimit > loopMem.partialSatInLAssets) {\n                        loopMem.includedTranches++;\n                        partialSaturation += satInRelativeLAssets;\n                    }\n                    totalSaturation += satInRelativeLAssets;\n\n                    // measure saturation\n                    loopMem.partialSatInLAssets += borrowerTranche.satInLAssets;\n                }\n\n                if (loopMem.includedTranches == loopMem.satArrayLength) {\n                    return userAssets;\n                }\n                remainingTranches = loopMem.satArrayLength - loopMem.includedTranches;\n            }\n            int16 adjTick = int16(uint16(remainingTranches));\n            uint256 sqrtPriceQ72 = TickMath.getSqrtPriceAtTick(\n                (netDebtX ? lastTranche + adjTick : lastTranche + 1 - adjTick) * int16(Saturation.TICKS_PER_TRANCHE)\n            );\n\n            liquidation = calcMutation(\n                userAssets, sqrtPriceQ72, partialSaturation, totalSaturation, activeLiquidityAssets, netDebtX\n            );\n        }\n    }\n\n    // helpers\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Builds a liquidation slice from raw tranche-boundary and saturation inputs.\n     *   It derives the target square-root price and asset weights from the same saturation window,\n     *   applies L through a shared weight, then solves the deposit-side delta directly from the\n     *   exact-LTV equation.\n     *\n     *   The target slice square-root price is the selected tranche boundary moved by the start\n     *   weight from the same saturation walk:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   s_\\Delta=b^T\\sqrt{w_s}\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   In signed notation, the debt-side slice then fixes one raw borrow amount:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   X_\\Delta=-borrowX\\cdot w_X\n     *     \\qquad \\text{for net debt X}\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   Y_\\Delta=-borrowY\\cdot w_Y\n     *     \\qquad \\text{for net debt Y}\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   L is selected next. The remaining side is solved last from the exact-LTV equation and may be\n     *   either collateral seized or same-side borrow repaid, depending on the sign of the solved\n     *   delta.\n     * @param userAssets Borrower assets in `[depositL, depositX, depositY, borrowL, borrowX, borrowY]` order.\n     * @param trancheBoundarySqrtPriceQ72 Boundary sqrt price for the tranche adjacent to the included slice.\n     * @param partialSaturation Relative saturation included in the partial liquidation.\n     * @param totalSaturation Total relative borrower saturation across all tranches.\n     * @param activeLiquidityAssets Active liquidity used to scale tranche saturation.\n     * @param netDebtX Whether the debt side is X. If false, the debt side is Y.\n     * @return liquidation Component-bounded asset amounts to remove from the borrower.\n     */\n    function calcMutation(\n        uint256[6] memory userAssets,\n        uint256 trancheBoundarySqrtPriceQ72,\n        uint256 partialSaturation,\n        uint256 totalSaturation,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets,\n        bool netDebtX\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256[6] memory liquidation) {\n        uint256 debtSideWeightQ72;\n        uint256 targetLiquidationSqrtPriceQ72;\n        {\n            uint256 sqrtStartWeightQ72 = calcSqrtStartWeightQ72(partialSaturation, activeLiquidityAssets, netDebtX);\n\n            uint256 sqrtEndWeightQ72 =\n                calcSqrtEndWeightQ72(partialSaturation, totalSaturation, activeLiquidityAssets, netDebtX);\n\n            debtSideWeightQ72 = calcDebtSideWeightQ72(sqrtStartWeightQ72, sqrtEndWeightQ72, netDebtX);\n\n            targetLiquidationSqrtPriceQ72 = trancheBoundarySqrtPriceQ72 * sqrtStartWeightQ72 / Q72;\n        }\n\n        uint256 debtSideDelta = Convert.mulDiv(userAssets[netDebtX ? BORROW_X : BORROW_Y], debtSideWeightQ72, Q72, true);\n\n        int256 lDelta;\n        {\n            uint256 lWeightQ72 =\n                calcLWeightQ72(userAssets, debtSideWeightQ72, debtSideDelta, targetLiquidationSqrtPriceQ72, netDebtX);\n\n            lDelta = applyLMutation(liquidation, userAssets, lWeightQ72);\n        }\n\n        {\n            int256 remainingSideDelta =\n                calcRemainingSideDelta(debtSideDelta, lDelta, targetLiquidationSqrtPriceQ72, netDebtX);\n\n            if (netDebtX) {\n                liquidation[BORROW_X] = debtSideDelta;\n                applyRemainingSideDelta(liquidation, userAssets, remainingSideDelta, DEPOSIT_Y, BORROW_Y);\n            } else {\n                liquidation[BORROW_Y] = debtSideDelta;\n                applyRemainingSideDelta(liquidation, userAssets, remainingSideDelta, DEPOSIT_X, BORROW_X);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Applies the L weight to both L legs and returns the resulting signed L delta.\n     *   The raw L legs are both included at the same weight:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   depositL_{\\Delta}=depositL\\cdot w_L,\\qquad borrowL_{\\Delta}=borrowL\\cdot w_L\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   The signed L contribution is:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   L_{\\Delta}=depositL_{\\Delta}-borrowL_{\\Delta}\n     *   ```\n     * @param liquidation Liquidation array being built.\n     * @param userAssets Borrower assets in `[depositL, depositX, depositY, borrowL, borrowX, borrowY]` order.\n     * @param lWeightQ72 Q72 weight to apply to deposit L and borrow L.\n     * @return lDelta Signed L contribution of the liquidated slice.\n     */\n    function applyLMutation(\n        uint256[6] memory liquidation,\n        uint256[6] memory userAssets,\n        uint256 lWeightQ72\n    ) private pure returns (int256 lDelta) {\n        liquidation[DEPOSIT_L] = Convert.mulDiv(userAssets[DEPOSIT_L], lWeightQ72, Q72, false);\n        liquidation[BORROW_L] = Convert.mulDiv(userAssets[BORROW_L], lWeightQ72, Q72, true);\n\n        lDelta = int256(liquidation[DEPOSIT_L]) - int256(liquidation[BORROW_L]);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the saturation-derived weight for the debt-side borrow asset.\n     *   The selected debt-side weight is:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   w_{debt}=\n     *   \\begin{cases}\n     *     w_X & \\text{if net debt X} \\\\\n     *     w_Y & \\text{if net debt Y}\n     *   \\end{cases}\n     *   ```\n     * @param sqrtStartWeightQ72 Start sqrt weight of the included saturation window.\n     * @param sqrtEndWeightQ72 End sqrt weight of the included saturation window.\n     * @param netDebtX Whether the debt side is X. If false, the debt side is Y.\n     * @return debtSideWeightQ72 Q72 weight applied to the debt-side borrow amount.\n     */\n    function calcDebtSideWeightQ72(\n        uint256 sqrtStartWeightQ72,\n        uint256 sqrtEndWeightQ72,\n        bool netDebtX\n    ) private pure returns (uint256 debtSideWeightQ72) {\n        uint256 xWeightQ72 = calcXWeightQ72(sqrtStartWeightQ72, sqrtEndWeightQ72);\n        debtSideWeightQ72 = netDebtX ? xWeightQ72 : calcYWeightQ72(xWeightQ72, sqrtEndWeightQ72);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Applies the signed remaining-side delta to the matching deposit or borrow leg.\n     *   Positive deltas seize remaining-side collateral. Negative deltas repay borrow on that side.\n     *   Partial borrow repayment is valid as long as it is not greater than the starting borrow.\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   R_\\Delta>0 \\Rightarrow deposit_\\Delta=\\min(R_\\Delta, deposit)\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   R_\\Delta<0 \\Rightarrow borrow_\\Delta=\\min(-R_\\Delta, borrow)\n     *   ```\n     * @param liquidation Liquidation array being built.\n     * @param userAssets Borrower assets in `[depositL, depositX, depositY, borrowL, borrowX, borrowY]` order.\n     * @param remainingSideDelta Signed amount for the side opposite the fixed debt-side borrow.\n     * @param depositIndex Deposit token index for the remaining side.\n     * @param borrowIndex Borrow token index for the remaining side.\n     */\n    function applyRemainingSideDelta(\n        uint256[6] memory liquidation,\n        uint256[6] memory userAssets,\n        int256 remainingSideDelta,\n        uint256 depositIndex,\n        uint256 borrowIndex\n    ) private pure {\n        if (remainingSideDelta > 0) {\n            liquidation[depositIndex] = Math.min(uint256(remainingSideDelta), userAssets[depositIndex]);\n        } else if (remainingSideDelta < 0) {\n            liquidation[borrowIndex] = Math.min(uint256(-remainingSideDelta), userAssets[borrowIndex]);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Solves the signed remaining-side delta for the fixed debt-side repayment.\n     *   The returned delta is positive when the slice must seize deposit-side collateral and\n     *   negative when it must repay borrow on that side to land on expected LTV.\n     *\n     *   For net debt X, define the fixed X-side debt value in L-units:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   debtSideValueInL=-\\frac{X_\\Delta}{s_\\Delta}=\\frac{borrowX_\\Delta}{s_\\Delta}\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   Then:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   Y_\\Delta=\\dfrac{debtSideValueInL-(1+k)L_\\Delta}{ks_\\Delta}\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   For net debt Y, define the fixed Y-side debt value in L-units:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   debtSideValueInL=-Y_\\Delta s_\\Delta=borrowY_\\Delta s_\\Delta\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   Then:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   X_\\Delta=\\dfrac{s_\\Delta(debtSideValueInL-(1+k)L_\\Delta)}{k}\n     *   ```\n     * @param borrowDelta Final debt-side borrow amount repaid by the liquidation.\n     * @param lDelta Signed L contribution already included in the liquidation.\n     * @param sqrtPriceQ72 Target liquidation sqrt price for the slice.\n     * @param netDebtX Whether the debt side is X. If false, the debt side is Y.\n     * @return remainingSideDelta Signed delta for the remaining side.\n     */\n    function calcRemainingSideDelta(\n        uint256 borrowDelta,\n        int256 lDelta,\n        uint256 sqrtPriceQ72,\n        bool netDebtX\n    ) private pure returns (int256 remainingSideDelta) {\n        uint256 debtSideValueInL = netDebtX\n            ? Convert.mulDiv(borrowDelta, Q72, sqrtPriceQ72, false)\n            : Convert.mulDiv(borrowDelta, sqrtPriceQ72, Q72, false);\n\n        int256 numerator =\n            int256(debtSideValueInL) * MAG2_INT - lDelta * int256(Saturation.EXPECTED_SATURATION_LTV_PLUS_ONE_MAG2);\n        uint256 mulFactor = netDebtX ? Q72 : sqrtPriceQ72;\n        uint256 denominator = EXPECTED_SATURATION_LTV_MAG2 * (netDebtX ? sqrtPriceQ72 : Q72);\n        remainingSideDelta = numerator >= 0\n            ? int256(Convert.mulDiv(uint256(numerator), mulFactor, denominator, false))\n            : -int256(Convert.mulDiv(uint256(-numerator), mulFactor, denominator, true));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the partial liquidation weight for asset X.\n     *   In unscaled terms, `sqrtStartWeightQ72` is $\\sqrt{w_s}$ and\n     *   `sqrtEndWeightQ72` is $\\sqrt{w_e}$. The branch matches which side of\n     *   one the saturation window starts on:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   w_X =\n     *   \\begin{cases}\n     *     \\dfrac{\\sqrt{w_s}-1}{\\sqrt{w_s}-\\sqrt{w_e}} & \\text{if } \\sqrt{w_s}>1 \\\\\n     *     \\dfrac{1-\\sqrt{w_s}}{\\sqrt{w_e}-\\sqrt{w_s}} & \\text{otherwise}\n     *   \\end{cases}\n     *   ```\n     * @param sqrtStartWeightQ72 Q72 start sqrt weight for the included saturation window.\n     * @param sqrtEndWeightQ72 Q72 end sqrt weight for the included saturation window.\n     * @return xWeightQ72 Q72 weight for asset X.\n     */\n    function calcXWeightQ72(\n        uint256 sqrtStartWeightQ72,\n        uint256 sqrtEndWeightQ72\n    ) private pure returns (uint256 xWeightQ72) {\n        if (sqrtStartWeightQ72 > Q72) {\n            xWeightQ72 = (sqrtStartWeightQ72 - Q72) * Q72 / (sqrtStartWeightQ72 - sqrtEndWeightQ72);\n        } else {\n            xWeightQ72 = (Q72 - sqrtStartWeightQ72) * Q72 / (sqrtEndWeightQ72 - sqrtStartWeightQ72);\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the partial liquidation weight for asset Y.\n     *   Formula for `w_Y`:\n     *  ```math\n     *  \\begin{equation}\n     *    w_Y = \\sqrt{w_e} \\cdot w_X\n     *  \\end{equation}\n     *  ```\n     * @param weightXQ72 Q72 weight for asset X.\n     * @param sqrtEndWeightQ72 Q72 end sqrt weight for the included saturation window.\n     * @return yWeightQ72 Q72 weight for asset Y.\n     */\n    function calcYWeightQ72(uint256 weightXQ72, uint256 sqrtEndWeightQ72) private pure returns (uint256 yWeightQ72) {\n        yWeightQ72 = sqrtEndWeightQ72 * weightXQ72 / Q72;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the partial liquidation weight for asset L from the debt-side slice.\n     *   Net-zero L is omitted. Net-borrow L uses the debt-side weight. Net-deposit L\n     *   chooses the lower feasible L endpoint implied by the fixed debt-side repayment.\n     *\n     *   For net-borrow L:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   L_\\Delta=L_0\\cdot w_{debt}\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   For net-deposit L, L is the free collateral-side variable:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   0 \\le L_\\Delta \\le L_0\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   Let `debtSideValueInL` be the fixed debt-side value and `remainingCollateralInL` be the\n     *   remaining positive deposit-side collateral, both in L-units:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   debtSideValueInL=\n     *   \\begin{cases}\n     *     \\dfrac{borrowX_\\Delta}{s_\\Delta} & \\text{if net debt X} \\\\\n     *     borrowY_\\Delta s_\\Delta & \\text{if net debt Y}\n     *   \\end{cases}\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   remainingCollateralInL=\n     *   \\begin{cases}\n     *     (depositY-borrowY)s_\\Delta & \\text{if net debt X and } depositY>borrowY \\\\\n     *     \\dfrac{depositX-borrowX}{s_\\Delta} & \\text{if net debt Y and } depositX>borrowX \\\\\n     *     0 & \\text{otherwise}\n     *   \\end{cases}\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   Exact LTV gives the lower feasible endpoint. Only positive remaining-side collateral changes\n     *   that endpoint; zero or net-borrow remaining-side value uses the same lower bound:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   L_\\Delta=\\max\\left(0,\\frac{debtSideValueInL-k\\cdot remainingCollateralInL}{1+k}\\right)\n     *     \\qquad \\text{when } remainingCollateralInL>0\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   L_\\Delta=\\frac{debtSideValueInL}{1+k}\n     *     \\qquad \\text{when } remainingCollateralInL=0\n     *   ```\n     * @param userAssets Borrower assets in `[depositL, depositX, depositY, borrowL, borrowX, borrowY]` order.\n     * @param debtSideWeightQ72 Q72 weight applied to the debt-side borrow amount.\n     * @param debtSideDelta Final debt-side borrow amount repaid by the liquidation.\n     * @param targetLiquidationSqrtPriceQ72 Target liquidation sqrt price for the slice.\n     * @param netDebtX Whether the debt side is X. If false, the debt side is Y.\n     * @return lWeightQ72 Q72 weight applied to deposit L and borrow L.\n     */\n    function calcLWeightQ72(\n        uint256[6] memory userAssets,\n        uint256 debtSideWeightQ72,\n        uint256 debtSideDelta,\n        uint256 targetLiquidationSqrtPriceQ72,\n        bool netDebtX\n    ) private pure returns (uint256 lWeightQ72) {\n        int256 netL = int256(userAssets[DEPOSIT_L]) - int256(userAssets[BORROW_L]);\n\n        if (netL == 0) {\n            lWeightQ72 = 0;\n        } else if (netL < 0) {\n            lWeightQ72 = debtSideWeightQ72;\n        } else {\n            uint256 debtSideAssetsInL = netDebtX\n                ? Convert.mulDiv(debtSideDelta, Q72, targetLiquidationSqrtPriceQ72, false)\n                : Convert.mulDiv(debtSideDelta, targetLiquidationSqrtPriceQ72, Q72, false);\n\n            uint256 remainingSideAssets;\n            if (netDebtX) {\n                if (userAssets[DEPOSIT_Y] > userAssets[BORROW_Y]) {\n                    remainingSideAssets = userAssets[DEPOSIT_Y] - userAssets[BORROW_Y];\n                }\n            } else {\n                if (userAssets[DEPOSIT_X] > userAssets[BORROW_X]) {\n                    remainingSideAssets = userAssets[DEPOSIT_X] - userAssets[BORROW_X];\n                }\n            }\n\n            uint256 denominator = Saturation.EXPECTED_SATURATION_LTV_PLUS_ONE_MAG2;\n            uint256 lower;\n            if (remainingSideAssets > 0) {\n                uint256 remainingSideAssetsInL = netDebtX\n                    ? Convert.mulDiv(remainingSideAssets, targetLiquidationSqrtPriceQ72, Q72, false)\n                    : Convert.mulDiv(remainingSideAssets, Q72, targetLiquidationSqrtPriceQ72, false);\n\n                uint256 debtSideComponent = debtSideAssetsInL * MAG2;\n\n                uint256 remainingSideComponent = EXPECTED_SATURATION_LTV_MAG2 * remainingSideAssetsInL;\n\n                lower = debtSideComponent > remainingSideComponent\n                    ? (debtSideComponent - remainingSideComponent) / denominator\n                    : 0;\n            } else {\n                lower = debtSideAssetsInL * MAG2 / denominator;\n            }\n\n            uint256 netLAbs = uint256(netL);\n            lWeightQ72 = lower < netLAbs ? Convert.mulDiv(lower, Q72, netLAbs, true) : Q72;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the start sqrt weight for the included saturation window.\n     *   The saturation is normalized by the active liquidity scaled by the maximum allowed\n     *   saturation ratio. Define:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   r_{max}=\\frac{MAX\\_SATURATION\\_RATIO\\_IN\\_MAG2}{MAG2}\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   We round up the sqrt to avoid `sqrtStartWeightQ72 == Q72`, which would return `0` from `calcXWeightQ72`.\n     *\n     *   Normalize the included saturation by active liquidity:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   a_s=\\frac{sat}{r_{max}L}\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   The underlying non-sqrt start weight is:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   \\begin{equation}\n     *     w_s = \\begin{cases}\n     *       1+a_s(B-1)\n     *         & \\text{ if net debt of X } \\\\\n     *       \\frac{1}{1+a_s(B-1)}\n     *         & \\text{ if net debt of Y}\n     *     \\end{cases}\n     *   \\end{equation}\n     *   ```\n     * @param partialSaturation Saturation included in the partial liquidation.\n     * @param activeLiquidityAssets Active liquidity used to scale tranche saturation.\n     * @param netDebtX Whether the debt side is X. If false, the reciprocal weight is used.\n     * @return sqrtStartWeightQ72 Q72 start sqrt weight, adjusted for the debt side.\n     */\n    function calcSqrtStartWeightQ72(\n        uint256 partialSaturation,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets,\n        bool netDebtX\n    ) internal pure returns (uint256 sqrtStartWeightQ72) {\n        sqrtStartWeightQ72 = Math.sqrt(\n            Q72\n                * (\n                    Q72\n                        + Convert.mulDiv(\n                            (TRANCHE_B_MINUS_ONE_IN_Q72) * MAG2,\n                            partialSaturation,\n                            activeLiquidityAssets * MAX_SATURATION_RATIO_IN_MAG2,\n                            true\n                        )\n                ),\n            Math.Rounding.Ceil\n        );\n\n        sqrtStartWeightQ72 = weightInNumeratorOrDenominator(sqrtStartWeightQ72, netDebtX);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Calculates the end sqrt weight for the included saturation window.\n     *   Normalize the remaining saturation by active liquidity:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   r_{max}=\\frac{MAX\\_SATURATION\\_RATIO\\_IN\\_MAG2}{MAG2},\n     *   \\qquad\n     *   a_e=\\frac{sat_{total}-sat}{r_{max}L}\n     *   ```\n     *\n     *   The underlying non-sqrt end weight is:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   \\begin{equation}\n     *     w_e = \\begin{cases}\n     *       1-a_e(B-1)\n     *         & \\text{ if net debt of X } \\\\\n     *       \\frac{1}{1-a_e(B-1)}\n     *         & \\text{ if net debt of Y}\n     *     \\end{cases}\n     *   \\end{equation}\n     *   ```\n     * @param partialSaturation Saturation included in the partial liquidation.\n     * @param totalSaturation Total borrower saturation across all tranches.\n     * @param activeLiquidityAssets Active liquidity used to scale tranche saturation.\n     * @param netDebtX Whether the debt side is X. If false, the reciprocal weight is used.\n     * @return sqrtEndWeightQ72 Q72 end sqrt weight, adjusted for the debt side.\n     */\n    function calcSqrtEndWeightQ72(\n        uint256 partialSaturation,\n        uint256 totalSaturation,\n        uint256 activeLiquidityAssets,\n        bool netDebtX\n    ) private pure returns (uint256 sqrtEndWeightQ72) {\n        sqrtEndWeightQ72 = Math.sqrt(\n            Q72\n                * (\n                    Q72\n                        - Convert.mulDiv(\n                            totalSaturation - partialSaturation,\n                            (TRANCHE_B_MINUS_ONE_IN_Q72) * MAG2,\n                            activeLiquidityAssets * MAX_SATURATION_RATIO_IN_MAG2,\n                            false\n                        )\n                )\n        );\n\n        sqrtEndWeightQ72 = weightInNumeratorOrDenominator(sqrtEndWeightQ72, netDebtX);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Adjusts a sqrt weight into numerator form for X debt or denominator form for Y debt.\n     *   Net debt Y walks the same tranche geometry in inverted square-root-price space:\n     *\n     *   ```math\n     *   \\sqrt{w}\\mapsto\\frac{1}{\\sqrt{w}}\n     *   ```\n     * @param sqrtWeight Q72 sqrt weight before debt-side orientation.\n     * @param netDebtX Whether the debt side is X. If false, the reciprocal weight is returned.\n     * @return adjustedSqrtWeight Q72 sqrt weight oriented for the debt side.\n     */\n    function weightInNumeratorOrDenominator(\n        uint256 sqrtWeight,\n        bool netDebtX\n    ) private pure returns (uint256 adjustedSqrtWeight) {\n        adjustedSqrtWeight = netDebtX ? sqrtWeight : Q144 / sqrtWeight;\n    }\n}\n"
    },
    "lib/core-v1/lib/mangrove-core/lib/core/BitLib.sol": {
      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: MIT\npragma solidity ^0.8.17;\n\nlibrary BitLib {\n  // - if x is a nonzero uint64: \n  //   return number of zeroes in x that do not have a 1 to their right\n  // - otherwise:\n  //    return 64\n  function ctz64(uint x) internal pure returns (uint256 c) {\n    unchecked {\n      assembly (\"memory-safe\") {\n        // clean\n        x:= and(x,0xffffffffffffffff)\n\n        // 7th bit\n        c:= shl(6,iszero(x))\n\n        // isolate lsb\n        x := and(x, add(not(x), 1))\n\n        // 6th bit\n        c := or(c,shl(5, lt(0xffffffff, x)))\n\n        // debruijn lookup\n        c := or(c, byte(shr(251, mul(shr(c, x), shl(224, 0x077cb531))), \n            0x00011c021d0e18031e16140f191104081f1b0d17151310071a0c12060b050a09))\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  // Function fls is MIT License. Copyright (c) 2022 Solady.\n/// @dev find last set.\n    /// Returns the index of the most significant bit of `x`,\n    /// counting from the least significant bit position.\n    /// If `x` is zero, returns 256.\n    /// Equivalent to `log2(x)`, but without reverting for the zero case.\n    function fls(uint256 x) internal pure returns (uint256 r) {\n        assembly (\"memory-safe\") {\n            r := shl(8, iszero(x))\n\n            r := or(r, shl(7, lt(0xffffffffffffffffffffffffffffffff, x)))\n            r := or(r, shl(6, lt(0xffffffffffffffff, shr(r, x))))\n            r := or(r, shl(5, lt(0xffffffff, shr(r, x))))\n\n            // For the remaining 32 bits, use a De Bruijn lookup.\n            x := shr(r, x)\n            x := or(x, shr(1, x))\n            x := or(x, shr(2, x))\n            x := or(x, shr(4, x))\n            x := or(x, shr(8, x))\n            x := or(x, shr(16, x))\n\n            // forgefmt: disable-next-item\n            r := or(r, byte(shr(251, mul(x, shl(224, 0x07c4acdd))),\n                0x0009010a0d15021d0b0e10121619031e080c141c0f111807131b17061a05041f))\n        }\n    }\n}\n"

      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only\npragma solidity ^0.8.34;\n\n/**\n * based on https://github.com/rob-Hitchens/SetTypes/blob/master/contracts/UintSet.sol\n * @notice Key sets with enumeration and delete. Uses mappings for random\n * and existence checks and dynamic arrays for enumeration. Key uniqueness is enforced.\n * @dev Sets are unordered. Delete operations reorder keys. All operations have a\n * fixed gas cost at any scale, O(1).\n * author: Rob Hitchens\n */\nlibrary Uint16Set {\n    struct Set {\n        mapping(uint16 => uint16) keyPointers;\n        uint16[] keyList;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice insert a key.\n     * @dev duplicate keys are not permitted.\n     * @param self storage pointer to a Set.\n     * @param key value to insert.\n     * @return keyAlreadyExists whether the key already existed in the set\n     */\n    function insert(Set storage self, uint16 key) internal returns (bool keyAlreadyExists) {\n        if (exists(self, key)) return true;\n        self.keyList.push(key);\n        self.keyPointers[key] = uint16(self.keyList.length) - 1;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice remove a key.\n     * @dev If the key does not exist, this function is a no-op and returns true.\n     * @param self storage pointer to a Set.\n     * @param key value to remove.\n     * @return isSetEmpty whether the key did not exist or the set still has items after removal\n     */\n    function remove(Set storage self, uint16 key) internal returns (bool isSetEmpty) {\n        if (!exists(self, key)) {\n            isSetEmpty = true;\n        } else {\n            uint16 last = count(self) - 1;\n            uint16 rowToReplace = self.keyPointers[key];\n            if (rowToReplace != last) {\n                uint16 keyToMove = self.keyList[last];\n                self.keyPointers[keyToMove] = rowToReplace;\n                self.keyList[rowToReplace] = keyToMove;\n            }\n            delete self.keyPointers[key];\n            self.keyList.pop();\n            isSetEmpty = last > 0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice count the keys.\n     * @param self storage pointer to a Set.\n     */\n    function count(\n        Set storage self\n    ) internal view returns (uint16) {\n        return uint16(self.keyList.length);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice check if a key is in the Set.\n     * @param self storage pointer to a Set.\n     * @param key value to check.\n     * @return bool true: Set member, false: not a Set member.\n     */\n    function exists(Set storage self, uint16 key) internal view returns (bool) {\n        if (self.keyList.length == 0) return false;\n        return self.keyList[self.keyPointers[key]] == key;\n    }\n}\n"

This can\n * later be changed with {transferOwnership}.\n *\n * This module is used through inheritance. It will make available the modifier\n * `onlyOwner`, which can be applied to your functions to restrict their use to\n * the owner.\n */\nabstract contract OwnableUpgradeable is Initializable, ContextUpgradeable {\n    /// @custom:storage-location erc7201:openzeppelin.storage.Ownable\n    struct OwnableStorage {\n        address _owner;\n    }\n\n    // keccak256(abi.encode(uint256(keccak256(\"openzeppelin.storage.Ownable\")) - 1)) & ~bytes32(uint256(0xff))\n    bytes32 private constant OwnableStorageLocation = 0x9016d09d72d40fdae2fd8ceac6b6234c7706214fd39c1cd1e609a0528c199300;\n\n    function _getOwnableStorage() private pure returns (OwnableStorage storage $) {\n        assembly {\n            $.slot := OwnableStorageLocation\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev The caller account is not authorized to perform an operation.\n     */\n    error OwnableUnauthorizedAccount(address account);\n\n    /**\n     * @dev The owner is not a valid owner account. (eg. `address(0)`)\n     */\n    error OwnableInvalidOwner(address owner);\n\n    event OwnershipTransferred(address indexed previousOwner, address indexed newOwner);\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Initializes the contract setting the address provided by the deployer as the initial owner.\n     */\n    function __Ownable_init(address initialOwner) internal onlyInitializing {\n        __Ownable_init_unchained(initialOwner);\n    }\n\n    function __Ownable_init_unchained(address initialOwner) internal onlyInitializing {\n        if (initialOwner == address(0)) {\n            revert OwnableInvalidOwner(address(0));\n        }\n        _transferOwnership(initialOwner);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Throws if called by any account other than the owner.\n     */\n    modifier onlyOwner() {\n        _checkOwner();\n        _;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Returns the address of the current owner.\n     */\n    function owner() public view virtual returns (address) {\n        OwnableStorage storage $ = _getOwnableStorage();\n        return $._owner;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Throws if the sender is not the owner.\n     */\n    function _checkOwner() internal view virtual {\n        if (owner() != _msgSender()) {\n            revert OwnableUnauthorizedAccount(_msgSender());\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Leaves the contract without owner. It will not be possible to call\n     * `onlyOwner` functions. Can only be called by the current owner.\n     *\n     * NOTE: Renouncing ownership will leave the contract without an owner,\n     * thereby disabling any functionality that is only available to the owner.\n     */\n    function renounceOwnership() public virtual onlyOwner {\n        _transferOwnership(address(0));\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Transfers ownership of the contract to a new account (`newOwner`).\n     * Can only be called by the current owner.\n     */\n    function transferOwnership(address newOwner) public virtual onlyOwner {\n        if (newOwner == address(0)) {\n            revert OwnableInvalidOwner(address(0));\n        }\n        _transferOwnership(newOwner);\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @dev Transfers ownership of the contract to a new account (`newOwner`).\n     * Internal function without access restriction.\n     */\n    function _transferOwnership(address newOwner) internal virtual {\n        OwnableStorage storage $ = _getOwnableStorage();\n        address oldOwner = $._owner;\n        $._owner = newOwner;\n        emit OwnershipTransferred(oldOwner, newOwner);\n    }\n}\n"

      "content": "// SPDX-License-Identifier: GPL-3.0-only\npragma solidity ^0.8.34;\n\nimport {GeometricTWAP} from 'contracts/libraries/GeometricTWAP.sol';\nimport {TickMath} from 'contracts/libraries/TickMath.sol';\n\n/**\n * @notice Tracks intra-observation price extremes to mitigate TWAP lag attacks.\n * @dev Records max/min ticks per observation interval so arb-driven price signals are captured\n *      even if the attacker resets the price afterward.\n */\nlibrary PriceExtremes {\n    /**\n     * @notice State for tracking price extremes within an observation interval.\n     */\n    struct State {\n        uint32 currentTimestamp;\n        uint32 previousTimestamp;\n        int16 currentMaxTick;\n        int16 currentMinTick;\n        int16 previousMaxTick;\n        int16 previousMinTick;\n        bool currentInitialized;\n        bool previousInitialized;\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Record a priceQ128 observation as inclusive min and exclusive max ticks.\n     * @param state     The per-pair extreme tracking state\n     * @param priceQ128 The current price as reserveX * Q128 / reserveY\n     * @param interval  The observation interval (DEFAULT_MID_TERM_INTERVAL)\n     */\n    function record(State storage state, uint256 priceQ128, uint32 interval) internal {\n        int16 minTick = TickMath.getTickAtPrice(priceQ128);\n        int16 maxTick = minTick + 1;\n        uint32 currentTimestamp = GeometricTWAP.getCurrentTimestamp();\n        uint32 elapsed;\n        unchecked {\n            elapsed = currentTimestamp - state.currentTimestamp;\n        }\n\n        if (!state.currentInitialized || elapsed >= interval) {\n            // Roll current → previous\n            state.previousTimestamp = state.currentTimestamp;\n            state.previousMaxTick = state.currentMaxTick;\n            state.previousMinTick = state.currentMinTick;\n            state.previousInitialized = state.currentInitialized;\n            // Initialize new current\n            state.currentTimestamp = currentTimestamp;\n            state.currentMaxTick = maxTick;\n            state.currentMinTick = minTick;\n            state.currentInitialized = true;\n        } else {\n            if (maxTick > state.currentMaxTick) state.currentMaxTick = maxTick;\n            if (minTick < state.currentMinTick) state.currentMinTick = minTick;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /**\n     * @notice Widen a [minTick, maxTick) range using fresh price extremes. Never narrows.\n     * @param state    The per-pair extreme tracking state\n     * @param minTick  Current minimum tick (from getTickRange)\n     * @param maxTick  Current exclusive maximum tick (from getTickRange)\n     * @param interval The observation interval (DEFAULT_MID_TERM_INTERVAL)\n     * @return widenedMin  The potentially widened minimum tick\n     * @return widenedMax  The potentially widened maximum tick\n     */\n    function widen(\n        State storage state,\n        int16 minTick,\n        int16 maxTick,\n        uint32 interval\n    ) internal view returns (int16 widenedMin, int16 widenedMax) {\n        widenedMin = minTick;\n        widenedMax = maxTick;\n        uint32 currentTimestamp = GeometricTWAP.getCurrentTimestamp();\n        // previousTimestamp is set at rollover to the prior slot's start, which by the rollover\n        // condition is already >= interval old. 2 * interval gives the previous slot one further\n        // interval of freshness; tighter bounds would discard it immediately after every rollover.\n        uint32 maxAge = 2 * interval;\n\n        (widenedMin, widenedMax) = _widen(\n            state.currentTimestamp,\n            state.currentMinTick,\n            state.currentMaxTick,\n            state.currentInitialized,\n            currentTimestamp,\n            maxAge,\n            widenedMin,\n            widenedMax\n        );\n        (widenedMin, widenedMax) = _widen(\n            state.previousTimestamp,\n            state.previousMinTick,\n            state.previousMaxTick,\n            state.previousInitialized,\n            currentTimestamp,\n            maxAge,\n            widenedMin,\n            widenedMax\n        );\n    }\n\n    function _widen(\n        uint32 stateTimestamp,\n        int16 slotMinTick,\n        int16 slotMaxTick,\n        bool initialized,\n        uint32 currentTimestamp,\n        uint32 maxAge,\n        int16 widenMin,\n        int16 widenMax\n    ) private pure returns (int16, int16) {\n        // Subtraction is unchecked so the freshness check stays correct across the 2^32 wrap.\n        uint32 age;\n        unchecked {\n            age = currentTimestamp - stateTimestamp;\n        }\n        if (initialized && age < maxAge) {\n            if (slotMaxTick > widenMax) widenMax = slotMaxTick;\n            if (slotMinTick < widenMin) widenMin = slotMinTick;\n        }\n        return (widenMin, widenMax);\n    }\n}\n"
